点击展开
#include "ui_btn.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#define TOUCH_SLOP_PX   8 // 8 像素滑动防抖死区

/* ================= 1. 辅助几何与文本测量 ================= */

static inline bool ui_btn_hit_test(const ui_rect_t *rect, int16_t x, int16_t y) {
    return (x >= rect->x && x < (rect->x + rect->w) &&
            y >= rect->y && y < (rect->y + rect->h));
}

static uint8_t ui_btn_get_font_height(FontSize_t size) {
    switch (size) {
        case FONT_16: return 16;
        case FONT_20: return 20;
        case FONT_24: return 24;
        default:      return 16;
    }
}

static int16_t ui_btn_measure_string_width(const char *str, FontSize_t size) {
    if (!str) return 0;
    uint8_t font_h = ui_btn_get_font_height(size);
    uint8_t half_w = font_h / 2;
    int16_t width = 0;

    while (*str) {
        if ((uint8_t)*str >= 0x80) {
            // GB2312 双字节汉字
            if (*(str + 1) == '\0') break;
            width += font_h;
            str += 2;
        } else {
            // ASCII 半角字符
            width += half_w;
            str += 1;
        }
    }
    return width;
}

/* ================= 2. 按钮初始化 ================= */

void ui_btn_init(ui_btn_t *btn, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h,
                 const char *text, ui_btn_cb_t on_click) {
    if (!btn) return;

    btn->rect = ui_rect_make(x, y, w, h);

    btn->text           = text;
    btn->font_size      = FONT_16;
    btn->mode           = UI_BTN_MODE_PUSH;
    btn->style          = UI_BTN_STYLE_RECT;

    btn->color_bg       = 0x2104; // 默认深灰底
    btn->color_active   = 0x07E0; // 默认亮绿激活色
    btn->color_text     = 0xFFFF; // 默认纯白字

    btn->is_pressed     = false;
    btn->is_checked     = false;
    btn->show_underline = false;

    btn->last_tick      = 0;
    btn->debounce_ms    = 50;

    btn->down_x         = 0;
    btn->down_y         = 0;
    btn->is_touch_down  = false;
    btn->is_dragged     = false;

    btn->on_click       = on_click;
    btn->user_data      = NULL;
}

void ui_btn_set_color(ui_btn_t *btn, uint16_t bg, uint16_t active, uint16_t text) {
    if (!btn) return;
    btn->color_bg     = bg;
    btn->color_active = active;
    btn->color_text   = text;
}

/* ================= 3. 触控状态机 (Touch Slop + Touch Up 触发) ================= */

bool ui_btn_process_touch(ui_btn_t *btn, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    if (!btn) return false;

    bool hit = is_touching && ui_btn_hit_test(&btn->rect, tx, ty);
    bool need_redraw = false;

    if (is_touching) {
        if (!btn->is_touch_down) {
            // A. 初次按下 (Touch Down): 仅在命中本按钮时记录坐标起点
            if (hit) {
                btn->is_touch_down = true;
                btn->is_dragged    = false;
                btn->down_x        = tx;
                btn->down_y        = ty;
                btn->is_pressed    = true;
                need_redraw        = true;
            }
        } else {
            // B. 持续移动 (Touch Move): 8px 防抖死区与移出范围判定
            int16_t dx = abs(tx - btn->down_x);
            int16_t dy = abs(ty - btn->down_y);

            // 位移超限判定为滑动，立即取消按下高亮
            if (!btn->is_dragged && (dx >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX || dy >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX)) {
                btn->is_dragged = true;
                if (btn->is_pressed) {
                    btn->is_pressed = false;
                    need_redraw     = true;
                }
            }

            // 手指滑出按钮区域立即解除高亮
            if (btn->is_pressed && !hit) {
                btn->is_pressed = false;
                need_redraw     = true;
            }
        }
    } else {
        // C. ⚡ 手指松开 (Touch Up): 唯一允许触发业务回调的时机
        if (btn->is_touch_down) {
            btn->is_touch_down = false;

            // 必须满足：未发生拖拽滑动 + 处于按下状态 (或松手点仍在按钮内)
            if (!btn->is_dragged && (btn->is_pressed || hit)) {
                btn->is_pressed = false;

                // Toggle 模式翻转状态
                if (btn->mode == UI_BTN_MODE_TOGGLE) {
                    btn->is_checked = !btn->is_checked;
                }

                // 冷却防抖判定
                if (now_tick - btn->last_tick >= btn->debounce_ms) {
                    btn->last_tick = now_tick;
                    if (btn->on_click) {
                        btn->on_click(btn, btn->user_data);
                    }
                }
                btn->is_dragged = false;
                return true;
            }

            // 滑动取消后的抬手释放
            if (btn->is_pressed) {
                btn->is_pressed = false;
                need_redraw     = true;
            }
            btn->is_dragged = false;
        }
    }

    return need_redraw;
}

/* ================= 4. 切片安全光栅化渲染 ================= */

void ui_btn_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_btn_t *btn) {
    if (!tile || !btn) return;

    // 1. 📐 与当前切片显存求物理交集 (Early Exit)
    ui_rect_t overlap;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &btn->rect, &overlap)) {
        return;
    }

    // 2. 🎨 状态颜色解析
    bool is_active = btn->is_pressed || btn->is_checked;
    uint16_t current_bg = (btn->style == UI_BTN_STYLE_TEXT) ? btn->color_bg 
                                                             : (is_active ? btn->color_active : btn->color_bg);
    uint16_t current_fg = (btn->style == UI_BTN_STYLE_TEXT && is_active) ? btn->color_active 
                                                                         : btn->color_text;

    // 3. 🖌️ 形状光栅化 (背景仅填充与 Tile 相交的 overlap 区域)
    if (btn->style == UI_BTN_STYLE_RECT) {
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &overlap, current_bg);
        ui_draw_rect(tile, &btn->rect, is_active ? 0x07FF : 0x4208);
    } 
    else if (btn->style == UI_BTN_STYLE_ROUND) {
        int16_t r = btn->rect.h / 2;
        int16_t left_cx  = btn->rect.x + r;
        int16_t right_cx = btn->rect.x + btn->rect.w - r;
        int16_t cy       = btn->rect.y + r;

        ui_draw_fill_circle(tile, left_cx, cy, r, current_bg);
        ui_draw_fill_circle(tile, right_cx, cy, r, current_bg);

        if (btn->rect.w > btn->rect.h) {
            ui_rect_t mid_rect = ui_rect_make(left_cx, btn->rect.y, (int16_t)(btn->rect.w - 2 * r), btn->rect.h);
            ui_rect_t mid_ov;
            if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &mid_rect, &mid_ov)) {
                ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &mid_ov, current_bg);
            }
        }
    }

    // 4. 🔤 文字居中计算与视口安全渲染
    if (btn->text && btn->text[0] != '\0') {
        uint8_t font_h = ui_btn_get_font_height(btn->font_size);
        int16_t text_w = ui_btn_measure_string_width(btn->text, btn->font_size);

        int16_t tx = btn->rect.x + (btn->rect.w - text_w) / 2;
        int16_t ty = btn->rect.y + (btn->rect.h - font_h) / 2;

        if (tx < btn->rect.x) tx = btn->rect.x + 2;

        // 仅当文字垂直跨度与当前切片重叠时才执行字库点阵光栅化
        if ((ty + font_h) > tile->rect.y && ty < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
            ui_draw_string(tile, tx, ty, btn->text, btn->font_size, current_fg, current_bg);

            // 5. ➖ 选中下划线
            if (is_active && btn->show_underline) {
                int16_t line_y = ty + font_h + 2;
                if (line_y >= tile->rect.y && line_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
                    ui_draw_fast_hline(tile, tx, line_y, text_w, btn->color_active);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

/*
 * @Author: 轩
 * @Date: 2026-08-19 19:58:59
 * @LastEditTime: 2026-08-21 10:35:49
 * @FilePath: \pico\ui\ui_btn.h
 */
#ifndef UI_BUTTON_H
#define UI_BUTTON_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include "ui_geometry.h"
#include "ui_tile.h"
#include "chinese.h" // 提供 FontSize_t 定义

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

/* ================= 1. 交互模式与外观样式枚举 ================= */

/** 按钮交互模式 */
typedef enum {
    UI_BTN_MODE_PUSH = 0, // 普通点按：松手触发回调
    UI_BTN_MODE_TOGGLE    // 状态切换：松手翻转选中状态并触发回调
} ui_btn_mode_t;

/** 按钮视觉形态 */
typedef enum {
    UI_BTN_STYLE_RECT = 0, // 标准矩形
    UI_BTN_STYLE_ROUND,    // 胶囊形 / 圆角矩形
    UI_BTN_STYLE_TEXT      // 无边框纯文本
} ui_btn_style_t;

/* 前向声明与回调函数类型 */
struct ui_btn;
typedef void (*ui_btn_cb_t)(struct ui_btn *btn, void *user_data);

/* ================= 2. 按钮控件结构体 ================= */

typedef struct ui_btn {
    ui_rect_t       rect;           // 坐标与尺寸 (x, y, w, h)
    const char     *text;           // 显示文本
    FontSize_t      font_size;      // 字号 (FONT_16 / FONT_20 / FONT_24)
    ui_btn_mode_t   mode;           // 交互模式 (Push / Toggle)
    ui_btn_style_t  style;          // 视觉形态 (Rect / Round / Text)

    uint16_t        color_bg;       // 默认背景色
    uint16_t        color_active;   // 激活/按下时的背景或高亮色
    uint16_t        color_text;     // 默认文本颜色

    bool            is_pressed;     // 当前是否正被有效按压
    bool            is_checked;     // Toggle 模式下的选中状态
    bool            show_underline; // 处于激活/选中时是否绘制下划线

    uint32_t        last_tick;      // 防抖时间戳
    uint32_t        debounce_ms;    // 防抖冷却周期 (默认 50ms)

    // ⚡ 触控手势保护与防抖状态机
    int16_t         down_x;
    int16_t         down_y;
    bool            is_touch_down;
    bool            is_dragged;     // 是否已识别为拖拽/滑动

    ui_btn_cb_t     on_click;       // 点击事件回调
    void           *user_data;      // 用户私有数据指针
} ui_btn_t;

/* ================= 3. 核心 API 接口 ================= */

/**
 * @brief 初始化按钮基础属性
 */
void ui_btn_init(ui_btn_t *btn, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h,
                 const char *text, ui_btn_cb_t on_click);

/**
 * @brief 触控输入处理（集成 8px 死区过滤与 Touch-Up 抬手触发）
 * @return true 表示状态发生改变需要局部/切片重绘
 */
bool ui_btn_process_touch(ui_btn_t *btn, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick);

/**
 * @brief 在切片显存中执行光栅化渲染（严格边界裁剪）
 */
void ui_btn_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_btn_t *btn);

/**
 * @brief 动态配置三态主题颜色
 */
void ui_btn_set_color(ui_btn_t *btn, uint16_t bg, uint16_t active, uint16_t text);

/**
 * @brief 获取按钮自身的包围盒（用于局部推流 ui_render_dirty_clipped）
 */
static inline ui_rect_t ui_btn_get_dirty_rect(const ui_btn_t *btn) {
    return btn ? btn->rect : ui_rect_make(0, 0, 0, 0);
}

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

#endif // UI_BUTTON_H
ui_checkbox_group.c.c 263 行 · 10498 B

#include "ui_checkbox_group.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

#define TOUCH_SLOP_PX   8 // 8 像素滑动防抖死区

void ui_checkbox_group_init(ui_checkbox_group_t *cg, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h,
                            ui_check_mode_t mode, int16_t item_h, ui_checkbox_cb_t on_change) {
    if (!cg) return;

    cg->rect       = ui_rect_make(x, y, w, h);
    cg->mode       = mode;
    cg->item_count = 0;
    cg->item_h     = (item_h > 20) ? item_h : 36;
    cg->scroll_y   = 0;
    cg->content_h  = 0;

    cg->down_x         = 0;
    cg->down_y         = 0;
    cg->scroll_start_y = 0;
    cg->is_touch_down  = false;
    cg->is_dragged     = false;
    cg->is_scrolling   = false;

    cg->color_bg            = 0x0842; // 极深蓝黑底
    cg->color_box_bg        = 0x18C3; // 选项框深灰底
    cg->color_box_border    = 0x39E7; // 选项框外框
    cg->color_checked       = 0x07E0; // 亮绿勾选色
    cg->color_text          = 0xFFFF; // 纯白字
    cg->color_text_disabled = 0x632C; // 禁用暗灰
    cg->color_border        = 0x39E7; // 外框分割线
    cg->color_scrollbar     = 0x07FF; // 青蓝滚动指示条

    cg->on_change = on_change;
    cg->user_data = NULL;
}

bool ui_checkbox_group_add_item(ui_checkbox_group_t *cg, const char *label, bool init_checked, bool is_disabled) {
    if (!cg || cg->item_count >= UI_CHECK_MAX_ITEMS) return false;

    uint8_t idx = cg->item_count;
    strncpy(cg->items[idx].label, label ? label : "", sizeof(cg->items[idx].label) - 1);
    cg->items[idx].label[sizeof(cg->items[idx].label) - 1] = '\0';
    cg->items[idx].is_checked  = init_checked;
    cg->items[idx].is_disabled = is_disabled;

    cg->item_count++;
    cg->content_h = cg->item_count * cg->item_h;
    return true;
}

void ui_checkbox_group_set_checked(ui_checkbox_group_t *cg, uint8_t index, bool checked) {
    if (!cg || index >= cg->item_count || cg->items[index].is_disabled) return;

    if (cg->mode == UI_CHECK_MODE_SINGLE) {
        if (checked) {
            for (uint8_t i = 0; i < cg->item_count; i++) {
                cg->items[i].is_checked = (i == index);
            }
            if (cg->on_change) cg->on_change(cg, index, true, cg->user_data);
        }
    } else {
        if (cg->items[index].is_checked != checked) {
            cg->items[index].is_checked = checked;
            if (cg->on_change) cg->on_change(cg, index, checked, cg->user_data);
        }
    }
}

bool ui_checkbox_group_is_checked(const ui_checkbox_group_t *cg, uint8_t index) {
    if (!cg || index >= cg->item_count) return false;
    return cg->items[index].is_checked;
}

void ui_checkbox_group_clear(ui_checkbox_group_t *cg) {
    if (!cg) return;
    cg->item_count = 0;
    cg->content_h  = 0;
    cg->scroll_y   = 0;
}

/* ================= 触控状态机 (拖拽滚动 + Touch-Up 勾选) ================= */

bool ui_checkbox_group_process_touch(ui_checkbox_group_t *cg, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    (void)now_tick;
    if (!cg || cg->item_count == 0) return false;

    int16_t max_scroll = cg->content_h - cg->rect.h;
    if (max_scroll < 0) max_scroll = 0;

    bool need_redraw = false;

    if (is_touching) {
        if (!cg->is_touch_down) {
            // A. 初次按下 (Touch Down): 命中视口
            if (ui_rect_contains_point(&cg->rect, tx, ty)) {
                cg->is_touch_down  = true;
                cg->is_dragged     = false;
                cg->is_scrolling   = false;
                cg->down_x         = tx;
                cg->down_y         = ty;
                cg->scroll_start_y = cg->scroll_y;
            }
        } else {
            // B. 持续移动 (Touch Move): 8px 防抖死区与平滑滚动追踪
            int16_t dy = ty - cg->down_y;
            int16_t dx = tx - cg->down_x;

            if (!cg->is_dragged && (abs(dy) >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX || abs(dx) >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX)) {
                cg->is_dragged   = true;
                cg->is_scrolling = true;
            }

            if (cg->is_dragged && max_scroll > 0) {
                int16_t new_scroll = cg->scroll_start_y - dy;
                if (new_scroll < 0) new_scroll = 0;
                if (new_scroll > max_scroll) new_scroll = max_scroll;

                if (cg->scroll_y != new_scroll) {
                    cg->scroll_y = new_scroll;
                    need_redraw  = true;
                }
            }
        }
        return need_redraw;
    } else {
        // C. ⚡ 手指松开 (Touch Up): 判定点击切换
        if (cg->is_touch_down) {
            cg->is_touch_down = false;

            // 仅在未发生滑动且松手点仍在视口内时结算点击
            if (!cg->is_dragged && ui_rect_contains_point(&cg->rect, tx, ty)) {
                int16_t rel_y = ty - (cg->rect.y - cg->scroll_y);
                if (rel_y >= 0 && rel_y < cg->content_h) {
                    uint8_t clicked_idx = (uint8_t)(rel_y / cg->item_h);

                    if (clicked_idx < cg->item_count && !cg->items[clicked_idx].is_disabled) {
                        if (cg->mode == UI_CHECK_MODE_SINGLE) {
                            for (uint8_t i = 0; i < cg->item_count; i++) {
                                cg->items[i].is_checked = (i == clicked_idx);
                            }
                            if (cg->on_change) cg->on_change(cg, clicked_idx, true, cg->user_data);
                        } else {
                            cg->items[clicked_idx].is_checked = !cg->items[clicked_idx].is_checked;
                            if (cg->on_change) cg->on_change(cg, clicked_idx, cg->items[clicked_idx].is_checked, cg->user_data);
                        }
                        need_redraw = true;
                    }
                }
            }

            cg->is_dragged   = false;
            cg->is_scrolling = false;
        }
    }

    return need_redraw;
}

/* ================= 切片光栅化渲染 (O(1) 行级剪枝) ================= */

void ui_checkbox_group_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_checkbox_group_t *cg) {
    if (!tile || !cg || cg->item_count == 0) return;

    ui_rect_t overlap;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &cg->rect, &overlap)) {
        return;
    }

    // 1. 擦除背景与外边框
    ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &overlap, cg->color_bg);
    ui_draw_rect(tile, &cg->rect, cg->color_border);

    // 2. 启用像素级局部视口裁剪
    ui_draw_set_clip(&cg->rect);

    int16_t base_y = cg->rect.y - cg->scroll_y;

    // ⚡ 3. 核心行级算术剪枝 (从 O(N) 降至 O(1))
    int16_t first_idx = (tile->rect.y - base_y) / cg->item_h;
    int16_t last_idx  = (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h - 1 - base_y) / cg->item_h;

    if (first_idx < 0) first_idx = 0;
    if (last_idx >= (int16_t)cg->item_count) last_idx = (int16_t)cg->item_count - 1;

    for (int16_t i = first_idx; i <= last_idx; i++) {
        int16_t item_y = base_y + i * cg->item_h;
        const ui_check_item_t *item = &cg->items[i];

        int16_t box_size = 18;
        int16_t box_x = cg->rect.x + 10;
        int16_t box_y = item_y + (cg->item_h - box_size) / 2;

        // A. 绘制勾选框 / 单选圆钮
        if (cg->mode == UI_CHECK_MODE_MULTI) {
            ui_rect_t box_rect = ui_rect_make(box_x, box_y, box_size, box_size);
            ui_rect_t box_ov;
            if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &box_rect, &box_ov)) {
                ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &box_ov, cg->color_box_bg);
                ui_draw_rect(tile, &box_rect, item->is_checked ? cg->color_checked : cg->color_box_border);

                if (item->is_checked) {
                    int16_t cx = box_x + box_size / 2;
                    int16_t cy = box_y + box_size / 2;
                    uint16_t col = item->is_disabled ? cg->color_text_disabled : cg->color_checked;
                    ui_draw_line(tile, cx - 4, cy, cx - 1, cy + 3, col);
                    ui_draw_line(tile, cx - 1, cy + 3, cx + 5, cy - 4, col);
                }
            }
        } else {
            int16_t cx = box_x + box_size / 2;
            int16_t cy = box_y + box_size / 2;
            int16_t r  = box_size / 2;

            ui_draw_fill_circle(tile, cx, cy, r, cg->color_box_bg);
            ui_draw_circle(tile, cx, cy, r, item->is_checked ? cg->color_checked : cg->color_box_border);

            if (item->is_checked) {
                uint16_t col = item->is_disabled ? cg->color_text_disabled : cg->color_checked;
                ui_draw_fill_circle(tile, cx, cy, r - 4, col);
            }
        }

        // B. 绘制选项文字
        int16_t text_x = box_x + box_size + 10;
        int16_t text_y = item_y + (cg->item_h - 16) / 2;

        if ((text_y + 16) > tile->rect.y && text_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
            uint16_t text_col = item->is_disabled ? cg->color_text_disabled : cg->color_text;
            ui_draw_string(tile, text_x, text_y, item->label, FONT_16, text_col, cg->color_bg);
        }

        // C. 行底部分割线
        int16_t line_y = item_y + cg->item_h - 1;
        if (line_y >= tile->rect.y && line_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h) && (i < cg->item_count - 1)) {
            ui_draw_fast_hline(tile, cg->rect.x + 6, line_y, cg->rect.w - 12, 0x18C3);
        }
    }

    // 4. 超出视口时绘制微型滚动条
    if (cg->content_h > cg->rect.h) {
        int16_t bar_w = 2;
        int16_t track_h = cg->rect.h - 4;
        int16_t thumb_h = (int16_t)(((int32_t)cg->rect.h * track_h) / cg->content_h);
        if (thumb_h < 12) thumb_h = 12;

        int16_t max_scroll = cg->content_h - cg->rect.h;
        int16_t thumb_y = cg->rect.y + 2;
        if (max_scroll > 0) {
            thumb_y += (int16_t)(((int32_t)cg->scroll_y * (track_h - thumb_h)) / max_scroll);
        }
        int16_t thumb_x = cg->rect.x + cg->rect.w - bar_w - 2;

        ui_rect_t thumb_rect = ui_rect_make(thumb_x, thumb_y, bar_w, thumb_h);
        ui_rect_t thumb_overlap;
        if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &thumb_rect, &thumb_overlap)) {
            ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &thumb_overlap, cg->color_scrollbar);
        }
    }

    ui_draw_reset_clip();
}

#ifndef UI_CHECKBOX_GROUP_H
#define UI_CHECKBOX_GROUP_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include "ui_geometry.h"
#include "ui_tile.h"
#include "chinese.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

#define UI_CHECK_MAX_ITEMS 32

typedef enum {
    UI_CHECK_MODE_MULTI = 0,    // 多选复选框模式 (Checkbox, 方形勾选框)
    UI_CHECK_MODE_SINGLE        // 互斥单选钮模式 (Radio Button, 圆形单选钮)
} ui_check_mode_t;

typedef struct {
    char        label[32];      // 选项文本
    bool        is_checked;     // 勾选状态
    bool        is_disabled;    // 禁用置灰状态
} ui_check_item_t;

typedef struct ui_checkbox_group ui_checkbox_group_t;

typedef void (*ui_checkbox_cb_t)(ui_checkbox_group_t *cg, uint8_t index, bool is_checked, void *user_data);

struct ui_checkbox_group {
    ui_rect_t           rect;                           // 控件物理包围盒
    ui_check_mode_t     mode;                           // 运行模式 (多选 / 单选互斥)
    ui_check_item_t     items[UI_CHECK_MAX_ITEMS];
    uint8_t             item_count;                     // 选项总数
    int16_t             item_h;                         // 单行高度 (默认 36px)

    // ⚡ 滚动与视口机制
    int16_t             scroll_y;                       // 当前 Y 轴滚动偏移
    int16_t             content_h;                      // 虚拟内容总高度 (item_count * item_h)

    // ⚡ 触控手势防抖与滑动追踪状态机
    int16_t             down_x;
    int16_t             down_y;
    int16_t             scroll_start_y;
    bool                is_touch_down;
    bool                is_dragged;                     // 8px 防抖死区锁定
    bool                is_scrolling;                   // 是否处于拖拽滚动中

    // 配色方案 (RGB565)
    uint16_t            color_bg;                       // 背景底色 (极深黑蓝)
    uint16_t            color_box_bg;                   // 勾选框未选中底色
    uint16_t            color_box_border;               // 勾选框边框色
    uint16_t            color_checked;                  // 勾选激活高亮色 (亮绿/青蓝)
    uint16_t            color_text;                     // 选项文字颜色
    uint16_t            color_text_disabled;            // 禁用置灰文字色
    uint16_t            color_border;                   // 控件外框分割线
    uint16_t            color_scrollbar;                // 滚动条滑块颜色

    ui_checkbox_cb_t    on_change;
    void               *user_data;
};

// 初始化支持滑动的复选框/单选组
void ui_checkbox_group_init(ui_checkbox_group_t *cg, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h,
                            ui_check_mode_t mode, int16_t item_h, ui_checkbox_cb_t on_change);

// 添加选项条目 (自动重算总虚拟高度)
bool ui_checkbox_group_add_item(ui_checkbox_group_t *cg, const char *label, bool init_checked, bool is_disabled);

// 设置指定选项的勾选状态
void ui_checkbox_group_set_checked(ui_checkbox_group_t *cg, uint8_t index, bool checked);

// 查询指定选项是否被勾选
bool ui_checkbox_group_is_checked(const ui_checkbox_group_t *cg, uint8_t index);

// 清空全部选项
void ui_checkbox_group_clear(ui_checkbox_group_t *cg);

// 触控交互驱动 (支持拖拽滚动 + 8px 死区保护 + Touch-Up 抬手勾选)
bool ui_checkbox_group_process_touch(ui_checkbox_group_t *cg, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick);

// 切片光栅化渲染 (带 O(1) 行剪枝与视口硬裁剪)
void ui_checkbox_group_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_checkbox_group_t *cg);

// 获取控件局部脏矩形包围盒
static inline ui_rect_t ui_checkbox_group_get_dirty_rect(const ui_checkbox_group_t *cg) {
    return cg ? cg->rect : ui_rect_make(0, 0, 0, 0);
}

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

#endif // UI_CHECKBOX_GROUP_H

void ui_render_tiles(uint8_t start_row, uint8_t end_row, ui_scene_draw_cb_t draw_scene)
{
    if (!draw_scene || start_row >= UI_TILE_ROWS) return;
    if (end_row >= UI_TILE_ROWS) end_row = UI_TILE_ROWS - 1;
    if (start_row > end_row) return;

    for (uint8_t row = start_row; row <= end_row; row++) {
        // 1. 获取当前切片显存指针 (内部自动复用乒乓 Buffer)[cite: 8]
        ui_tile_t *tile = ui_tile_get_next(row);

        // 2. 触发场景光栅化合成[cite: 8]
        draw_scene(tile);

        // 3. 启动整行 DMA 异步推流并切换双缓冲[cite: 8]
        ui_tile_flush(tile);
    }

    // 4. 等待最后一包传输收尾[cite: 8]
    ui_tile_wait_finish();
}

void ui_render_frame(ui_scene_draw_cb_t draw_scene)
{
    ui_render_tiles(0, UI_TILE_ROWS - 1, draw_scene);
}

void ui_render_dirty_clipped(const ui_rect_t *dirty, ui_scene_draw_cb_t draw_scene)
{
    if (!dirty || !draw_scene || dirty->w <= 0 || dirty->h <= 0) return;

    // 1. 计算受脏矩形影响的切片行起止索引
    int16_t start_row = dirty->y / UI_TILE_H;
    int16_t end_row   = (dirty->y + dirty->h - 1) / UI_TILE_H;

    // 边界安全收敛
    if (start_row < 0) start_row = 0;
    if (end_row >= UI_TILE_ROWS) end_row = UI_TILE_ROWS - 1;
    if (start_row > end_row) return;

    // 2. 仅遍历相交的切片行
    for (uint8_t row = (uint8_t)start_row; row <= (uint8_t)end_row; row++) {
        ui_tile_t *tile = ui_tile_get_next(row);

        // 3. 计算脏矩形与当前切片的物理重叠矩形
        ui_rect_t clip_rect;
        if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, dirty, &clip_rect)) {
            continue;
        }

        // ⚡ 核心补齐：强制约束软光栅视口！
        // 超出 clip_rect 的任何右侧组件，ui_draw 内部在打点前直接 0 开销跳过
        ui_draw_set_clip(&clip_rect);

        // 4. 执行场景合成 (受 clip 保护，右侧完全不参与计算)
        draw_scene(tile);

        // 恢复视口
        ui_draw_reset_clip();

        // 5. 计算子矩形在切片显存内部的相对偏移
        uint16_t src_off_x = (uint16_t)(clip_rect.x - tile->rect.x);
        uint16_t src_off_y = (uint16_t)(clip_rect.y - tile->rect.y);

        // 6. 调用 LCD 驱动进行单次 DMA 局部开窗极速推流
        LCD_PushSubTile((uint16_t)clip_rect.x, (uint16_t)clip_rect.y,
                        (uint16_t)clip_rect.w, (uint16_t)clip_rect.h,
                        tile->buf, UI_TILE_W,
                        src_off_x, src_off_y);
    }

    ui_tile_wait_finish();
}

/**

 * @param dirty      待更新的目标局部脏矩形
 * @param draw_scene 场景绘制回调
 */

ui_draw.c.c 287 行 · 9670 B
点击展开
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include "lcd.h"
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

/* ================= 1. 全局裁剪视口与基础打点 ================= */

static ui_rect_t g_clip_rect = {0, 0, LCD_WIDTH, LCD_HEIGHT};

void ui_draw_set_clip(const ui_rect_t *clip) {
    if (clip) {
        g_clip_rect = *clip;
    } else {
        ui_draw_reset_clip();
    }
}

void ui_draw_reset_clip(void) {
    g_clip_rect.x = 0;
    g_clip_rect.y = 0;
    g_clip_rect.w = LCD_WIDTH;
    g_clip_rect.h = LCD_HEIGHT;
}

ui_rect_t ui_draw_get_clip(void) {
    return g_clip_rect;
}

void ui_draw_point(ui_tile_t *tile, int16_t x, int16_t y, uint16_t color) {
    if (!tile || !tile->buf) return;

    // 1. 全局视口裁剪判定
    if (x < g_clip_rect.x || x >= (g_clip_rect.x + g_clip_rect.w) ||
        y < g_clip_rect.y || y >= (g_clip_rect.y + g_clip_rect.h)) {
        return;
    }

    // 2. 当前切片内存边界判定
    if (!ui_rect_contains_point(&tile->rect, x, y)) {
        return;
    }

    int16_t lx = x - tile->rect.x;
    int16_t ly = y - tile->rect.y;
    tile->buf[ly * UI_TILE_W + lx] = color;
}

/* ================= 2. 几何图元快速光栅化 ================= */

void ui_draw_fill_rect(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_rect_t *rect, uint16_t color) {
    if (!tile || !tile->buf || !rect || rect->w <= 0 || rect->h <= 0) return;

    // 视口交集
    ui_rect_t clip_v;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(rect, &g_clip_rect, &clip_v)) return;

    // 切片交集
    ui_rect_t clip_t;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &clip_v, &clip_t)) return;

    for (int16_t y = clip_t.y; y < clip_t.y + clip_t.h; y++) {
        int16_t ly = y - tile->rect.y;
        uint16_t *line = &tile->buf[ly * UI_TILE_W + (clip_t.x - tile->rect.x)];
        for (int16_t x = 0; x < clip_t.w; x++) {
            line[x] = color;
        }
    }
}

void ui_draw_fast_hline(ui_tile_t *tile, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, uint16_t color) {
    ui_rect_t r = ui_rect_make(x, y, w, 1);
    ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &r, color);
}

void ui_draw_fast_vline(ui_tile_t *tile, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t h, uint16_t color) {
    ui_rect_t r = ui_rect_make(x, y, 1, h);
    ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &r, color);
}

void ui_draw_rect(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_rect_t *rect, uint16_t color) {
    if (!tile || !rect || rect->w <= 0 || rect->h <= 0) return;
    ui_draw_fast_hline(tile, rect->x, rect->y, rect->w, color);
    ui_draw_fast_hline(tile, rect->x, rect->y + rect->h - 1, rect->w, color);
    ui_draw_fast_vline(tile, rect->x, rect->y, rect->h, color);
    ui_draw_fast_vline(tile, rect->x + rect->w - 1, rect->y, rect->h, color);
}

void ui_draw_line(ui_tile_t *tile, int16_t x0, int16_t y0, int16_t x1, int16_t y1, uint16_t color) {
    if (!tile) return;

    // 线段包围盒与切片求交，不相交直接跳过
    int16_t min_x = (x0 < x1) ? x0 : x1;
    int16_t max_x = (x0 > x1) ? x0 : x1;
    int16_t min_y = (y0 < y1) ? y0 : y1;
    int16_t max_y = (y0 > y1) ? y0 : y1;
    ui_rect_t box = ui_rect_make(min_x, min_y, (int16_t)(max_x - min_x + 1), (int16_t)(max_y - min_y + 1));
    ui_rect_t ov;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &box, &ov)) return;

    int16_t dx = abs(x1 - x0);
    int16_t sx = (x0 < x1) ? 1 : -1;
    int16_t dy = -abs(y1 - y0);
    int16_t sy = (y0 < y1) ? 1 : -1;
    int16_t err = dx + dy;

    while (1) {
        ui_draw_point(tile, x0, y0, color);
        if (x0 == x1 && y0 == y1) break;
        int16_t e2 = 2 * err;
        if (e2 >= dy) { err += dy; x0 += sx; }
        if (e2 <= dx) { err += dx; y0 += sy; }
    }
}

void ui_draw_circle(ui_tile_t *tile, int16_t x0, int16_t y0, int16_t r, uint16_t color) {
    if (!tile || r <= 0) return;

    ui_rect_t box = ui_rect_make((int16_t)(x0 - r), (int16_t)(y0 - r), (int16_t)(2 * r + 1), (int16_t)(2 * r + 1));
    ui_rect_t ov;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &box, &ov)) return;

    int16_t x = 0;
    int16_t y = r;
    int16_t d = 3 - 2 * r;

    while (x <= y) {
        ui_draw_point(tile, x0 + x, y0 + y, color);
        ui_draw_point(tile, x0 - x, y0 + y, color);
        ui_draw_point(tile, x0 + x, y0 - y, color);
        ui_draw_point(tile, x0 - x, y0 - y, color);
        ui_draw_point(tile, x0 + y, y0 + x, color);
        ui_draw_point(tile, x0 - y, y0 + x, color);
        ui_draw_point(tile, x0 + y, y0 - x, color);
        ui_draw_point(tile, x0 - y, y0 - x, color);

        if (d < 0) {
            d += 4 * x + 6;
        } else {
            d += 4 * (x - y) + 10;
            y--;
        }
        x++;
    }
}

void ui_draw_fill_circle(ui_tile_t *tile, int16_t x0, int16_t y0, int16_t r, uint16_t color) {
    if (!tile || r <= 0) return;

    ui_rect_t box = ui_rect_make((int16_t)(x0 - r), (int16_t)(y0 - r), (int16_t)(2 * r + 1), (int16_t)(2 * r + 1));
    ui_rect_t ov;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &box, &ov)) return;

    int16_t x = 0;
    int16_t y = r;
    int16_t d = 3 - 2 * r;

    while (x <= y) {
        ui_draw_fast_hline(tile, x0 - x, y0 + y, 2 * x + 1, color);
        ui_draw_fast_hline(tile, x0 - x, y0 - y, 2 * x + 1, color);
        ui_draw_fast_hline(tile, x0 - y, y0 + x, 2 * y + 1, color);
        ui_draw_fast_hline(tile, x0 - y, y0 - x, 2 * y + 1, color);

        if (d < 0) {
            d += 4 * x + 6;
        } else {
            d += 4 * (x - y) + 10;
            y--;
        }
        x++;
    }
}

/* ================= 3. 点阵与文本优化光栅化 ================= */

void ui_draw_bitmap(ui_tile_t *tile, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h, 
                    const uint8_t *bitmap, uint16_t color, uint16_t bg_color) {
    if (!tile || !bitmap || w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;

    ui_rect_t box = ui_rect_make(x, y, w, h);
    ui_rect_t ov_clip;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&box, &g_clip_rect, &ov_clip)) return;
    ui_rect_t ov;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &ov_clip, &ov)) return;

    int16_t bytes_per_row = (w + 7) / 8;

    for (int16_t r = (ov.y - y); r < (ov.y - y + ov.h); r++) {
        for (int16_t c = (ov.x - x); c < (ov.x - x + ov.w); c++) {
            uint8_t byte_data = bitmap[r * bytes_per_row + (c / 8)];
            bool is_set = (byte_data & (0x80 >> (c % 8))) != 0;

            int16_t px = x + c;
            int16_t py = y + r;

            if (is_set) {
                ui_draw_point(tile, px, py, color);
            } else if (bg_color != color) {
                ui_draw_point(tile, px, py, bg_color);
            }
        }
    }
}

extern const uint8_t Font5x7[][5];

void ui_draw_char(ui_tile_t *tile, int16_t x, int16_t y, char c, 
                  FontSize_t size, uint16_t color, uint16_t bg) {
    if (!tile) return;
    if (c < ' ' || c > '~') c = ' ';

    uint8_t scale = (size >= 24) ? 2 : 1;
    uint16_t char_w = (scale == 2) ? 12 : 6;
    uint16_t glyph_h = 8 * scale;
    uint16_t y_offset = (size > glyph_h) ? (size - glyph_h) / 2 : 0;

    ui_rect_t box = ui_rect_make(x, y, char_w, size);
    ui_rect_t ov;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &box, &ov)) return;

    const uint8_t *p = Font5x7[c - ' '];

    for (uint16_t row = 0; row < size; row++) {
        int16_t py = y + row;
        if (py < ov.y || py >= ov.y + ov.h) continue;

        for (uint16_t col = 0; col < char_w; col++) {
            int16_t px = x + col;
            if (px < ov.x || px >= ov.x + ov.w) continue;

            bool is_fg = false;
            if (row >= y_offset && row < y_offset + glyph_h) {
                uint16_t font_row = (row - y_offset) / scale;
                uint16_t font_col = col / scale;
                if (font_col < 5 && font_row < 8) {
                    if (p[font_col] & (1 << font_row)) {
                        is_fg = true;
                    }
                }
            }

            if (is_fg) {
                ui_draw_point(tile, px, py, color);
            } else if (bg != color) {
                ui_draw_point(tile, px, py, bg);
            }
        }
    }
}

void ui_draw_string(ui_tile_t *tile, int16_t x, int16_t y, const char *str, 
                    FontSize_t size, uint16_t color, uint16_t bg) {
    if (!tile || !str) return;

    int16_t cur_x = x;
    int16_t cur_y = y;

    uint8_t asc_scale = (size >= 24) ? 2 : 1;
    uint16_t asc_step = (asc_scale == 2) ? 12 : 6;

    while (*str) {
        if ((uint8_t)*str < 0x80) {
            char ch = *str++;
            ui_rect_t char_box = ui_rect_make(cur_x, cur_y, asc_step, size);
            ui_rect_t ov;
            if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &char_box, &ov)) {
                ui_draw_char(tile, cur_x, cur_y, ch, size, color, bg);
            }
            cur_x += asc_step;
        } else {
            if (*(str + 1) == '\0') break;

            uint16_t gb = ((uint8_t)str[0] << 8) | (uint8_t)str[1];
            str += 2;

            ui_rect_t hz_box = ui_rect_make(cur_x, cur_y, size, size);
            ui_rect_t ov;
            // ⚡ 核心优化：仅当汉字范围与当前切片重叠时，才从 Flash 读取点阵数据！
            if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &hz_box, &ov)) {
                uint16_t bytes_per = 0;
                const uint8_t *dot_buf = GetFontBitmap(gb, size, &bytes_per);
                if (dot_buf) {
                    ui_draw_bitmap(tile, cur_x, cur_y, size, size, dot_buf, color, bg);
                }
            }
            cur_x += size;
        }
    }
}

ui_drawer.c.c 202 行 · 7476 B
点击展开
#include "ui_drawer.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

#define TOUCH_SLOP_PX   8 // 8 像素滑动防抖死区

static void drawer_scroll_content_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_rect_t *viewport, int16_t scroll_y, void *user_data) {
    ui_drawer_t *d = (ui_drawer_t *)user_data;
    if (!d || !tile || !viewport) return;

    int16_t base_y = viewport->y - scroll_y;

    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < d->app_count; i++) {
        int16_t item_y = base_y + i * 42 + 3;
        ui_rect_t item_rect = ui_rect_make((int16_t)(viewport->x + 4), item_y, (int16_t)(viewport->w - 8), 36);

        // 1. 视口裁剪
        ui_rect_t item_vp;
        if (!ui_rect_intersect(&item_rect, viewport, &item_vp)) continue;

        // 2. 切片显存裁剪
        ui_rect_t item_tile;
        if (!ui_rect_intersect(&item_vp, &tile->rect, &item_tile)) continue;

        app_t *app = d->apps[i];
        if (!app) continue;

        bool is_sel = (d->selected_idx == (int8_t)i);

        // ⚡ 固定底色：统一采用 0x10A2 暗底色，消除滑动闪烁
        uint16_t ibg = 0x10A2;
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &item_tile, ibg);
        ui_draw_rect(tile, &item_vp, 0x2126);

        // 3. 文字与图标视口边界安全守卫
        int16_t text_y = item_rect.y + 10;
        if (text_y >= viewport->y && (text_y + 16) <= (viewport->y + viewport->h)) {
            // 图标徽章
            ui_rect_t badge = ui_rect_make((int16_t)(item_rect.x + 4), (int16_t)(item_rect.y + 5), 32, 26);
            ui_rect_t badge_tile;
            if (ui_rect_intersect(&badge, &item_tile, &badge_tile)) {
                ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &badge_tile, app->icon_color);
                int16_t sym_w = (int16_t)strlen(app->icon_symbol) * 8;
                int16_t sx = (int16_t)(badge.x + (badge.w - sym_w) / 2);
                ui_draw_string(tile, sx, (int16_t)(badge.y + 5), app->icon_symbol, FONT_16, 0x0000, app->icon_color);
            }

            // 应用文字标题 (当前选中的应用青色高亮，其他为白色)
            ui_draw_string(tile, (int16_t)(item_rect.x + 40), text_y, app->name, FONT_16, is_sel ? 0x07FF : 0xFFFF, ibg);
        }
    }
}

void ui_drawer_init(ui_drawer_t *d, int16_t y, int16_t h, int16_t w, ui_drawer_cb_t on_select, void *user_data) {
    if (!d) return;

    // 抽屉高度固定为 204px (36px 顶栏 + 168px 内部视口，容纳 4 个完整选项)
    int16_t exact_drawer_h = 204;
    d->rect = ui_rect_make(0, y, w, exact_drawer_h);
    d->is_open = false;

    d->app_count      = 0;
    d->selected_idx   = 0;
    d->pressed_idx    = -1;
    d->on_select      = on_select;
    d->user_data      = user_data;
    d->is_touch_down  = false;
    d->is_dragged     = false;

    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < UI_DRAWER_MAX_APPS; i++) {
        d->apps[i] = NULL;
    }

    ui_scrollview_init(&d->sv_body, 0, (int16_t)(y + 36), w, 168, 400, drawer_scroll_content_draw);
    d->sv_body.user_data       = d;
    d->sv_body.color_bg        = 0x1084;
    d->sv_body.color_scrollbar = 0x07FF;
}

bool ui_drawer_register_app(ui_drawer_t *d, app_t *app) {
    if (!d || !app || d->app_count >= UI_DRAWER_MAX_APPS) return false;
    d->apps[d->app_count++] = app;
    d->sv_body.content_h = d->app_count * 42 + 6;
    return true;
}

void ui_drawer_open(ui_drawer_t *d) {
    if (!d) return;
    d->is_open = true;
    d->pressed_idx = -1;
    d->is_touch_down = false;
    d->is_dragged = false;
}

void ui_drawer_close(ui_drawer_t *d) {
    if (!d) return;
    d->is_open = false;
    d->pressed_idx = -1;
    d->is_touch_down = false;
    d->is_dragged = false;
}

void ui_drawer_toggle(ui_drawer_t *d) {
    if (!d) return;
    if (d->is_open) ui_drawer_close(d);
    else ui_drawer_open(d);
}

bool ui_drawer_process_touch(ui_drawer_t *d, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    if (!d || !d->is_open || d->app_count == 0) return false;

    // 1. 点击抽屉外部空白区域 -> 收起抽屉
    if (is_touching && (tx > d->rect.w || ty < d->rect.y || ty > (d->rect.y + d->rect.h))) {
        ui_drawer_close(d);
        return true;
    }

    bool refresh = false;

    // 2. 触控状态机调度
    if (is_touching) {
        if (!d->is_touch_down) {
            // A. 手指按下 (Touch Down): 仅记录坐标起点
            d->is_touch_down = true;
            d->is_dragged    = false;
            d->down_x        = tx;
            d->down_y        = ty;
        } else {
            // B. 手指移动 (Touch Move): 超过 8px 判定为滑动
            int16_t dy = abs(ty - d->down_y);
            int16_t dx = abs(tx - d->down_x);

            if (dy >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX || dx >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX) {
                d->is_dragged = true;
            }
        }

        // C. 将触控事件送入 scrollview 计算位移
        refresh |= ui_scrollview_process_touch(&d->sv_body, true, tx, ty, now_tick);

    } else {
        // D. ⚡ 手指松开 (Touch Up): 唯一允许跳转判定的时机
        if (d->is_touch_down) {
            d->is_touch_down = false;

            refresh |= ui_scrollview_process_touch(&d->sv_body, false, tx, ty, now_tick);

            // 仅在完全没有滑动的纯点击时触发跳转
            if (!d->is_dragged) {
                int16_t rel_y = ty - d->sv_body.view_rect.y + d->sv_body.scroll_y;
                if (rel_y >= 0 && tx <= d->rect.w && ty >= d->sv_body.view_rect.y && ty < (d->sv_body.view_rect.y + d->sv_body.view_rect.h)) {
                    uint8_t sel = (uint8_t)(rel_y / 42);
                    if (sel < d->app_count) {
                        d->selected_idx = (int8_t)sel;
                        ui_drawer_close(d);
                        if (d->on_select) {
                            d->on_select(sel, d->user_data);
                        }
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }

            d->is_dragged = false;
        }
    }

    return refresh;
}

void ui_drawer_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_drawer_t *d) {
    if (!tile || !d || !d->is_open) return;

    // 图层 1: 底板
    ui_rect_t panel_ov;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &d->rect, &panel_ov)) return;
    ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &panel_ov, 0x1084);

    // 图层 2: 滚动列表项
    ui_scrollview_draw(tile, &d->sv_body);

    // 图层 3: 标头遮罩 (盖住向上滑出的残留文字)
    ui_rect_t hdr_rect = ui_rect_make(0, d->rect.y, d->rect.w, 36);
    ui_rect_t hdr_ov;
    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &hdr_rect, &hdr_ov)) {
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &hdr_ov, 0x18C3);
        ui_rect_t hline = ui_rect_make(0, (int16_t)(d->rect.y + 35), d->rect.w, 1);
        ui_rect_t hline_ov;
        if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &hline, &hline_ov)) {
            ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &hline_ov, 0x07FF);
        }
        ui_draw_string(tile, 38, (int16_t)(d->rect.y + 10), "功能清单", FONT_16, 0x07FF, 0x18C3);
    }

    // 图层 4: 右侧发光分割线
    ui_rect_t vline_rect = ui_rect_make((int16_t)(d->rect.w - 1), d->rect.y, 1, d->rect.h);
    ui_rect_t vline_ov;
    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &vline_rect, &vline_ov)) {
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &vline_ov, 0x07FF);
    }
}

    ui_rect_t       rect;           // 抽屉整体物理包围盒
    bool            is_open;        // 打开/收起状态
    int8_t          selected_idx;   // 当前选中高亮索引

} ui_drawer_t;

ui_dropdown.c.c 210 行 · 8345 B
点击展开
#include "ui_dropdown.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

#define TOUCH_SLOP_PX   8 // 8 像素滑动防抖死区

/* ================= 1. 内部辅助函数 ================= */

static inline bool ui_dd_hit_rect(const ui_rect_t *rect, int16_t x, int16_t y) {
    return (x >= rect->x && x < (rect->x + rect->w) &&
            y >= rect->y && y < (rect->y + rect->h));
}

static uint8_t ui_dd_get_font_height(FontSize_t size) {
    switch (size) {
        case FONT_16: return 16;
        case FONT_20: return 20;
        case FONT_24: return 24;
        default:      return 16;
    }
}

ui_rect_t ui_dropdown_get_total_rect(const ui_dropdown_t *dd) {
    if (!dd) return ui_rect_make(0, 0, 0, 0);
    ui_rect_t r = dd->rect;
    if (dd->is_open && dd->items && dd->item_count > 0) {
        r.h = (int16_t)(dd->rect.h * (1 + dd->item_count));
    }
    return r;
}

/* ================= 2. 初始化与配置 ================= */

void ui_dropdown_init(ui_dropdown_t *dd, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h,
                      const char **items, uint8_t item_count, int8_t default_idx,
                      ui_dropdown_cb_t on_change) {
    if (!dd) return;

    dd->rect          = ui_rect_make(x, y, w, h);
    dd->items         = items;
    dd->item_count    = item_count;
    dd->selected_idx  = (default_idx >= 0 && default_idx < item_count) ? default_idx : 0;

    dd->is_open       = false;
    dd->font_size     = FONT_16;

    dd->color_bg      = 0x18C3; // 深灰黑底色
    dd->color_border  = 0x7BEF; // 浅灰边框
    dd->color_text    = 0xFFFF; // 白色文字
    dd->color_active  = 0x07E0; // 绿色高亮

    dd->down_x        = 0;
    dd->down_y        = 0;
    dd->is_touch_down = false;
    dd->is_dragged    = false;

    dd->on_change     = on_change;
    dd->user_data     = NULL;
}

void ui_dropdown_set_color(ui_dropdown_t *dd, uint16_t bg, uint16_t border, uint16_t text, uint16_t active) {
    if (!dd) return;
    dd->color_bg     = bg;
    dd->color_border = border;
    dd->color_text   = text;
    dd->color_active = active;
}

/* ================= 3. 触控状态机 (Touch Slop + Touch-Up 触发) ================= */

bool ui_dropdown_process_touch(ui_dropdown_t *dd, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    (void)now_tick;
    if (!dd) return false;

    if (is_touching) {
        if (!dd->is_touch_down) {
            // A. 初次按下 (Touch Down): 仅记录起点坐标
            dd->is_touch_down = true;
            dd->is_dragged    = false;
            dd->down_x        = tx;
            dd->down_y        = ty;
        } else {
            // B. 持续移动 (Touch Move): 8px 防抖死区判定
            int16_t dx = abs(tx - dd->down_x);
            int16_t dy = abs(ty - dd->down_y);
            if (!dd->is_dragged && (dx >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX || dy >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX)) {
                dd->is_dragged = true; // 锁定为滑动，禁止触发选择
            }
        }
        return false;
    } else {
        // C. ⚡ 手指松开 (Touch Up): 唯一允许触发展开、收起或选中的时机
        if (dd->is_touch_down) {
            dd->is_touch_down = false;

            // 只有未发生滑动的纯点按才执行动作
            if (!dd->is_dragged) {
                // 1. 收起状态：点击表头展开
                if (!dd->is_open) {
                    if (ui_dd_hit_rect(&dd->rect, tx, ty)) {
                        dd->is_open = true;
                        return true;
                    }
                }
                // 2. 展开状态：点击选项、表头或外部
                else {
                    ui_rect_t total_rect = ui_dropdown_get_total_rect(dd);

                    // 点击在表头上 -> 收起
                    if (ui_dd_hit_rect(&dd->rect, tx, ty)) {
                        dd->is_open = false;
                        return true;
                    }
                    // 点击在下拉选项列表中 -> 选中并收起
                    else if (ui_dd_hit_rect(&total_rect, tx, ty)) {
                        int16_t rel_y = ty - (dd->rect.y + dd->rect.h);
                        int8_t clicked_idx = (int8_t)(rel_y / dd->rect.h);

                        if (clicked_idx >= 0 && clicked_idx < dd->item_count) {
                            dd->selected_idx = clicked_idx;
                            dd->is_open = false;

                            if (dd->on_change) {
                                dd->on_change(dd, dd->selected_idx, dd->user_data);
                            }
                            return true;
                        }
                    }
                    // 点击在外部空白区 -> 收起
                    else {
                        dd->is_open = false;
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
            dd->is_dragged = false;
        }
    }

    return false;
}

/* ================= 4. 切片安全光栅化渲染 ================= */

void ui_dropdown_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_dropdown_t *dd) {
    if (!tile || !dd) return;

    ui_rect_t total_rect = ui_dropdown_get_total_rect(dd);
    ui_rect_t overlap;

    // 1. 📐 与当前切片显存做快速包围盒裁剪
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &total_rect, &overlap)) {
        return;
    }

    uint8_t font_h = ui_dd_get_font_height(dd->font_size);
    int16_t text_pad_y = (dd->rect.h - font_h) / 2;

    // 2. 🔲 绘制主表头
    ui_rect_t hdr_ov;
    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &dd->rect, &hdr_ov)) {
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &hdr_ov, dd->color_bg);
        ui_draw_rect(tile, &dd->rect, dd->is_open ? dd->color_active : dd->color_border);

        // 绘制表头选中文字 (带切片相交防护)
        if (dd->items && dd->selected_idx >= 0 && dd->selected_idx < dd->item_count) {
            int16_t ty = dd->rect.y + text_pad_y;
            if ((ty + font_h) > tile->rect.y && ty < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
                ui_draw_string(tile, dd->rect.x + 6, ty, 
                               dd->items[dd->selected_idx], dd->font_size, dd->color_text, dd->color_bg);
            }
        }

        // 绘制右侧三角指示图标 (▼ / ▲)
        int16_t arrow_cx = dd->rect.x + dd->rect.w - 10;
        int16_t arrow_cy = dd->rect.y + dd->rect.h / 2;
        if (dd->is_open) {
            ui_draw_line(tile, arrow_cx - 4, arrow_cy + 2, arrow_cx + 4, arrow_cy + 2, dd->color_active);
            ui_draw_line(tile, arrow_cx - 2, arrow_cy,     arrow_cx + 2, arrow_cy,     dd->color_active);
            ui_draw_line(tile, arrow_cx,     arrow_cy - 2, arrow_cx,     arrow_cy - 2, dd->color_active);
        } else {
            ui_draw_line(tile, arrow_cx - 4, arrow_cy - 2, arrow_cx + 4, arrow_cy - 2, dd->color_border);
            ui_draw_line(tile, arrow_cx - 2, arrow_cy,     arrow_cx + 2, arrow_cy,     dd->color_border);
            ui_draw_line(tile, arrow_cx,     arrow_cy + 2, arrow_cx,     arrow_cy + 2, dd->color_border);
        }
    }

    // 3. 📜 绘制下拉选项列表
    if (dd->is_open && dd->items) {
        for (uint8_t i = 0; i < dd->item_count; i++) {
            ui_rect_t item_rect = ui_rect_make(dd->rect.x, (int16_t)(dd->rect.y + (i + 1) * dd->rect.h), dd->rect.w, dd->rect.h);
            ui_rect_t item_ov;

            if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &item_rect, &item_ov)) {
                bool is_cur = (i == dd->selected_idx);
                uint16_t item_bg = is_cur ? 0x000F : dd->color_bg;
                uint16_t item_fg = is_cur ? dd->color_active : dd->color_text;

                ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &item_ov, item_bg);
                ui_draw_rect(tile, &item_rect, dd->color_border);

                int16_t ty = item_rect.y + text_pad_y;
                if ((ty + font_h) > tile->rect.y && ty < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
                    ui_draw_string(tile, item_rect.x + 6, ty,
                                   dd->items[i], dd->font_size, item_fg, item_bg);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

ui_file_browser.c.c 315 行 · 13111 B
点击展开
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <ctype.h>

#include "ui_file_browser.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"

#define TOUCH_SLOP_PX   8 // 8 像素滑动防抖死区

static bool str_ends_with_case_insensitive(const char *str, const char *ext) {
    if (!str || !ext) return false; /*[cite: 18] */
    size_t str_len = strlen(str); /*[cite: 18] */
    size_t ext_len = strlen(ext); /*[cite: 18] */
    if (str_len < ext_len) return false; /*[cite: 18] */

    const char *p_str = str + (str_len - ext_len); /*[cite: 18] */
    while (*p_str && *ext) { /*[cite: 18] */
        if (tolower((unsigned char)*p_str) != tolower((unsigned char)*ext)) { /*[cite: 18] */
            return false; /*[cite: 18] */
        }
        p_str++; /*[cite: 18] */
        ext++; /*[cite: 18] */
    }
    return true; /*[cite: 18] */
}

static ui_file_type_t detect_file_type(const char *name, bool is_dir) {
    if (is_dir) return UI_FILE_TYPE_DIR; /*[cite: 18] */
    if (!name) return UI_FILE_TYPE_UNKNOWN; /*[cite: 18] */

    if (str_ends_with_case_insensitive(name, ".bin")) return UI_FILE_TYPE_BIN; /*[cite: 18] */
    if (str_ends_with_case_insensitive(name, ".hex")) return UI_FILE_TYPE_HEX; /*[cite: 18] */
    if (str_ends_with_case_insensitive(name, ".uf2")) return UI_FILE_TYPE_UF2; /*[cite: 18] */
    if (str_ends_with_case_insensitive(name, ".txt") || str_ends_with_case_insensitive(name, ".log")) { /*[cite: 18] */
        return UI_FILE_TYPE_TXT; /*[cite: 18] */
    }

    return UI_FILE_TYPE_UNKNOWN; /*[cite: 18] */
}

// ⚡ 极速文件名按像素宽度安全截断与防溢出算法
static void truncate_filename_by_width(const char *src, char *dst, size_t dst_size, int16_t max_pixel_w) {
    if (!src || !dst || dst_size == 0 || max_pixel_w <= 0) {
        if (dst && dst_size) dst[0] = '\0';
        return;
    }

    int16_t cur_w = 0;
    size_t s_idx = 0;
    size_t d_idx = 0;

    int16_t limit_w = max_pixel_w - 12; // 预留 ".." (12px) 宽度
    if (limit_w < 18) limit_w = max_pixel_w;

    bool truncated = false;
    while (src[s_idx] && (d_idx + 3 < dst_size)) {
        uint8_t c = (uint8_t)src[s_idx];
        int16_t char_w = (c < 0x80) ? 6 : 16;
        size_t step = (c < 0x80) ? 1 : 2;

        if (cur_w + char_w > limit_w) {
            truncated = true;
            break;
        }

        if (step == 2 && src[s_idx + 1] == '\0') break;

        for (size_t k = 0; k < step; k++) {
            dst[d_idx++] = src[s_idx++];
        }
        cur_w += char_w;
    }

    if (truncated && (d_idx + 2 < dst_size)) {
        dst[d_idx++] = '.';
        dst[d_idx++] = '.';
    }
    dst[d_idx] = '\0';
}

void ui_file_browser_init(ui_file_browser_t *fb, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h, ui_file_select_cb_t on_select) {
    if (!fb) return; /*[cite: 18] */

    fb->rect = ui_rect_make(x, y, w, h); /*[cite: 18] */
    fb->item_count   = 0; /*[cite: 18] */
    fb->selected_idx = -1; /*[cite: 18] */
    fb->item_h       = 30; // 紧凑行高 (30px)
    fb->scroll_y     = 0; /*[cite: 18] */

    fb->is_scrolling    = false; /*[cite: 18] */
    fb->is_touch_down   = false;
    fb->is_dragged      = false;
    fb->down_x          = 0;
    fb->down_y          = 0;
    fb->scroll_start_y  = 0;

    fb->color_bg        = 0x0842; /* 极深灰黑底[cite: 18] */
    fb->color_item_bg   = 0x1082; /* 深蓝黑条目底[cite: 18] */
    fb->color_active_bg = 0x2146; /* 选中条目暗青蓝高亮[cite: 18] */
    fb->color_border    = 0x39E7; /* 分割线深灰[cite: 18] */
    fb->color_scrollbar = 0x07FF; /* 青蓝滚动指示条[cite: 18] */

    fb->on_select = on_select; /*[cite: 18] */
    fb->user_data = NULL; /*[cite: 18] */
}

void ui_file_browser_clear(ui_file_browser_t *fb) {
    if (!fb) return; /*[cite: 18] */
    fb->item_count   = 0; /*[cite: 18] */
    fb->selected_idx = -1; /*[cite: 18] */
    fb->scroll_y     = 0; /*[cite: 18] */
}

bool ui_file_browser_add_item(ui_file_browser_t *fb, const char *name, uint32_t size_bytes, bool is_dir) {
    if (!fb || !name || fb->item_count >= UI_FB_MAX_ITEMS) return false; /*[cite: 18] */

    ui_file_item_t *item = &fb->items[fb->item_count]; /*[cite: 18] */
    strncpy(item->name, name, UI_FB_NAME_LEN - 1); /*[cite: 18] */
    item->name[UI_FB_NAME_LEN - 1] = '\0'; /*[cite: 18] */
    item->size_bytes = size_bytes; /*[cite: 18] */
    item->type       = detect_file_type(name, is_dir); /*[cite: 18] */

    fb->item_count++; /*[cite: 18] */
    return true; /*[cite: 18] */
}

const ui_file_item_t *ui_file_browser_get_selected(const ui_file_browser_t *fb) {
    if (!fb || fb->selected_idx < 0 || fb->selected_idx >= (int8_t)fb->item_count) return NULL; /*[cite: 18] */
    return &fb->items[fb->selected_idx]; /*[cite: 18] */
}

/* ================= 触控状态机 (8px 防抖 + Touch-Up 触发) ================= */

bool ui_file_browser_process_touch(ui_file_browser_t *fb, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    (void)now_tick;
    if (!fb || fb->item_count == 0) return false; /*[cite: 18] */

    int16_t total_h = (int16_t)fb->item_count * fb->item_h; /*[cite: 18] */
    int16_t max_scroll = total_h - fb->rect.h; /*[cite: 18] */
    if (max_scroll < 0) max_scroll = 0; /*[cite: 18] */

    bool need_redraw = false;

    if (is_touching) {
        if (!fb->is_touch_down) {
            /* A. 初次按下 (Touch Down): 仅在视口内记录起点 */
            if (ui_rect_contains_point(&fb->rect, tx, ty)) {
                fb->is_touch_down  = true;
                fb->is_dragged     = false;
                fb->is_scrolling   = false;
                fb->down_x         = tx;
                fb->down_y         = ty;
                fb->scroll_start_y = fb->scroll_y;
            }
        } else {
            /* B. 持续移动 (Touch Move): 8px 防抖死区判定 */
            int16_t dy = ty - fb->down_y;
            int16_t dx = tx - fb->down_x;

            if (!fb->is_dragged && (abs(dy) >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX || abs(dx) >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX)) {
                fb->is_dragged   = true;
                fb->is_scrolling = true;
            }

            /* C. 拖拽滚动计算 */
            if (fb->is_dragged && max_scroll > 0) {
                int16_t new_scroll = fb->scroll_start_y - dy;
                if (new_scroll < 0) new_scroll = 0;
                if (new_scroll > max_scroll) new_scroll = max_scroll;

                if (fb->scroll_y != new_scroll) {
                    fb->scroll_y = new_scroll;
                    need_redraw  = true;
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        /* D. ⚡ 手指松开 (Touch Up): 唯一允许触发条目选中的时机 */
        if (fb->is_touch_down) {
            fb->is_touch_down = false;

            /* 只有从未发生滑动的纯点按，且松开点在视口内才触发选中 */
            if (!fb->is_dragged && ui_rect_contains_point(&fb->rect, tx, ty)) {
                int16_t rel_y = ty - (fb->rect.y - fb->scroll_y);
                if (rel_y >= 0 && rel_y < total_h) {
                    int8_t clicked_idx = (int8_t)(rel_y / fb->item_h);
                    if (clicked_idx < (int8_t)fb->item_count) {
                        fb->selected_idx = clicked_idx;
                        if (fb->on_select) {
                            fb->on_select(fb, clicked_idx, &fb->items[clicked_idx], fb->user_data);
                        }
                        need_redraw = true;
                    }
                }
            }

            fb->is_dragged   = false;
            fb->is_scrolling = false;
        }
    }

    return need_redraw;
}

/* ================= 切片安全光栅化渲染 ================= */

void ui_file_browser_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_file_browser_t *fb) {
    if (!tile || !fb) return; /*[cite: 18] */

    ui_rect_t overlap;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &fb->rect, &overlap)) return; /*[cite: 18] */

    /* 1. 视口底板与外框 */
    ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &overlap, fb->color_bg); /*[cite: 18] */
    ui_draw_rect(tile, &fb->rect, fb->color_border); /*[cite: 18] */

    /* 2. 开启视口裁剪保护 */
    ui_draw_set_clip(&fb->rect); /*[cite: 18] */

    int16_t base_y = fb->rect.y - fb->scroll_y; /*[cite: 18] */

    /* 3. 遍历渲染文件条目 */
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < fb->item_count; i++) {
        int16_t item_y = base_y + (int16_t)i * fb->item_h; /*[cite: 18] */
        ui_rect_t item_rect = ui_rect_make(fb->rect.x + 2, item_y + 1, fb->rect.w - 4, fb->item_h - 2);

        /* 切片相交裁剪 */
        ui_rect_t item_overlap;
        if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &item_rect, &item_overlap)) continue; /*[cite: 18] */

        bool is_selected = ((int8_t)i == fb->selected_idx);
        uint16_t row_bg = is_selected ? fb->color_active_bg : fb->color_item_bg; /*[cite: 18] */

        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &item_overlap, row_bg); /*[cite: 18] */
        ui_draw_rect(tile, &item_rect, is_selected ? 0x07FF : 0x2124); /*[cite: 18] */

        /* 文字视口边界守卫 */
        int16_t text_y = item_rect.y + 7;
        if ((text_y + 16) > fb->rect.y && text_y < (fb->rect.y + fb->rect.h)) {
            const ui_file_item_t *item = &fb->items[i]; /*[cite: 18] */

            /* 绘制文件类型图标标签 (占宽 36px) */
            const char *tag_str = "BIN";
            uint16_t tag_color  = 0x07E0; /*[cite: 18] */
            if (item->type == UI_FILE_TYPE_DIR) {
                tag_str = "DIR"; tag_color = 0x07FF;
            } else if (item->type == UI_FILE_TYPE_HEX) {
                tag_str = "HEX"; tag_color = 0xFFE0;
            } else if (item->type == UI_FILE_TYPE_UF2) {
                tag_str = "UF2"; tag_color = 0xF81F;
            } else if (item->type == UI_FILE_TYPE_TXT) {
                tag_str = "TXT"; tag_color = 0xFD20;
            }

            ui_draw_string(tile, item_rect.x + 4, text_y, tag_str, FONT_16, tag_color, row_bg);

            /* 计算文件大小文本与所需宽度 */
            char size_str[16] = "";
            int16_t size_w = 0;
            if (item->type != UI_FILE_TYPE_DIR && fb->rect.w >= 180) {
                if (item->size_bytes < 1024) {
                    snprintf(size_str, sizeof(size_str), "%luB", (unsigned long)item->size_bytes);
                } else if (item->size_bytes < 1024 * 1024) {
                    snprintf(size_str, sizeof(size_str), "%luK", (unsigned long)(item->size_bytes / 1024));
                } else {
                    snprintf(size_str, sizeof(size_str), "%.1fM", (float)item->size_bytes / (1024.0f * 1024.0f));
                }
                size_w = (int16_t)strlen(size_str) * 6 + 6;
            }

            /* ⚡ 核心优化：根据可用宽度（总宽 - 标签宽 - 大小宽）自动截断文件名，超出追加 '..' */
            int16_t name_start_x = item_rect.x + 32;
            int16_t max_name_w = item_rect.w - 36 - size_w;
            if (max_name_w < 20) max_name_w = 20;

            char truncated_name[UI_FB_NAME_LEN];
            truncate_filename_by_width(item->name, truncated_name, sizeof(truncated_name), max_name_w);

            /* 绘制截断后的安全文件名 (绝不产生任何重叠或 Flash 算力浪费) */
            ui_draw_string(tile, name_start_x, text_y, truncated_name, FONT_16, is_selected ? 0xFFFF : 0xC618, row_bg);

            /* 绘制右对齐文件大小 */
            if (size_w > 0) {
                ui_draw_string(tile, item_rect.x + item_rect.w - size_w, text_y, size_str, FONT_16, 0x8410, row_bg);
            }
        }
    }

    /* 4. 绘制滚动指示滑块 */
    int16_t total_h = (int16_t)fb->item_count * fb->item_h; /*[cite: 18] */
    if (total_h > fb->rect.h) { /*[cite: 18] */
        int16_t bar_w = 2; /*[cite: 18] */
        int16_t track_h = fb->rect.h - 4; /*[cite: 18] */
        int16_t thumb_h = (int16_t)(((int32_t)fb->rect.h * track_h) / total_h); /*[cite: 18] */
        if (thumb_h < 12) thumb_h = 12; /*[cite: 18] */

        int16_t max_scroll = total_h - fb->rect.h; /*[cite: 18] */
        int16_t thumb_y = fb->rect.y + 2; /*[cite: 18] */
        if (max_scroll > 0) { /*[cite: 18] */
            thumb_y += (int16_t)(((int32_t)fb->scroll_y * (track_h - thumb_h)) / max_scroll); /*[cite: 18] */
        }
        int16_t thumb_x = fb->rect.x + fb->rect.w - bar_w - 1;

        ui_rect_t thumb_rect = ui_rect_make(thumb_x, thumb_y, bar_w, thumb_h); /*[cite: 18] */
        ui_rect_t thumb_overlap;
        if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &thumb_rect, &thumb_overlap)) { /*[cite: 18] */
            ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &thumb_overlap, fb->color_scrollbar); /*[cite: 18] */
        }
    }

    ui_draw_reset_clip(); /*[cite: 18] */
}
ui_file_browser.h.h 78 行 · 3068 B
点击展开
#ifndef UI_FILE_BROWSER_H
#define UI_FILE_BROWSER_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>

#include "ui_geometry.h"
#include "ui_tile.h"
#include "chinese.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

#define UI_FB_MAX_ITEMS     32
#define UI_FB_NAME_LEN      32

typedef enum {
    UI_FILE_TYPE_UNKNOWN = 0,
    UI_FILE_TYPE_DIR,       /* 目录文件夹 [DIR] */
    UI_FILE_TYPE_BIN,       /* .bin 固件 [BIN] */
    UI_FILE_TYPE_HEX,       /* .hex 固件 [HEX] */
    UI_FILE_TYPE_UF2,       /* .uf2 固件 [UF2] */
    UI_FILE_TYPE_TXT        /* 文本/日志 [TXT] */
} ui_file_type_t;

typedef struct {
    char            name[UI_FB_NAME_LEN];
    uint32_t        size_bytes;
    ui_file_type_t  type;
} ui_file_item_t;

typedef struct ui_file_browser ui_file_browser_t;
typedef void (*ui_file_select_cb_t)(ui_file_browser_t *fb, int8_t index, const ui_file_item_t *item, void *user_data);

struct ui_file_browser {
    ui_rect_t           rect;           /* 控件物理包围盒 (支持任意半宽/全宽)[cite: 19] */
    ui_file_item_t      items[UI_FB_MAX_ITEMS]; /*[cite: 19] */
    uint8_t             item_count; /*[cite: 19] */
    int8_t              selected_idx;   /* 选中条目索引 (-1 为未选中)[cite: 19] */

    int16_t             item_h;         /* 单行高度 (默认 30px) */
    int16_t             scroll_y;       /* 当前滚动偏移[cite: 19] */
    bool                is_scrolling;   /* 是否处于拖拽滑动状态[cite: 19] */

    /* ⚡ 触控手势防抖与防误触状态机 */
    int16_t             down_x;
    int16_t             down_y;
    int16_t             scroll_start_y;
    bool                is_touch_down;
    bool                is_dragged;     /* 是否已识别为滑动手势 (位移 > 8px) */

    uint16_t            color_bg; /*[cite: 19] */
    uint16_t            color_item_bg; /*[cite: 19] */
    uint16_t            color_active_bg; /*[cite: 19] */
    uint16_t            color_border; /*[cite: 19] */
    uint16_t            color_scrollbar; /*[cite: 19] */

    ui_file_select_cb_t on_select; /*[cite: 19] */
    void               *user_data; /*[cite: 19] */
};

void ui_file_browser_init(ui_file_browser_t *fb, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h, ui_file_select_cb_t on_select); /*[cite: 19] */
void ui_file_browser_clear(ui_file_browser_t *fb); /*[cite: 19] */
bool ui_file_browser_add_item(ui_file_browser_t *fb, const char *name, uint32_t size_bytes, bool is_dir); /*[cite: 19] */
const ui_file_item_t *ui_file_browser_get_selected(const ui_file_browser_t *fb); /*[cite: 19] */

bool ui_file_browser_process_touch(ui_file_browser_t *fb, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick); /*[cite: 19] */

void ui_file_browser_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_file_browser_t *fb); /*[cite: 19] */

ui_geometry.c.c 68 行 · 2089 B

/*
 * @Author: 轩
 * @Date: 2026-08-19 16:39:51
 * @LastEditTime: 2026-08-21 10:56:54
 * @FilePath: \pico\ui\ui_geometry.c
 */
#include "ui_geometry.h"

#define UI_MIN(a, b) ((a) < (b) ? (a) : (b))
#define UI_MAX(a, b) ((a) > (b) ? (a) : (b))

bool ui_rect_intersect(const ui_rect_t *r1, const ui_rect_t *r2, ui_rect_t *out_intersect)
{
    if (!out_intersect) return false;

    if (!r1 || !r2 || r1->w <= 0 || r1->h <= 0 || r2->w <= 0 || r2->h <= 0) {
        *out_intersect = ui_rect_make(0, 0, 0, 0);
        return false;
    }

    // 1. 计算重叠边界四边
    int16_t left   = UI_MAX(r1->x, r2->x);
    int16_t top    = UI_MAX(r1->y, r2->y);
    int16_t right  = UI_MIN((int16_t)(r1->x + r1->w), (int16_t)(r2->x + r2->w));
    int16_t bottom = UI_MIN((int16_t)(r1->y + r1->h), (int16_t)(r2->y + r2->h));

    // 2. 判断是否存在有效重叠面积
    if (right > left && bottom > top) {
        out_intersect->x = left;
        out_intersect->y = top;
        out_intersect->w = (int16_t)(right - left);
        out_intersect->h = (int16_t)(bottom - top);
        return true;
    }

    *out_intersect = ui_rect_make(0, 0, 0, 0);
    return false;
}

void ui_rect_union(const ui_rect_t *r1, const ui_rect_t *r2, ui_rect_t *out_union)
{
    if (!out_union) return;

    bool r1_empty = (!r1 || r1->w <= 0 || r1->h <= 0);
    bool r2_empty = (!r2 || r2->w <= 0 || r2->h <= 0);

    if (r1_empty && r2_empty) {
        *out_union = ui_rect_make(0, 0, 0, 0);
        return;
    }
    if (r1_empty) {
        *out_union = *r2;
        return;
    }
    if (r2_empty) {
        *out_union = *r1;
        return;
    }

    int16_t left   = UI_MIN(r1->x, r2->x);
    int16_t top    = UI_MIN(r1->y, r2->y);
    int16_t right  = UI_MAX((int16_t)(r1->x + r1->w), (int16_t)(r2->x + r2->w));
    int16_t bottom = UI_MAX((int16_t)(r1->y + r1->h), (int16_t)(r2->y + r2->h));

    out_union->x = left;
    out_union->y = top;
    out_union->w = (int16_t)(right - left);
    out_union->h = (int16_t)(bottom - top);
}
ui_geometry.h.h 100 行 · 2903 B

#ifndef UI_GEOMETRY_H
#define UI_GEOMETRY_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

/* 基础矩形定义 (以左上角坐标 + 宽高表示) */
typedef struct {
    int16_t x;
    int16_t y;
    int16_t w;
    int16_t h;
} ui_rect_t;

/* 基础二维点坐标 */
typedef struct {
    int16_t x;
    int16_t y;
} ui_point_t;

/* ================= 核心内联几何算法 ================= */

/**
 * @brief 构造并初始化矩形
 */
static inline ui_rect_t ui_rect_make(int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h) {
    ui_rect_t r = {x, y, w, h};
    return r;
}

/**
 * @brief 判定矩形是否为空或非法
 */
static inline bool ui_rect_is_empty(const ui_rect_t *r) {
    return (!r || r->w <= 0 || r->h <= 0);
}

/**
 * @brief 判断点是否落在矩形内 (含边界判定)
 */
static inline bool ui_rect_contains_point(const ui_rect_t *r, int16_t x, int16_t y) {
    if (!r || r->w <= 0 || r->h <= 0) return false;
    return (x >= r->x && x < (r->x + r->w) && y >= r->y && y < (r->y + r->h));
}

/**
 * @brief 判定两个矩形是否完全相等
 */
static inline bool ui_rect_equal(const ui_rect_t *r1, const ui_rect_t *r2) {
    if (!r1 || !r2) return false;
    return (r1->x == r2->x && r1->y == r2->y && r1->w == r2->w && r1->h == r2->h);
}

/**
 * @brief 矩形平移变换
 */
static inline ui_rect_t ui_rect_offset(const ui_rect_t *r, int16_t dx, int16_t dy) {
    if (!r) return ui_rect_make(0, 0, 0, 0);
    return ui_rect_make((int16_t)(r->x + dx), (int16_t)(r->y + dy), r->w, r->h);
}

/**
 * @brief 矩形向内收缩 / 向外扩展 (dx/dy 为正则内缩，为负则外扩)
 */
static inline ui_rect_t ui_rect_inset(const ui_rect_t *r, int16_t dx, int16_t dy) {
    if (!r) return ui_rect_make(0, 0, 0, 0);
    int16_t nw = (int16_t)(r->w - 2 * dx);
    int16_t nh = (int16_t)(r->h - 2 * dy);
    if (nw < 0) nw = 0;
    if (nh < 0) nh = 0;
    return ui_rect_make((int16_t)(r->x + dx), (int16_t)(r->y + dy), nw, nh);
}

/* ================= 几何重叠与包围盒运算 ================= */

/**
 * @brief 计算两个矩形的交集 (用于切片与控件区域的相交裁剪)
 * @param r1 输入矩形 1
 * @param r2 输入矩形 2
 * @param out_intersect 输出相交区域 (无交集返回 false，输出为 0 矩形)
 * @return true 存在有效交集，false 完全不相交
 */
bool ui_rect_intersect(const ui_rect_t *r1, const ui_rect_t *r2, ui_rect_t *out_intersect);

/**
 * @brief 计算包含两个矩形的最小外接矩形 (用于脏矩形 Dirty-Rect 合并)
 * @param r1 输入矩形 1
 * @param r2 输入矩形 2
 * @param out_union 输出合并后的外接矩形
 */
void ui_rect_union(const ui_rect_t *r1, const ui_rect_t *r2, ui_rect_t *out_union);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

#endif /* UI_GEOMETRY_H */
ui_hex_view.c.c 256 行 · 10347 B
点击展开
#include "ui_hex_view.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

#define TOUCH_SLOP_PX   8 // 8 像素滑动防抖死区

// 紧凑排版常数定义
#define ADDR_COL_W      76  // 地址列宽度 "08000000:" (72px + 4px)
#define HEX_BYTE_W      22  // 每个 Hex 单元宽度 (2 字符 16px + 6px 间距)
#define HEX_COL_W       (UI_HEX_BYTES_PER_ROW * HEX_BYTE_W) // 8 * 22 = 176px
#define ASCII_COL_W     (UI_HEX_BYTES_PER_ROW * 8)          // 8 * 8 = 64px

void ui_hex_view_init(ui_hex_view_t *hv, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h,
                      ui_hex_select_cb_t on_select) {
    if (!hv) return;

    hv->rect              = ui_rect_make(x, y, w, h);
    hv->data              = NULL;
    hv->data_len          = 0;
    hv->base_addr         = 0;

    hv->row_h             = 20;
    hv->scroll_y          = 0;
    hv->selected_byte_idx = -1;

    hv->down_x            = 0;
    hv->down_y            = 0;
    hv->scroll_start_y    = 0;
    hv->is_touch_down     = false;
    hv->is_dragged        = false;
    hv->is_scrolling      = false;

    hv->color_bg            = 0x0842; // 极深蓝黑底
    hv->color_addr          = 0xFD20; // 橙黄基地址
    hv->color_hex           = 0xFFFF; // 纯白 Hex 数据
    hv->color_ascii         = 0x07FF; // 青蓝 ASCII 字符
    hv->color_selected_bg   = 0x07E0; // 亮绿选中底色
    hv->color_selected_text = 0x0000; // 纯黑选中字
    hv->color_border        = 0x39E7; // 深灰外框
    hv->color_scrollbar     = 0x07FF; // 青蓝滚动指示条

    hv->on_select         = on_select;
    hv->user_data         = NULL;
}

void ui_hex_view_bind(ui_hex_view_t *hv, const uint8_t *data, uint32_t data_len, uint32_t base_addr) {
    if (!hv) return;
    hv->data              = data;
    hv->data_len          = data_len;
    hv->base_addr         = base_addr;
    hv->scroll_y          = 0;
    hv->selected_byte_idx = -1;
}

void ui_hex_view_jump_to_addr(ui_hex_view_t *hv, uint32_t target_addr) {
    if (!hv || !hv->data || hv->data_len == 0) return;

    uint32_t offset = (target_addr >= hv->base_addr) ? (target_addr - hv->base_addr) : 0;
    if (offset >= hv->data_len) offset = hv->data_len - 1;

    uint32_t target_row = offset / UI_HEX_BYTES_PER_ROW;
    int16_t new_scroll = (int16_t)(target_row * hv->row_h);

    uint32_t total_rows = (hv->data_len + UI_HEX_BYTES_PER_ROW - 1) / UI_HEX_BYTES_PER_ROW;
    int16_t max_scroll = (int16_t)(total_rows * hv->row_h) - hv->rect.h;
    if (max_scroll < 0) max_scroll = 0;

    if (new_scroll > max_scroll) new_scroll = max_scroll;
    hv->scroll_y = new_scroll;
}

/* ================= 触控状态机 (8px 防抖 + 精准点选) ================= */

bool ui_hex_view_process_touch(ui_hex_view_t *hv, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    (void)now_tick;
    if (!hv || !hv->data || hv->data_len == 0) return false;

    uint32_t total_rows = (hv->data_len + UI_HEX_BYTES_PER_ROW - 1) / UI_HEX_BYTES_PER_ROW;
    int16_t total_h = (int16_t)(total_rows * hv->row_h);
    int16_t max_scroll = total_h - hv->rect.h;
    if (max_scroll < 0) max_scroll = 0;

    bool need_redraw = false;

    if (is_touching) {
        if (!hv->is_touch_down) {
            if (ui_rect_contains_point(&hv->rect, tx, ty)) {
                hv->is_touch_down  = true;
                hv->is_dragged     = false;
                hv->is_scrolling   = false;
                hv->down_x         = tx;
                hv->down_y         = ty;
                hv->scroll_start_y = hv->scroll_y;
            }
        } else {
            int16_t dy = ty - hv->down_y;
            int16_t dx = tx - hv->down_x;

            if (!hv->is_dragged && (abs(dy) >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX || abs(dx) >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX)) {
                hv->is_dragged   = true;
                hv->is_scrolling = true;
            }

            if (hv->is_dragged && max_scroll > 0) {
                int16_t new_scroll = hv->scroll_start_y - dy;
                if (new_scroll < 0) new_scroll = 0;
                if (new_scroll > max_scroll) new_scroll = max_scroll;

                if (hv->scroll_y != new_scroll) {
                    hv->scroll_y = new_scroll;
                    need_redraw  = true;
                }
            }
        }
        return need_redraw;
    } else {
        if (hv->is_touch_down) {
            hv->is_touch_down = false;

            if (!hv->is_dragged && ui_rect_contains_point(&hv->rect, tx, ty)) {
                int16_t rel_y = ty - (hv->rect.y - hv->scroll_y);
                uint32_t row = (uint32_t)(rel_y / hv->row_h);

                int16_t hex_start_x   = hv->rect.x + ADDR_COL_W + 4;
                int16_t ascii_start_x = hex_start_x + HEX_COL_W + 8;

                int8_t col = -1;
                if (tx >= hex_start_x && tx < (hex_start_x + HEX_COL_W)) {
                    col = (int8_t)((tx - hex_start_x) / HEX_BYTE_W);
                } else if (tx >= ascii_start_x && tx < (ascii_start_x + ASCII_COL_W)) {
                    col = (int8_t)((tx - ascii_start_x) / 8);
                }

                if (col >= 0 && col < UI_HEX_BYTES_PER_ROW) {
                    uint32_t byte_idx = row * UI_HEX_BYTES_PER_ROW + col;
                    if (byte_idx < hv->data_len) {
                        hv->selected_byte_idx = (int32_t)byte_idx;
                        if (hv->on_select) {
                            hv->on_select(hv, byte_idx, hv->data[byte_idx], hv->user_data);
                        }
                        need_redraw = true;
                    }
                }
            }

            hv->is_dragged   = false;
            hv->is_scrolling = false;
        }
    }

    return need_redraw;
}

/* ================= 切片光栅化渲染 (零截断精确排版) ================= */

void ui_hex_view_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_hex_view_t *hv) {
    if (!tile || !hv || !hv->data || hv->data_len == 0) return;

    ui_rect_t overlap;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &hv->rect, &overlap)) {
        return;
    }

    // 1. 擦除背景与绘制边框
    ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &overlap, hv->color_bg);
    ui_draw_rect(tile, &hv->rect, hv->color_border);

    // 2. 启用像素级局部视口裁剪保护
    ui_draw_set_clip(&hv->rect);

    int16_t base_y = hv->rect.y + 4 - hv->scroll_y;
    uint32_t total_rows = (hv->data_len + UI_HEX_BYTES_PER_ROW - 1) / UI_HEX_BYTES_PER_ROW;

    int16_t first_row = (tile->rect.y - base_y) / hv->row_h;
    int16_t last_row  = (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h - 1 - base_y) / hv->row_h;

    if (first_row < 0) first_row = 0;
    if (last_row >= (int16_t)total_rows) last_row = (int16_t)total_rows - 1;

    int16_t hex_start_x   = hv->rect.x + ADDR_COL_W + 4;
    int16_t ascii_start_x = hex_start_x + HEX_COL_W + 8;

    for (int16_t r = first_row; r <= last_row; r++) {
        int16_t row_y = base_y + r * hv->row_h;
        uint32_t row_byte_start = (uint32_t)r * UI_HEX_BYTES_PER_ROW;

        if ((row_y + 16) > tile->rect.y && row_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
            // A. 绘制地址列 "08000000:" (宽 72px)
            char addr_str[16];
            snprintf(addr_str, sizeof(addr_str), "%08lX:", (unsigned long)(hv->base_addr + row_byte_start));
            ui_draw_string(tile, hv->rect.x + 4, row_y, addr_str, FONT_16, hv->color_addr, hv->color_bg);

            // B. 绘制 8 个 Hex 字节与 ASCII 对照
            for (uint8_t c = 0; c < UI_HEX_BYTES_PER_ROW; c++) {
                uint32_t byte_idx = row_byte_start + c;
                if (byte_idx >= hv->data_len) break;

                uint8_t b_val = hv->data[byte_idx];
                bool is_selected = (hv->selected_byte_idx == (int32_t)byte_idx);

                int16_t bx = hex_start_x + c * HEX_BYTE_W;
                int16_t ax = ascii_start_x + c * 8;

                // 1) 绘制 Hex 字符 (如 "5A")
                char hex_str[4];
                snprintf(hex_str, sizeof(hex_str), "%02X", b_val);

                if (is_selected) {
                    ui_rect_t sel_box = ui_rect_make((int16_t)(bx - 1), (int16_t)(row_y - 1), 18, 18);
                    ui_rect_t sel_ov;
                    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &sel_box, &sel_ov)) {
                        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &sel_ov, hv->color_selected_bg);
                    }
                    ui_draw_string(tile, bx, row_y, hex_str, FONT_16, hv->color_selected_text, hv->color_selected_bg);
                } else {
                    ui_draw_string(tile, bx, row_y, hex_str, FONT_16, hv->color_hex, hv->color_bg);
                }

                // 2) 绘制 ASCII 对照字符 (8 字节连续无缝排列)
                char ch = (b_val >= 32 && b_val <= 126) ? (char)b_val : '.';
                char ch_str[2] = {ch, '\0'};

                if (is_selected) {
                    ui_draw_string(tile, ax, row_y, ch_str, FONT_16, hv->color_selected_bg, hv->color_bg);
                } else {
                    ui_draw_string(tile, ax, row_y, ch_str, FONT_16, hv->color_ascii, hv->color_bg);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // 4. 超出视口时绘制右侧细滚动条
    int16_t total_h = (int16_t)(total_rows * hv->row_h);
    if (total_h > (hv->rect.h - 8)) {
        int16_t bar_w = 2;
        int16_t track_h = hv->rect.h - 8;
        int16_t thumb_h = (int16_t)(((int32_t)(hv->rect.h - 8) * track_h) / total_h);
        if (thumb_h < 12) thumb_h = 12;

        int16_t max_scroll = total_h - hv->rect.h;
        int16_t thumb_y = hv->rect.y + 4;
        if (max_scroll > 0) {
            thumb_y += (int16_t)(((int32_t)hv->scroll_y * (track_h - thumb_h)) / max_scroll);
        }
        int16_t thumb_x = hv->rect.x + hv->rect.w - bar_w - 2;

        ui_rect_t thumb_rect = ui_rect_make(thumb_x, thumb_y, bar_w, thumb_h);
        ui_rect_t thumb_overlap;
        if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &thumb_rect, &thumb_overlap)) {
            ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &thumb_overlap, hv->color_scrollbar);
        }
    }

    ui_draw_reset_clip();
}
