/*
 * @Author: 轩
 * @Date: 2026-08-21 14:24:25
 * @LastEditTime: 2026-08-21 14:24:30
 * @FilePath: \pico\ui\ui_hex_view.h
 */
#ifndef UI_HEX_VIEW_H
#define UI_HEX_VIEW_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include "ui_geometry.h"
#include "ui_tile.h"
#include "chinese.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

#define UI_HEX_BYTES_PER_ROW 8 // 480px 屏宽配合 FONT_16 最佳每行字节数 (地址 + 8字节Hex + ASCII)

typedef struct ui_hex_view ui_hex_view_t;

// 字节单元点选交互回调
typedef void (*ui_hex_select_cb_t)(ui_hex_view_t *hv, uint32_t byte_offset, uint8_t val, void *user_data);

struct ui_hex_view {
    ui_rect_t           rect;                                       // 控件物理包围盒
    const uint8_t      *data;                                       // 数据源内存指针 (RAM / Flash / Buffer)
    uint32_t            data_len;                                   // 数据总字节数
    uint32_t            base_addr;                                  // 基准物理地址偏移 (如 0x08000000, 0x10000000)

    int16_t             row_h;                                      // 单行高度 (默认 20px)
    int16_t             scroll_y;                                   // 当前 Y 轴滚动偏移
    int32_t             selected_byte_idx;                          // 当前选中的字节索引 (-1 为未选中)

    // ⚡ 触控手势防抖与滑动追踪状态机
    int16_t             down_x;
    int16_t             down_y;
    int16_t             scroll_start_y;
    bool                is_touch_down;
    bool                is_dragged;                                 // 8px 防抖死区锁定
    bool                is_scrolling;                               // 是否处于拖拽滚动中

    // 配色方案 (RGB565)
    uint16_t            color_bg;                                   // 视口底色 (极深黑蓝)
    uint16_t            color_addr;                                 // 地址列文本色 (暗金黄/橙黄)
    uint16_t            color_hex;                                  // Hex 16进制数据色 (纯白)
    uint16_t            color_ascii;                                // ASCII 对照字符色 (青蓝)
    uint16_t            color_selected_bg;                          // 选中字节高亮底色 (亮绿)
    uint16_t            color_selected_text;                        // 选中字节文本色 (纯黑)
    uint16_t            color_border;                               // 分割线颜色
    uint16_t            color_scrollbar;                            // 滚动指示条颜色

    ui_hex_select_cb_t  on_select;
    void               *user_data;
};

// 初始化 16 进制内存检视器
void ui_hex_view_init(ui_hex_view_t *hv, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h,
                      ui_hex_select_cb_t on_select);

// 绑定数据源 (内存指针、总字节数、物理基地址)
void ui_hex_view_bind(ui_hex_view_t *hv, const uint8_t *data, uint32_t data_len, uint32_t base_addr);

// 快速跳转至指定绝对地址
void ui_hex_view_jump_to_addr(ui_hex_view_t *hv, uint32_t target_addr);

// 触控交互驱动 (支持拖拽上下浏览 + 8px 死区保护 + Touch-Up 精准点选字节)
bool ui_hex_view_process_touch(ui_hex_view_t *hv, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick);

// 切片光栅化渲染 (O(1) 行级算术剪枝 + 视口硬裁剪保护)
void ui_hex_view_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_hex_view_t *hv);

// 获取控件局部脏矩形包围盒
static inline ui_rect_t ui_hex_view_get_dirty_rect(const ui_hex_view_t *hv) {
    return hv ? hv->rect : ui_rect_make(0, 0, 0, 0);
}

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

#endif // UI_HEX_VIEW_H
ui_input.c.c 117 行 · 4252 B
点击展开
#include "ui_input.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

#define TOUCH_SLOP_PX   8 // 8 像素滑动防抖死区

void ui_input_init(ui_input_t *input, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h, 
                   const char *placeholder, ui_input_cb_t on_focus) {
    if (!input) return;

    input->rect = ui_rect_make(x, y, w, h);
    input->text[0] = '\0';
    input->placeholder = placeholder ? placeholder : "";
    input->cursor_pos = 0;
    input->is_focused = false;

    input->color_bg           = 0x1082; // 极深蓝黑底
    input->color_border       = 0x39E7; // 默认深灰边框
    input->color_focus_border = 0x07FF; // 聚焦时青蓝高亮光圈
    input->color_text         = 0xFFFF; // 纯白字
    input->color_placeholder  = 0x8410; // 暗灰提示字

    input->down_x        = 0;
    input->down_y        = 0;
    input->is_touch_down = false;
    input->is_dragged    = false;

    input->on_focus   = on_focus;
    input->user_data  = NULL;
}

void ui_input_set_text(ui_input_t *input, const char *str) {
    if (!input) return;
    if (str) {
        strncpy(input->text, str, UI_INPUT_MAX_LEN - 1);
        input->text[UI_INPUT_MAX_LEN - 1] = '\0';
        input->cursor_pos = (uint8_t)strlen(input->text);
    } else {
        input->text[0] = '\0';
        input->cursor_pos = 0;
    }
}

void ui_input_clear(ui_input_t *input) {
    if (!input) return;
    input->text[0] = '\0';
    input->cursor_pos = 0;
}

bool ui_input_process_touch(ui_input_t *input, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    (void)now_tick;
    if (!input) return false;

    if (is_touching) {
        if (!input->is_touch_down) {
            // A. 初次按下 (Touch Down): 仅在命中本输入框时记录起点
            if (ui_rect_contains_point(&input->rect, tx, ty)) {
                input->is_touch_down = true;
                input->is_dragged    = false;
                input->down_x        = tx;
                input->down_y        = ty;
            }
        } else {
            // B. 持续移动 (Touch Move): 8px 防抖死区判定
            int16_t dx = abs(tx - input->down_x);
            int16_t dy = abs(ty - input->down_y);
            if (!input->is_dragged && (dx >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX || dy >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX)) {
                input->is_dragged = true; // 锁定为滑动，放弃聚焦
            }
        }
        return false;
    } else {
        // C. ⚡ 手指松开 (Touch Up): 唯一允许触发聚焦与呼出键盘的时机
        if (input->is_touch_down) {
            input->is_touch_down = false;

            // 只有未发生滑动的纯点按，且松开点仍在输入框内才触发聚焦
            if (!input->is_dragged && ui_rect_contains_point(&input->rect, tx, ty)) {
                input->is_focused = true;
                if (input->on_focus) {
                    input->on_focus(input, input->user_data);
                }
                return true;
            }
            input->is_dragged = false;
        }
    }

    return false;
}

void ui_input_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_input_t *input) {
    if (!tile || !input) return;

    ui_rect_t overlap;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &input->rect, &overlap)) return;

    // 1. 绘制底色与边框 (相交裁剪安全保护)
    ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &overlap, input->color_bg);
    ui_draw_rect(tile, &input->rect, input->is_focused ? input->color_focus_border : input->color_border);

    // 2. 局部视口裁剪
    ui_draw_set_clip(&input->rect);

    int16_t text_x = input->rect.x + 8;
    int16_t text_y = input->rect.y + (input->rect.h - 16) / 2;

    if ((text_y + 16) > tile->rect.y && text_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
        if (input->text[0] != '\0') {
            ui_draw_string(tile, text_x, text_y, input->text, FONT_16, input->color_text, input->color_bg);
        } else if (input->placeholder && input->placeholder[0] != '\0') {
            ui_draw_string(tile, text_x, text_y, input->placeholder, FONT_16, input->color_placeholder, input->color_bg);
        }
    }

    ui_draw_reset_clip();
}
ui_input.h.h 57 行 · 1952 B

#ifndef UI_INPUT_H
#define UI_INPUT_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include "ui_geometry.h"
#include "ui_tile.h"
#include "chinese.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

#define UI_INPUT_MAX_LEN 64

typedef struct ui_input ui_input_t;
typedef void (*ui_input_cb_t)(ui_input_t *input, void *user_data);

struct ui_input {
    ui_rect_t       rect;                       // 控件物理包围盒
    char            text[UI_INPUT_MAX_LEN];     // 文本内容缓冲区
    const char     *placeholder;                // 占位提示符 (如 "点击输入指令...")
    uint8_t         cursor_pos;                 // 光标位置
    bool            is_focused;                 // 焦点/选中状态

    // 配色方案 (RGB565)
    uint16_t        color_bg;
    uint16_t        color_border;
    uint16_t        color_focus_border;
    uint16_t        color_text;
    uint16_t        color_placeholder;

    // ⚡ 触控手势防抖与防误触状态机
    int16_t         down_x;
    int16_t         down_y;
    bool            is_touch_down;
    bool            is_dragged;                 // 是否已识别为滑动手势 (位移 > 8px)

    ui_input_cb_t   on_focus;                   // 点击聚焦回调 (用于呼出键盘)
    void           *user_data;
};

void ui_input_init(ui_input_t *input, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h, 
                   const char *placeholder, ui_input_cb_t on_focus);
void ui_input_set_text(ui_input_t *input, const char *str);
void ui_input_clear(ui_input_t *input);

// 触控驱动状态机 (集成 8px 死区保护与 Touch-Up 松手触发)
bool ui_input_process_touch(ui_input_t *input, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick);

// 切片光栅化渲染 (受全局视口裁剪保护)
void ui_input_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_input_t *input);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

#endif // UI_INPUT_H
ui_keyboard.c.c 299 行 · 10698 B
点击展开
#include "ui_keyboard.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <string.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

#define TOUCH_SLOP_PX   8 // 8 像素滑动防抖死区

// 4 行按键定义表
static const char *k_keys_lower[4][10] = {
    {"q", "w", "e", "r", "t", "y", "u", "i", "o", "p"},
    {"a", "s", "d", "f", "g", "h", "j", "k", "l", "-"},
    {"CAPS", "z", "x", "c", "v", "b", "n", "m", "DEL", NULL},
    {"123", "SPACE", "ENTER", NULL}
};

static const char *k_keys_upper[4][10] = {
    {"Q", "W", "E", "R", "T", "Y", "U", "I", "O", "P"},
    {"A", "S", "D", "F", "G", "H", "J", "K", "L", "_"},
    {"caps", "Z", "X", "C", "V", "B", "N", "M", "DEL", NULL},
    {"123", "SPACE", "ENTER", NULL}
};

static const char *k_keys_num_sym[4][10] = {
    {"1", "2", "3", "4", "5", "6", "7", "8", "9", "0"},
    {"+", "-", "*", "/", "=", ":", ";", "(", ")", "#"},
    {"abc", "@", ".", ",", "\"", "'", "?", "!", "DEL", NULL},
    {"ABC", "SPACE", "ENTER", NULL}
};

void ui_keyboard_init(ui_keyboard_t *kb, ui_kb_submit_cb_t on_submit) {
    if (!kb) return;
    kb->rect = ui_rect_make(0, 120, 480, 200);
    kb->is_open = false;
    kb->mode = UI_KB_MODE_UPPER;
    kb->buffer[0] = '\0';
    kb->cursor_pos = 0;
    kb->target_input = NULL;

    kb->down_x        = 0;
    kb->down_y        = 0;
    kb->is_touch_down = false;
    kb->is_dragged    = false;

    kb->on_submit = on_submit;
    kb->user_data = NULL;
}

void ui_keyboard_open(ui_keyboard_t *kb, ui_input_t *target_input) {
    if (!kb) return;
    kb->is_open = true;
    kb->target_input = target_input;
    kb->is_touch_down = false;
    kb->is_dragged    = false;

    if (target_input) {
        strncpy(kb->buffer, target_input->text, UI_INPUT_MAX_LEN - 1);
        kb->buffer[UI_INPUT_MAX_LEN - 1] = '\0';
        kb->cursor_pos = (uint8_t)strlen(kb->buffer);
    } else {
        kb->buffer[0] = '\0';
        kb->cursor_pos = 0;
    }
}

void ui_keyboard_close(ui_keyboard_t *kb) {
    if (!kb) return;
    kb->is_open = false;
    kb->is_touch_down = false;
    kb->is_dragged    = false;
    if (kb->target_input) {
        kb->target_input->is_focused = false;
    }
}

static void get_key_rect(uint8_t row, uint8_t col, int16_t kb_y, ui_rect_t *out_rect) {
    int16_t key_h = 36;
    int16_t gap = 4;
    int16_t py = kb_y + 36 + row * (key_h + gap);

    if (row == 0 || row == 1) {
        int16_t key_w = 44;
        out_rect->x = 4 + col * (key_w + gap);
        out_rect->y = py;
        out_rect->w = key_w;
        out_rect->h = key_h;
    } else if (row == 2) {
        if (col == 0) {
            *out_rect = ui_rect_make(4, py, 60, key_h);
        } else if (col == 8) {
            *out_rect = ui_rect_make(416, py, 60, key_h);
        } else {
            *out_rect = ui_rect_make(68 + (col - 1) * 49, py, 45, key_h);
        }
    } else if (row == 3) {
        if (col == 0) {
            *out_rect = ui_rect_make(4, py, 80, key_h);
        } else if (col == 1) {
            *out_rect = ui_rect_make(88, py, 258, key_h);
        } else if (col == 2) {
            *out_rect = ui_rect_make(350, py, 126, key_h);
        }
    }
}

static void handle_key_action(ui_keyboard_t *kb, const char *key_str) {
    if (!key_str) return;

    if (strcmp(key_str, "CAPS") == 0) {
        kb->mode = UI_KB_MODE_UPPER;
    } else if (strcmp(key_str, "caps") == 0 || strcmp(key_str, "abc") == 0) {
        kb->mode = UI_KB_MODE_LOWER;
    } else if (strcmp(key_str, "123") == 0) {
        kb->mode = UI_KB_MODE_NUM_SYM;
    } else if (strcmp(key_str, "ABC") == 0) {
        kb->mode = UI_KB_MODE_UPPER;
    } else if (strcmp(key_str, "DEL") == 0) {
        if (kb->cursor_pos > 0) {
            kb->buffer[--kb->cursor_pos] = '\0';
        }
    } else if (strcmp(key_str, "SPACE") == 0) {
        if (kb->cursor_pos < UI_INPUT_MAX_LEN - 1) {
            kb->buffer[kb->cursor_pos++] = ' ';
            kb->buffer[kb->cursor_pos] = '\0';
        }
    } else if (strcmp(key_str, "ENTER") == 0) {
        if (kb->target_input) {
            ui_input_set_text(kb->target_input, kb->buffer);
        }
        if (kb->on_submit) {
            kb->on_submit(kb, kb->buffer, kb->user_data);
        }
        ui_keyboard_close(kb);
        return;
    } else {
        if (kb->cursor_pos < UI_INPUT_MAX_LEN - 1) {
            kb->buffer[kb->cursor_pos++] = key_str[0];
            kb->buffer[kb->cursor_pos] = '\0';
        }
    }

    if (kb->target_input) {
        ui_input_set_text(kb->target_input, kb->buffer);
    }
}

bool ui_keyboard_process_touch(ui_keyboard_t *kb, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    (void)now_tick;
    if (!kb || !kb->is_open) return false;

    if (is_touching) {
        if (!kb->is_touch_down) {
            // A. 初次按下 (Touch Down): 记录坐标起点
            kb->is_touch_down = true;
            kb->is_dragged    = false;
            kb->down_x        = tx;
            kb->down_y        = ty;
        } else {
            // B. 持续移动 (Touch Move): 8px 防抖死区判定
            int16_t dx = abs(tx - kb->down_x);
            int16_t dy = abs(ty - kb->down_y);
            if (!kb->is_dragged && (dx >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX || dy >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX)) {
                kb->is_dragged = true; // 锁定为滑动，放弃按键触发
            }
        }
        return false;
    } else {
        // C. ⚡ 手指松开 (Touch Up): 唯一允许输入字符或关闭键盘的时机
        if (kb->is_touch_down) {
            kb->is_touch_down = false;

            if (!kb->is_dragged) {
                // 1. 点击键盘上方空白区域 -> 收起键盘
                if (!ui_rect_contains_point(&kb->rect, tx, ty)) {
                    ui_keyboard_close(kb);
                    return true;
                }

                // 2. 点击顶部清空按钮 (CLR)
                ui_rect_t clr_rect = ui_rect_make(416, kb->rect.y + 4, 60, 28);
                if (ui_rect_contains_point(&clr_rect, tx, ty)) {
                    kb->buffer[0] = '\0';
                    kb->cursor_pos = 0;
                    if (kb->target_input) {
                        ui_input_clear(kb->target_input);
                    }
                    return true;
                }

                // 3. 遍历键帽判定命中
                const char *(*keys)[10] = (kb->mode == UI_KB_MODE_LOWER) ? k_keys_lower :
                                          ((kb->mode == UI_KB_MODE_UPPER) ? k_keys_upper : k_keys_num_sym);

                for (uint8_t r = 0; r < 4; r++) {
                    for (uint8_t c = 0; c < 10; c++) {
                        const char *k = keys[r][c];
                        if (!k) break;

                        ui_rect_t kr;
                        get_key_rect(r, c, kb->rect.y, &kr);
                        if (ui_rect_contains_point(&kr, tx, ty)) {
                            handle_key_action(kb, k);
                            return true;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            kb->is_dragged = false;
        }
    }

    return false;
}

void ui_keyboard_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_keyboard_t *kb) {
    if (!tile || !kb || !kb->is_open) return;

    ui_rect_t overlap;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &kb->rect, &overlap)) return;

    // 1. 键盘底色与顶部亮蓝分割线
    ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &overlap, 0x10A2);
    ui_draw_fast_hline(tile, kb->rect.x, kb->rect.y, kb->rect.w, 0x07FF);

    // 2. 顶部实时预览框 (Preview Bar)
    ui_rect_t preview_box = ui_rect_make(4, kb->rect.y + 4, 408, 28);
    ui_rect_t box_overlap;
    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &preview_box, &box_overlap)) {
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &box_overlap, 0x0842);
        ui_draw_rect(tile, &preview_box, 0x07FF);

        char show_buf[UI_INPUT_MAX_LEN + 4];
        if (kb->buffer[0] != '\0') {
            snprintf(show_buf, sizeof(show_buf), "%s_", kb->buffer);
        } else {
            snprintf(show_buf, sizeof(show_buf), "_");
        }

        int16_t str_len = (int16_t)strlen(show_buf);
        int16_t max_chars = (preview_box.w - 16) / 8;
        const char *draw_str = show_buf;
        if (str_len > max_chars) {
            draw_str = show_buf + (str_len - max_chars);
        }

        ui_draw_set_clip(&preview_box);
        ui_draw_string(tile, preview_box.x + 8, preview_box.y + 6, draw_str, FONT_16, 0xFFFF, 0x0842);
        ui_draw_reset_clip();
    }

    // 3. 顶部清空按钮 (CLR)
    ui_rect_t clr_rect = ui_rect_make(416, kb->rect.y + 4, 60, 28);
    ui_rect_t clr_overlap;
    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &clr_rect, &clr_overlap)) {
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &clr_overlap, 0x4208);
        ui_draw_rect(tile, &clr_rect, 0x8410);
        ui_draw_string(tile, clr_rect.x + 14, clr_rect.y + 6, "清空", FONT_16, 0xFFE0, 0x4208);
    }

    // 4. 遍历绘制所有按键
    const char *(*keys)[10] = (kb->mode == UI_KB_MODE_LOWER) ? k_keys_lower :
                              ((kb->mode == UI_KB_MODE_UPPER) ? k_keys_upper : k_keys_num_sym);

    for (uint8_t r = 0; r < 4; r++) {
        for (uint8_t c = 0; c < 10; c++) {
            const char *k = keys[r][c];
            if (!k) break;

            ui_rect_t kr;
            get_key_rect(r, c, kb->rect.y, &kr);

            ui_rect_t k_overlap;
            if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &kr, &k_overlap)) continue;

            uint16_t key_bg = 0x2126;
            uint16_t key_text_col = 0xFFFF;

            if (strcmp(k, "ENTER") == 0) {
                key_bg = 0x03E0; // 绿色回车
            } else if (strcmp(k, "DEL") == 0) {
                key_bg = 0x8000; // 暗红退格
            } else if (strcmp(k, "CAPS") == 0 || strcmp(k, "caps") == 0 ||
                       strcmp(k, "123") == 0  || strcmp(k, "ABC") == 0 || strcmp(k, "abc") == 0) {
                key_bg = 0x31A6; // 功能切换键
                key_text_col = 0x07FF;
            }

            ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &k_overlap, key_bg);
            ui_draw_rect(tile, &kr, 0x39E7);

            int16_t str_w = (int16_t)strlen(k) * 8;
            int16_t tx = kr.x + (kr.w - str_w) / 2;
            int16_t ty = kr.y + (kr.h - 16) / 2;

            if ((ty + 16) > tile->rect.y && ty < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
                ui_draw_string(tile, tx, ty, k, FONT_16, key_text_col, key_bg);
            }
        }
    }
}
ui_keyboard.h.h 56 行 · 1912 B

#ifndef UI_KEYBOARD_H
#define UI_KEYBOARD_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include "ui_geometry.h"
#include "ui_tile.h"
#include "ui_input.h"
#include "chinese.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

typedef enum {
    UI_KB_MODE_LOWER = 0,   // 小写字母 (q w e r t y...)
    UI_KB_MODE_UPPER,       // 大写字母 (Q W E R T Y...)
    UI_KB_MODE_NUM_SYM      // 数字与常用符号 (1 2 3 + - * / @ =...)
} ui_kb_mode_t;

typedef struct ui_keyboard ui_keyboard_t;
typedef void (*ui_kb_submit_cb_t)(ui_keyboard_t *kb, const char *text, void *user_data);

struct ui_keyboard {
    ui_rect_t           rect;           // 键盘整体覆盖区域 (X:0, Y:120, W:480, H:200)
    bool                is_open;        // 开启状态
    ui_kb_mode_t        mode;           // 当前按键模式

    char                buffer[UI_INPUT_MAX_LEN]; // 当前编辑中的文本
    uint8_t             cursor_pos;

    ui_input_t         *target_input;   // 关联绑定的目标输入框
    ui_kb_submit_cb_t   on_submit;      // 提交发送回调
    void               *user_data;

    // ⚡ 触控手势防抖与防误触状态机
    int16_t             down_x;
    int16_t             down_y;
    bool                is_touch_down;
    bool                is_dragged;     // 是否已识别为滑动手势 (位移 > 8px)
};

void ui_keyboard_init(ui_keyboard_t *kb, ui_kb_submit_cb_t on_submit);
void ui_keyboard_open(ui_keyboard_t *kb, ui_input_t *target_input);
void ui_keyboard_close(ui_keyboard_t *kb);

// 触控状态机 (集成 8px 死区保护与 Touch-Up 松手触发)
bool ui_keyboard_process_touch(ui_keyboard_t *kb, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick);

// 切片光栅化渲染
void ui_keyboard_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_keyboard_t *kb);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

#endif // UI_KEYBOARD_H
ui_modal.c.c 183 行 · 8125 B

#include "ui_modal.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

static void on_modal_close_btn_click(ui_btn_t *btn, void *user_data) {
    (void)btn;
    ui_modal_t *m = (ui_modal_t *)user_data;
    m->is_open = false;
    if (m->on_action) m->on_action(m, false, m->user_data);
}

static void on_modal_confirm_btn_click(ui_btn_t *btn, void *user_data) {
    (void)btn;
    ui_modal_t *m = (ui_modal_t *)user_data;
    m->is_open = false;
    if (m->on_action) m->on_action(m, true, m->user_data);
}

static void forward_scroll_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_rect_t *viewport, int16_t scroll_y, void *user_data) {
    ui_modal_t *m = (ui_modal_t *)user_data;
    if (m && m->draw_content) {
        m->draw_content(tile, viewport, scroll_y, m->user_data);
    }
}

void ui_modal_init(ui_modal_t *modal, int16_t screen_w, int16_t screen_h,
                   int16_t w, int16_t h, int16_t content_h,
                   const char *title, ui_modal_btn_mode_t btn_mode,
                   ui_modal_draw_content_cb_t draw_content,
                   ui_modal_touch_content_cb_t touch_content,
                   ui_modal_action_cb_t on_action) {
    if (!modal) return;

    modal->rect = ui_rect_make((screen_w - w) / 2, (screen_h - h) / 2, w, h);
    modal->is_open       = false;
    modal->title         = title;
    modal->btn_mode      = btn_mode;
    modal->draw_content  = draw_content;
    modal->touch_content = touch_content;
    modal->on_action     = on_action;
    modal->user_data     = NULL;

    modal->color_bg      = 0x10A2; // 深蓝黑实体底色
    modal->color_border  = 0x07FF; // 青蓝外框
    modal->color_title   = 0xFFE0; // 金黄标题

    int16_t title_bar_h  = 38;
    int16_t bottom_bar_h = (btn_mode != UI_MODAL_BTN_NONE) ? 46 : 6;
    int16_t body_y       = modal->rect.y + title_bar_h;
    int16_t body_h       = modal->rect.h - title_bar_h - bottom_bar_h;

    // 滚动视口初始化
    ui_scrollview_init(&modal->sv_body, modal->rect.x + 4, body_y, w - 8, body_h, content_h, forward_scroll_draw);
    modal->sv_body.user_data       = modal;
    modal->sv_body.color_bg        = modal->color_bg;
    modal->sv_body.color_scrollbar = 0x07FF;

    // 右上角关闭按钮
    ui_btn_init(&modal->btn_close, modal->rect.x + w - 28, modal->rect.y + 6, 22, 22, "X", on_modal_close_btn_click);
    modal->btn_close.style        = UI_BTN_STYLE_RECT;
    modal->btn_close.color_bg     = 0x3186;
    modal->btn_close.color_active = 0xF800;
    modal->btn_close.color_text   = 0xFFFF;
    modal->btn_close.user_data    = modal;

    // 底部按钮布局
    int16_t bot_y = modal->rect.y + modal->rect.h - 38;
    if (btn_mode == UI_MODAL_BTN_OK) {
        int16_t bw = 120;
        ui_btn_init(&modal->btn_confirm, modal->rect.x + (w - bw) / 2, bot_y, bw, 30, "确定", on_modal_confirm_btn_click);
        modal->btn_confirm.style        = UI_BTN_STYLE_RECT;
        modal->btn_confirm.color_bg     = 0x03E0;
        modal->btn_confirm.color_active = 0x07E0;
        modal->btn_confirm.color_text   = 0xFFFF;
        modal->btn_confirm.user_data    = modal;
    } else if (btn_mode == UI_MODAL_BTN_OK_CANCEL) {
        int16_t bw = (w - 36) / 2;
        ui_btn_init(&modal->btn_cancel, modal->rect.x + 12, bot_y, bw, 30, "取消", on_modal_close_btn_click);
        modal->btn_cancel.style        = UI_BTN_STYLE_RECT;
        modal->btn_cancel.color_bg     = 0x4208;
        modal->btn_cancel.color_active = 0xF800;
        modal->btn_cancel.color_text   = 0xFFFF;
        modal->btn_cancel.user_data    = modal;

        ui_btn_init(&modal->btn_confirm, modal->rect.x + w - bw - 12, bot_y, bw, 30, "应用生效", on_modal_confirm_btn_click);
        modal->btn_confirm.style        = UI_BTN_STYLE_RECT;
        modal->btn_confirm.color_bg     = 0x03E0;
        modal->btn_confirm.color_active = 0x07E0;
        modal->btn_confirm.color_text   = 0xFFFF;
        modal->btn_confirm.user_data    = modal;
    }
}

void ui_modal_show(ui_modal_t *modal, const char *title, void *user_data) {
    if (!modal) return;
    if (title) modal->title = title;
    modal->user_data        = user_data;
    modal->is_open          = true;
    modal->sv_body.scroll_y = 0;
}

void ui_modal_hide(ui_modal_t *modal) {
    if (modal) modal->is_open = false;
}

/* ================= 3. 模态触控调度 (防误触 + 滑动锁死) ================= */

bool ui_modal_process_touch(ui_modal_t *modal, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    if (!modal || !modal->is_open) return false;

    bool need_refresh = false;

    // 1. 优先将触摸事件分发给内部滚动视口 (内部已自带 8px 防抖与全局捕获锁)
    need_refresh |= ui_scrollview_process_touch(&modal->sv_body, is_touching, tx, ty, now_tick);

    // 2. ⚡ 核心保护：若用户正处于拖拽滑动状态，绝不触发固定按钮或子控件
    if (!modal->sv_body.is_dragged) {
        need_refresh |= ui_btn_process_touch(&modal->btn_close, is_touching, tx, ty, now_tick);
        if (modal->btn_mode == UI_MODAL_BTN_OK) {
            need_refresh |= ui_btn_process_touch(&modal->btn_confirm, is_touching, tx, ty, now_tick);
        } else if (modal->btn_mode == UI_MODAL_BTN_OK_CANCEL) {
            need_refresh |= ui_btn_process_touch(&modal->btn_cancel, is_touching, tx, ty, now_tick);
            need_refresh |= ui_btn_process_touch(&modal->btn_confirm, is_touching, tx, ty, now_tick);
        }

        // 分发给内容区自定义交互组件
        if (modal->touch_content) {
            need_refresh |= modal->touch_content(modal, is_touching, tx, ty, now_tick, modal->user_data);
        }
    }

    return need_refresh;
}

/* ================= 4. 切片光栅化渲染 (层级分明，零无用计算) ================= */

void ui_modal_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, ui_modal_t *modal) {
    if (!tile || !modal || !modal->is_open) return;

    ui_rect_t overlap;
    // 切片相交快速裁剪：完全不相交的切片直接 0 开销退出
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &modal->rect, &overlap)) {
        return;
    }

    // 图层 1: 擦除弹窗底板背景
    ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &overlap, modal->color_bg);

    // 图层 2: 视口区域内容光栅化 (受 ui_draw_set_clip 保护)
    ui_scrollview_draw(tile, &modal->sv_body);

    // 图层 3: 顶层标题栏遮罩 (强行遮盖向上滑出视口的内容残像)
    ui_rect_t header_rect = ui_rect_make(modal->rect.x + 1, modal->rect.y + 1, (int16_t)(modal->rect.w - 2), 37);
    ui_rect_t hdr_ov;
    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &header_rect, &hdr_ov)) {
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &hdr_ov, modal->color_bg);
        if (modal->title) {
            ui_draw_string(tile, modal->rect.x + 12, modal->rect.y + 11, modal->title, FONT_16, modal->color_title, modal->color_bg);
        }
        ui_draw_fast_hline(tile, modal->rect.x + 4, modal->rect.y + 37, modal->rect.w - 8, 0x4208);
        ui_btn_draw(tile, &modal->btn_close);
    }

    // 图层 4: 顶层底部操作栏遮罩 (强行遮盖向下滑出视口的内容残像)
    if (modal->btn_mode != UI_MODAL_BTN_NONE) {
        ui_rect_t footer_rect = ui_rect_make(modal->rect.x + 1, (int16_t)(modal->rect.y + modal->rect.h - 45), (int16_t)(modal->rect.w - 2), 44);
        ui_rect_t ftr_ov;
        if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &footer_rect, &ftr_ov)) {
            ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &ftr_ov, modal->color_bg);
            ui_draw_fast_hline(tile, modal->rect.x + 4, footer_rect.y, modal->rect.w - 8, 0x4208);
            if (modal->btn_mode == UI_MODAL_BTN_OK) {
                ui_btn_draw(tile, &modal->btn_confirm);
            } else {
                ui_btn_draw(tile, &modal->btn_cancel);
                ui_btn_draw(tile, &modal->btn_confirm);
            }
        }
    }

    // 图层 5: 弹窗最外层高亮发光边框
    ui_draw_rect(tile, &modal->rect, modal->color_border);
}

#include "ui_numpad.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#define TOUCH_SLOP_PX   8 // 8 像素滑动防抖死区

void ui_numpad_init(ui_numpad_t *np, ui_numpad_cb_t on_confirm) {
    if (!np) return; /*[cite: 23] */

    np->rect = ui_rect_make(50, 30, 380, 260); /*[cite: 23] */
    np->is_open = false; /*[cite: 23] */
    np->title[0] = '\0'; /*[cite: 23] */
    np->buffer[0] = '\0'; /*[cite: 23] */
    np->cursor_pos = 0; /*[cite: 23] */
    np->unit_count = 0; /*[cite: 23] */
    np->selected_unit = -1; /*[cite: 23] */

    np->down_x        = 0;
    np->down_y        = 0;
    np->is_touch_down = false;
    np->is_dragged    = false;

    np->color_bg          = 0x10A2; // 极深蓝黑底[cite: 23]
    np->color_preview_bg  = 0x0842; // 预览框深灰底[cite: 23]
    np->color_key_num_bg  = 0x2126; // 数字按键暗灰底[cite: 23]
    np->color_key_fn_bg   = 0x4208; // 功能按键红棕底[cite: 23]
    np->color_key_unit_bg = 0x18C3; // 单位按键深蓝底[cite: 23]
    np->color_key_ok_bg   = 0x03E0; // 确认按键亮绿底[cite: 23]
    np->color_border      = 0x07FF; // 青蓝边框高亮[cite: 23]

    np->on_confirm = on_confirm; /*[cite: 23] */
    np->user_data  = NULL; /*[cite: 23] */
}

void ui_numpad_open(ui_numpad_t *np, const char *title, const char *init_val,
                    const char **units, uint8_t unit_count, int8_t default_unit_idx, void *user_data) {
    if (!np) return; /*[cite: 23] */

    np->is_open = true; /*[cite: 23] */
    np->is_touch_down = false;
    np->is_dragged    = false;

    strncpy(np->title, title ? title : "数字微调输入", sizeof(np->title) - 1); /*[cite: 23] */
    np->title[sizeof(np->title) - 1] = '\0'; /*[cite: 23] */

    if (init_val) { /*[cite: 23] */
        strncpy(np->buffer, init_val, UI_NUMPAD_BUF_LEN - 1); /*[cite: 23] */
        np->buffer[UI_NUMPAD_BUF_LEN - 1] = '\0'; /*[cite: 23] */
        np->cursor_pos = (uint8_t)strlen(np->buffer); /*[cite: 23] */
    } else {
        np->buffer[0] = '\0'; /*[cite: 23] */
        np->cursor_pos = 0; /*[cite: 23] */
    }

    np->unit_count = (unit_count > UI_NUMPAD_MAX_UNITS) ? UI_NUMPAD_MAX_UNITS : unit_count; /*[cite: 23] */
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < np->unit_count; i++) { /*[cite: 23] */
        np->units[i] = units ? units[i] : ""; /*[cite: 23] */
    }
    np->selected_unit = (default_unit_idx < (int8_t)np->unit_count) ? default_unit_idx : -1; /*[cite: 23] */
    np->user_data = user_data; /*[cite: 23] */
}

void ui_numpad_close(ui_numpad_t *np) {
    if (!np) return; /*[cite: 23] */
    np->is_open       = false; /*[cite: 23] */
    np->is_touch_down = false;
    np->is_dragged    = false;
}

static void get_key_rect(const ui_numpad_t *np, uint8_t row, uint8_t col, ui_rect_t *out_rect) {
    int16_t start_x = np->rect.x + 8; /*[cite: 23] */
    int16_t start_y = np->rect.y + 76; /*[cite: 23] */
    int16_t key_w   = 68; /*[cite: 23] */
    int16_t key_h   = 38; /*[cite: 23] */
    int16_t gap_x   = 6; /*[cite: 23] */
    int16_t gap_y   = 6; /*[cite: 23] */

    if (col == 3 && row >= 2) { /*[cite: 23] */
        out_rect->x = start_x + 3 * (key_w + gap_x); /*[cite: 23] */
        out_rect->y = start_y + 2 * (key_h + gap_y); /*[cite: 23] */
        out_rect->w = key_w; /*[cite: 23] */
        out_rect->h = key_h * 2 + gap_y; /*[cite: 23] */
        return; /*[cite: 23] */
    }

    out_rect->x = start_x + col * (key_w + gap_x); /*[cite: 23] */
    out_rect->y = start_y + row * (key_h + gap_y); /*[cite: 23] */
    out_rect->w = key_w; /*[cite: 23] */
    out_rect->h = key_h; /*[cite: 23] */
}

static void handle_key_action(ui_numpad_t *np, uint8_t row, uint8_t col) {
    if (col < 3) { /*[cite: 23] */
        static const char k_key_map[4][3] = {
            {'7', '8', '9'},
            {'4', '5', '6'},
            {'1', '2', '3'},
            {'0', '.', '-'}
        };
        char ch = k_key_map[row][col]; /*[cite: 23] */

        if (ch == '-') { /*[cite: 23] */
            if (np->buffer[0] == '-') { /*[cite: 23] */
                memmove(np->buffer, np->buffer + 1, strlen(np->buffer)); /*[cite: 23] */
                if (np->cursor_pos > 0) np->cursor_pos--; /*[cite: 23] */
            } else if (np->cursor_pos < UI_NUMPAD_BUF_LEN - 1) { /*[cite: 23] */
                memmove(np->buffer + 1, np->buffer, strlen(np->buffer) + 1); /*[cite: 23] */
                np->buffer[0] = '-'; /*[cite: 23] */
                np->cursor_pos++; /*[cite: 23] */
            }
        } else if (ch == '.') { /*[cite: 23] */
            if (strchr(np->buffer, '.') == NULL && np->cursor_pos < UI_NUMPAD_BUF_LEN - 1) { /*[cite: 23] */
                np->buffer[np->cursor_pos++] = '.'; /*[cite: 23] */
                np->buffer[np->cursor_pos] = '\0'; /*[cite: 23] */
            }
        } else {
            if (np->cursor_pos < UI_NUMPAD_BUF_LEN - 1) { /*[cite: 23] */
                np->buffer[np->cursor_pos++] = ch; /*[cite: 23] */
                np->buffer[np->cursor_pos] = '\0'; /*[cite: 23] */
            }
        }
    } else if (col == 3) { /*[cite: 23] */
        if (row == 0) { // DEL 退格[cite: 23]
            if (np->cursor_pos > 0) { /*[cite: 23] */
                np->buffer[--np->cursor_pos] = '\0'; /*[cite: 23] */
            }
        } else if (row == 1) { // CLR 清空[cite: 23]
            np->buffer[0] = '\0'; /*[cite: 23] */
            np->cursor_pos = 0; /*[cite: 23] */
        } else { // OK 确认提交[cite: 23]
            double val = strtod(np->buffer, NULL); /*[cite: 23] */
            const char *unit_str = (np->selected_unit >= 0 && np->selected_unit < (int8_t)np->unit_count) ? 
                                    np->units[np->selected_unit] : ""; /*[cite: 23] */
            if (np->on_confirm) { /*[cite: 23] */
                np->on_confirm(np, val, unit_str, np->buffer, np->user_data); /*[cite: 23] */
            }
            ui_numpad_close(np); /*[cite: 23] */
        }
    } else if (col == 4) { /*[cite: 23] */
        if (row < np->unit_count) { /*[cite: 23] */
            np->selected_unit = (int8_t)row; /*[cite: 23] */
        }
    }
}

bool ui_numpad_process_touch(ui_numpad_t *np, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    (void)now_tick;
    if (!np || !np->is_open) return false; /*[cite: 23] */

    if (is_touching) {
        if (!np->is_touch_down) {
            // A. 初次按下 (Touch Down): 记录坐标起点
            np->is_touch_down = true;
            np->is_dragged    = false;
            np->down_x        = tx;
            np->down_y        = ty;
        } else {
            // B. 持续移动 (Touch Move): 8px 防抖死区判定
            int16_t dx = abs(tx - np->down_x);
            int16_t dy = abs(ty - np->down_y);
            if (!np->is_dragged && (dx >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX || dy >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX)) {
                np->is_dragged = true; // 锁定为滑动，放弃按键触发
            }
        }
        return false;
    } else {
        // C. ⚡ 手指松开 (Touch Up): 唯一允许触发按键输入与关闭的时机
        if (np->is_touch_down) {
            np->is_touch_down = false;

            if (!np->is_dragged) {
                // 1. 点击弹窗外部空白区域 -> 关闭
                if (!ui_rect_contains_point(&np->rect, tx, ty)) {
                    ui_numpad_close(np);
                    return true;
                }

                // 2. 右上角关闭按钮 [X]
                ui_rect_t close_btn = ui_rect_make(np->rect.x + np->rect.w - 36, np->rect.y + 6, 30, 22);
                if (ui_rect_contains_point(&close_btn, tx, ty)) {
                    ui_numpad_close(np);
                    return true;
                }

                // 3. 遍历 4x5 键帽矩阵
                for (uint8_t r = 0; r < 4; r++) {
                    for (uint8_t c = 0; c < 5; c++) {
                        if (c == 3 && r == 3) continue;
                        if (c == 4 && r >= np->unit_count) continue;

                        ui_rect_t kr;
                        get_key_rect(np, r, c, &kr);
                        if (ui_rect_contains_point(&kr, tx, ty)) {
                            handle_key_action(np, r, c);
                            return true;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            np->is_dragged = false;
        }
    }

    return false;
}

void ui_numpad_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_numpad_t *np) {
    if (!tile || !np || !np->is_open) return; /*[cite: 23] */

    ui_rect_t overlap;
    // 1. 切片相交快速裁剪：完全不相交的切片直接 0 开销退出
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &np->rect, &overlap)) return; /*[cite: 23] */

    // 2. 绘制弹窗背景与边框
    ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &overlap, np->color_bg); /*[cite: 23] */
    ui_draw_rect(tile, &np->rect, np->color_border); /*[cite: 23] */

    ui_draw_set_clip(&np->rect); /*[cite: 23] */

    // 3. 绘制标题栏 (带 Y 轴切片重叠守卫)
    int16_t title_y = np->rect.y + 8;
    if ((title_y + 16) > tile->rect.y && title_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
        ui_draw_string(tile, np->rect.x + 12, title_y, np->title, FONT_16, 0xFFE0, np->color_bg); /*[cite: 23] */
    }

    // 右上角关闭按钮 [X]
    ui_rect_t close_btn = ui_rect_make(np->rect.x + np->rect.w - 36, np->rect.y + 6, 30, 22); /*[cite: 23] */
    ui_rect_t close_overlap;
    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &close_btn, &close_overlap)) { /*[cite: 23] */
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &close_overlap, 0x4208); /*[cite: 23] */
        ui_draw_rect(tile, &close_btn, 0x8410); /*[cite: 23] */
        ui_draw_string(tile, close_btn.x + 10, close_btn.y + 3, "X", FONT_16, 0xFFFF, 0x4208); /*[cite: 23] */
    }

    // 4. 绘制输入预览看板 (Preview Box)
    ui_rect_t preview_box = ui_rect_make(np->rect.x + 8, np->rect.y + 34, np->rect.w - 16, 34); /*[cite: 23] */
    ui_rect_t box_overlap;
    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &preview_box, &box_overlap)) { /*[cite: 23] */
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &box_overlap, np->color_preview_bg); /*[cite: 23] */
        ui_draw_rect(tile, &preview_box, np->color_border); /*[cite: 23] */

        // 拼接实时字符串与光标
        char disp_str[UI_NUMPAD_BUF_LEN + 4];
        snprintf(disp_str, sizeof(disp_str), "%s_", np->buffer[0] ? np->buffer : "0"); /*[cite: 23] */
        
        int16_t text_y = preview_box.y + 7;
        if ((text_y + 20) > tile->rect.y && text_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
            ui_draw_string(tile, preview_box.x + 8, text_y, disp_str, FONT_20, 0xFFFF, np->color_preview_bg);
        }

        // 选中的单位胶囊 (右对齐)
        if (np->selected_unit >= 0 && np->selected_unit < (int8_t)np->unit_count) { /*[cite: 23] */
            const char *unit_str = np->units[np->selected_unit]; /*[cite: 23] */
            int16_t u_len = (int16_t)strlen(unit_str) * 8 + 12; /*[cite: 23] */
            ui_rect_t u_badge = ui_rect_make(preview_box.x + preview_box.w - u_len - 6, preview_box.y + 6, u_len, 22); /*[cite: 23] */
            ui_rect_t u_ov;
            if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &u_badge, &u_ov)) {
                ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &u_ov, 0x07FF);
                ui_draw_string(tile, u_badge.x + 6, u_badge.y + 3, unit_str, FONT_16, 0x0000, 0x07FF); /*[cite: 23] */
            }
        }
    }

    // 5. 遍历渲染 4x5 键帽矩阵 (算术切片剪枝)
    for (uint8_t r = 0; r < 4; r++) {
        for (uint8_t c = 0; c < 5; c++) {
            if (c == 3 && r == 3) continue; // OK 跨行跳过[cite: 23]
            if (c == 4 && r >= np->unit_count) continue; /*[cite: 23] */

            ui_rect_t kr;
            get_key_rect(np, r, c, &kr); /*[cite: 23] */

            ui_rect_t k_overlap;
            if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &kr, &k_overlap)) continue; /*[cite: 23] */

            uint16_t k_bg = np->color_key_num_bg; /*[cite: 23] */
            uint16_t k_text_col = 0xFFFF; /*[cite: 23] */
            char key_str[8] = ""; /*[cite: 23] */

            if (c < 3) { /*[cite: 23] */
                static const char *k_labels[4][3] = {
                    {"7", "8", "9"},
                    {"4", "5", "6"},
                    {"1", "2", "3"},
                    {"0", ".", "+/-"}
                };
                strncpy(key_str, k_labels[r][c], sizeof(key_str) - 1); /*[cite: 23] */
            } else if (c == 3) { /*[cite: 23] */
                if (r == 0) { strncpy(key_str, "DEL", sizeof(key_str)-1); k_bg = np->color_key_fn_bg; k_text_col = 0xF800; } /*[cite: 23] */
                else if (r == 1) { strncpy(key_str, "CLR", sizeof(key_str)-1); k_bg = np->color_key_fn_bg; k_text_col = 0xFFE0; } /*[cite: 23] */
                else { strncpy(key_str, "OK", sizeof(key_str)-1); k_bg = np->color_key_ok_bg; } /*[cite: 23] */
            } else if (c == 4) { /*[cite: 23] */
                strncpy(key_str, np->units[r], sizeof(key_str)-1); /*[cite: 23] */
                bool is_active_u = ((int8_t)r == np->selected_unit); /*[cite: 23] */
                k_bg = is_active_u ? 0x07FF : np->color_key_unit_bg; /*[cite: 23] */
                k_text_col = is_active_u ? 0x0000 : 0x07FF; /*[cite: 23] */
            }

            ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &k_overlap, k_bg); /*[cite: 23] */
            ui_draw_rect(tile, &kr, 0x39E7); /*[cite: 23] */

            // 键帽文字居中
            int16_t str_w = (int16_t)strlen(key_str) * 8; /*[cite: 23] */
            int16_t tx = kr.x + (kr.w - str_w) / 2; /*[cite: 23] */
            int16_t ty = kr.y + (kr.h - 16) / 2; /*[cite: 23] */

            if ((ty + 16) > tile->rect.y && ty < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
                ui_draw_string(tile, tx, ty, key_str, FONT_16, k_text_col, k_bg); /*[cite: 23] */
            }
        }
    }

    ui_draw_reset_clip(); /*[cite: 23] */
}

#ifndef UI_NUMPAD_H

#endif

#define UI_NUMPAD_MAX_UNITS 4
#define UI_NUMPAD_BUF_LEN   32

typedef struct ui_numpad ui_numpad_t;

// 确认提交回调：回传数字浮点值、单位字符串、原始文本与用户私有指针[cite: 24]
typedef void (*ui_numpad_cb_t)(ui_numpad_t *np, double val, const char *unit, const char *raw_str, void *user_data);

struct ui_numpad {
    ui_rect_t           rect;           // 弹窗物理包围盒 (居中 380x260)[cite: 24]
    bool                is_open; /*[cite: 24] */
    char                title[32];      // 弹窗标题[cite: 24]
    char                buffer[UI_NUMPAD_BUF_LEN]; // 输入缓冲区[cite: 24]
    uint8_t             cursor_pos; /*[cite: 24] */

    const char         *units[UI_NUMPAD_MAX_UNITS]; // 可选单位列表[cite: 24]
    uint8_t             unit_count; /*[cite: 24] */
    int8_t              selected_unit;  // 当前选中的单位索引 (-1 为无单位)[cite: 24]

    // ⚡ 触控手势防抖与防误触状态机
    int16_t             down_x;
    int16_t             down_y;
    bool                is_touch_down;
    bool                is_dragged;     // 是否已识别为滑动手势 (位移 > 8px)

    // 配色方案 (RGB565)[cite: 24]
    uint16_t            color_bg; /*[cite: 24] */
    uint16_t            color_preview_bg; /*[cite: 24] */
    uint16_t            color_key_num_bg; /*[cite: 24] */
    uint16_t            color_key_fn_bg; /*[cite: 24] */
    uint16_t            color_key_unit_bg; /*[cite: 24] */
    uint16_t            color_key_ok_bg; /*[cite: 24] */
    uint16_t            color_border; /*[cite: 24] */

    ui_numpad_cb_t      on_confirm; /*[cite: 24] */
    void               *user_data; /*[cite: 24] */
};

// 初始化数字小键盘[cite: 24]
void ui_numpad_init(ui_numpad_t *np, ui_numpad_cb_t on_confirm);

// 弹出数字小键盘 (传入标题、初始值、可选单位数组与默认单位索引)[cite: 24]
void ui_numpad_open(ui_numpad_t *np, const char *title, const char *init_val,
                    const char **units, uint8_t unit_count, int8_t default_unit_idx, void *user_data);

// 关闭键盘[cite: 24]
void ui_numpad_close(ui_numpad_t *np);

void ui_numpad_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_numpad_t *np);

ui_packet_table.c.c 315 行 · 13212 B
点击展开
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

#include "ui_packet_table.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"

#define TOUCH_SLOP_PX   8 // 8 像素滑动防抖死区

void ui_packet_table_init(ui_packet_table_t *tbl, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h, ui_pkt_select_cb_t on_select) {
    if (!tbl) return;

    tbl->rect = ui_rect_make(x, y, w, h);
    tbl->head         = 0;
    tbl->count        = 0;
    tbl->selected_idx = -1;
    tbl->header_h     = 22;
    tbl->row_h        = 24;
    tbl->scroll_y     = 0;
    tbl->auto_scroll  = true;

    tbl->down_x         = 0;
    tbl->down_y         = 0;
    tbl->scroll_start_y = 0;
    tbl->is_touch_down  = false;
    tbl->is_dragged     = false;
    tbl->is_scrolling   = false;

    tbl->color_bg          = 0x0842; // 极深灰底
    tbl->color_header_bg   = 0x10A2; // 表头深蓝黑底
    tbl->color_row_even    = 0x1082; // 偶数行底色
    tbl->color_row_odd     = 0x18C3; // 奇数行稍亮底色
    tbl->color_selected_bg = 0x2146; // 选中行暗青蓝高亮
    tbl->color_border      = 0x39E7; // 分割线颜色
    tbl->color_scrollbar   = 0x07FF; // 青蓝指示滑块

    tbl->on_select  = on_select;
    tbl->user_data  = NULL;
}

void ui_packet_table_clear(ui_packet_table_t *tbl) {
    if (!tbl) return;
    tbl->head         = 0;
    tbl->count        = 0;
    tbl->selected_idx = -1;
    tbl->scroll_y     = 0;
    tbl->auto_scroll  = true;
}

void ui_packet_table_push(ui_packet_table_t *tbl, uint32_t time_us, uint16_t addr, bool is_read, 
                          const uint8_t *data, uint8_t len, ui_pkt_status_t status) {
    if (!tbl) return;

    ui_pkt_row_t *row = &tbl->rows[tbl->head];
    row->time_us  = time_us;
    row->addr     = addr;
    row->is_read  = is_read;
    row->status   = status;
    row->data_len = (len > UI_PKT_DATA_MAX) ? UI_PKT_DATA_MAX : len;

    if (data && row->data_len > 0) {
        memcpy(row->data, data, row->data_len);
    }

    tbl->head = (tbl->head + 1) % UI_PKT_MAX_ROWS;
    if (tbl->count < UI_PKT_MAX_ROWS) {
        tbl->count++;
    }

    // 自动跟随吸底追踪最新报文
    if (tbl->auto_scroll) {
        int16_t body_h = tbl->rect.h - tbl->header_h;
        int16_t total_h = (int16_t)tbl->count * tbl->row_h;
        int16_t max_scroll = total_h - body_h;
        tbl->scroll_y = (max_scroll > 0) ? max_scroll : 0;
    }
}

const ui_pkt_row_t *ui_packet_table_get_selected(const ui_packet_table_t *tbl) {
    if (!tbl || tbl->selected_idx < 0 || tbl->selected_idx >= (int16_t)tbl->count) return NULL;

    uint16_t start_idx = (tbl->count < UI_PKT_MAX_ROWS) ? 0 : tbl->head;
    uint16_t ring_idx = (start_idx + tbl->selected_idx) % UI_PKT_MAX_ROWS;
    return &tbl->rows[ring_idx];
}

/* ================= 触控状态机 (Touch Slop + Touch-Up 触发) ================= */

bool ui_packet_table_process_touch(ui_packet_table_t *tbl, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    (void)now_tick;
    if (!tbl || tbl->count == 0) return false;

    ui_rect_t body_rect = ui_rect_make(tbl->rect.x, (int16_t)(tbl->rect.y + tbl->header_h), tbl->rect.w, (int16_t)(tbl->rect.h - tbl->header_h));

    int16_t total_h = (int16_t)tbl->count * tbl->row_h;
    int16_t max_scroll = total_h - body_rect.h;
    if (max_scroll < 0) max_scroll = 0;

    bool need_redraw = false;

    if (is_touching) {
        if (!tbl->is_touch_down) {
            // A. 初次按下 (Touch Down): 仅在表体区域记录起点
            if (ui_rect_contains_point(&body_rect, tx, ty)) {
                tbl->is_touch_down  = true;
                tbl->is_dragged     = false;
                tbl->is_scrolling   = false;
                tbl->down_x         = tx;
                tbl->down_y         = ty;
                tbl->scroll_start_y = tbl->scroll_y;
            }
        } else {
            // B. 持续移动 (Touch Move): 8px 防抖死区与 Touch Capture 全局追踪
            int16_t dy = ty - tbl->down_y;
            int16_t dx = tx - tbl->down_x;

            if (!tbl->is_dragged && (abs(dy) >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX || abs(dx) >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX)) {
                tbl->is_dragged   = true;
                tbl->is_scrolling = true;
                tbl->auto_scroll  = false; // 手动滑动时自动解除吸底
            }

            if (tbl->is_dragged && max_scroll > 0) {
                int16_t new_scroll = tbl->scroll_start_y - dy;
                if (new_scroll < 0) new_scroll = 0;
                if (new_scroll > max_scroll) new_scroll = max_scroll;

                if (tbl->scroll_y != new_scroll) {
                    tbl->scroll_y = new_scroll;
                    // 滑动到最底部时重新恢复吸底
                    tbl->auto_scroll = (tbl->scroll_y >= max_scroll);
                    need_redraw = true;
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        // C. ⚡ 手指松开 (Touch Up): 唯一允许触发条目选中的时机
        if (tbl->is_touch_down) {
            tbl->is_touch_down = false;

            // 只有从未发生滑动的纯点按才判定点击行
            if (!tbl->is_dragged && ui_rect_contains_point(&body_rect, tx, ty)) {
                int16_t rel_y = ty - (body_rect.y - tbl->scroll_y);
                if (rel_y >= 0 && rel_y < total_h) {
                    int16_t clicked_idx = rel_y / tbl->row_h;
                    if (clicked_idx < (int16_t)tbl->count) {
                        tbl->selected_idx = clicked_idx;
                        if (tbl->on_select) {
                            tbl->on_select(tbl, clicked_idx, ui_packet_table_get_selected(tbl), tbl->user_data);
                        }
                        need_redraw = true;
                    }
                }
            }

            tbl->is_dragged   = false;
            tbl->is_scrolling = false;
        }
    }

    return need_redraw;
}

static void format_pkt_time(char *buf, size_t sz, uint32_t us) {
    if (us < 1000) {
        snprintf(buf, sz, "%lu us", (unsigned long)us);
    } else if (us < 1000000) {
        snprintf(buf, sz, "%.2f ms", (float)us / 1000.0f);
    } else {
        snprintf(buf, sz, "%.3f s", (float)us / 1000000.0f);
    }
}

/* ================= 4. 切片光栅化渲染 (O(1) 索引剪枝) ================= */

void ui_packet_table_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_packet_table_t *tbl) {
    if (!tile || !tbl) return;

    ui_rect_t overlap;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &tbl->rect, &overlap)) return;

    // 1. 擦除背景与外边框
    ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &overlap, tbl->color_bg);
    ui_draw_rect(tile, &tbl->rect, tbl->color_border);

    // ================= A. 顶部固定表头 =================
    ui_rect_t header_rect = ui_rect_make(tbl->rect.x, tbl->rect.y, tbl->rect.w, tbl->header_h);
    ui_rect_t hdr_overlap;
    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &header_rect, &hdr_overlap)) {
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &hdr_overlap, tbl->color_header_bg);
        ui_draw_fast_hline(tile, tbl->rect.x, (int16_t)(tbl->rect.y + tbl->header_h - 1), tbl->rect.w, 0x07FF);

        int16_t hy = tbl->rect.y + 3;
        ui_draw_string(tile, tbl->rect.x + 4,   hy, "#",      FONT_16, 0x07FF, tbl->color_header_bg);
        ui_draw_string(tile, tbl->rect.x + 36,  hy, "Time",   FONT_16, 0x07FF, tbl->color_header_bg);
        ui_draw_string(tile, tbl->rect.x + 104, hy, "Dir",    FONT_16, 0x07FF, tbl->color_header_bg);
        ui_draw_string(tile, tbl->rect.x + 138, hy, "Addr",   FONT_16, 0x07FF, tbl->color_header_bg);
        ui_draw_string(tile, tbl->rect.x + 186, hy, "Payload",FONT_16, 0x07FF, tbl->color_header_bg);
        ui_draw_string(tile, tbl->rect.x + 322, hy, "Status", FONT_16, 0x07FF, tbl->color_header_bg);
    }

    // ================= B. 表体滚动区域 =================
    ui_rect_t body_rect = ui_rect_make(tbl->rect.x, (int16_t)(tbl->rect.y + tbl->header_h), tbl->rect.w, (int16_t)(tbl->rect.h - tbl->header_h));
    ui_draw_set_clip(&body_rect);

    int16_t base_y = body_rect.y - tbl->scroll_y;
    uint16_t start_idx = (tbl->count < UI_PKT_MAX_ROWS) ? 0 : tbl->head;

    // ⚡ 核心优化：仅计算与当前切片相交的行索引范围 (从 O(N) 降至 O(1))
    int16_t first_idx = (tile->rect.y - base_y) / tbl->row_h;
    int16_t last_idx  = (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h - 1 - base_y) / tbl->row_h;

    if (first_idx < 0) first_idx = 0;
    if (last_idx >= (int16_t)tbl->count) last_idx = (int16_t)tbl->count - 1;

    for (int16_t i = first_idx; i <= last_idx; i++) {
        int16_t row_y = (int16_t)(base_y + i * tbl->row_h);
        ui_rect_t row_rect = ui_rect_make((int16_t)(tbl->rect.x + 2), (int16_t)(row_y + 1), (int16_t)(tbl->rect.w - 4), (int16_t)(tbl->row_h - 2));

        ui_rect_t row_overlap;
        if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &row_rect, &row_overlap)) continue;

        bool is_selected = (i == tbl->selected_idx);
        uint16_t row_bg = is_selected ? tbl->color_selected_bg : ((i % 2 == 0) ? tbl->color_row_even : tbl->color_row_odd);

        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &row_overlap, row_bg);
        if (is_selected) {
            ui_draw_rect(tile, &row_rect, 0x07FF);
        }

        uint16_t ring_idx = (start_idx + i) % UI_PKT_MAX_ROWS;
        const ui_pkt_row_t *row = &tbl->rows[ring_idx];
        int16_t ty = row_y + 4;

        if ((ty + 16) > tile->rect.y && ty < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
            // 1. 序号
            char num_str[8];
            snprintf(num_str, sizeof(num_str), "%u", (unsigned int)(i + 1));
            ui_draw_string(tile, tbl->rect.x + 4, ty, num_str, FONT_16, 0x8410, row_bg);

            // 2. 时间戳
            char time_str[16];
            format_pkt_time(time_str, sizeof(time_str), row->time_us);
            ui_draw_string(tile, tbl->rect.x + 36, ty, time_str, FONT_16, 0xFFFF, row_bg);

            // 3. 读写方向
            if (row->status == UI_PKT_STAT_START || row->status == UI_PKT_STAT_STOP) {
                ui_draw_string(tile, tbl->rect.x + 104, ty, "-", FONT_16, 0x8410, row_bg);
            } else {
                ui_draw_string(tile, tbl->rect.x + 104, ty, row->is_read ? "R" : "W", FONT_16, row->is_read ? 0x07FF : 0xFFE0, row_bg);
            }

            // 4. 设备地址
            char addr_str[8];
            if (row->addr != 0xFFFF) {
                snprintf(addr_str, sizeof(addr_str), "0x%02X", (unsigned int)(row->addr & 0xFF));
                ui_draw_string(tile, tbl->rect.x + 138, ty, addr_str, FONT_16, 0xFD20, row_bg);
            } else {
                ui_draw_string(tile, tbl->rect.x + 138, ty, "--", FONT_16, 0x8410, row_bg);
            }

            // 5. 负载数据 Payload
            if (row->data_len > 0) {
                char data_str[24] = "";
                int off = 0;
                for (uint8_t d = 0; d < row->data_len && off < 20; d++) {
                    off += snprintf(data_str + off, sizeof(data_str) - off, "%02X ", row->data[d]);
                }
                ui_draw_string(tile, tbl->rect.x + 186, ty, data_str, FONT_16, 0xFFFF, row_bg);
            } else {
                ui_draw_string(tile, tbl->rect.x + 186, ty, "--", FONT_16, 0x8410, row_bg);
            }

            // 6. 状态 Status
            const char *stat_str = "ACK";
            uint16_t stat_col    = 0x07E0; // 亮绿
            if (row->status == UI_PKT_STAT_NACK) {
                stat_str = "NACK"; stat_col = 0xF800;
            } else if (row->status == UI_PKT_STAT_ERR) {
                stat_str = "ERR";  stat_col = 0xF800;
            } else if (row->status == UI_PKT_STAT_START) {
                stat_str = "START"; stat_col = 0xFFE0;
            } else if (row->status == UI_PKT_STAT_STOP) {
                stat_str = "STOP";  stat_col = 0xF81F;
            }
            ui_draw_string(tile, tbl->rect.x + 322, ty, stat_str, FONT_16, stat_col, row_bg);
        }
    }

    // ================= C. 右侧微型滚动条 =================
    int16_t total_h = (int16_t)tbl->count * tbl->row_h;
    if (total_h > body_rect.h) {
        int16_t bar_w = 2;
        int16_t track_h = body_rect.h - 4;
        int16_t thumb_h = (int16_t)(((int32_t)body_rect.h * track_h) / total_h);
        if (thumb_h < 12) thumb_h = 12;

        int16_t max_scroll = total_h - body_rect.h;
        int16_t thumb_y = body_rect.y + 2;
        if (max_scroll > 0) {
            thumb_y += (int16_t)(((int32_t)tbl->scroll_y * (track_h - thumb_h)) / max_scroll);
        }
        int16_t thumb_x = tbl->rect.x + tbl->rect.w - bar_w - 2;

        ui_rect_t thumb_rect = ui_rect_make(thumb_x, thumb_y, bar_w, thumb_h);
        ui_rect_t thumb_overlap;
        if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &thumb_rect, &thumb_overlap)) {
            ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &thumb_overlap, tbl->color_scrollbar);
        }
    }

    ui_draw_reset_clip();
}

#ifndef UI_PACKET_TABLE_H
#define UI_PACKET_TABLE_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>

#include "ui_geometry.h"
#include "ui_tile.h"
#include "chinese.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

#define UI_PKT_MAX_ROWS 48
#define UI_PKT_DATA_MAX 6

typedef enum {
    UI_PKT_STAT_ACK = 0,    // 应答 ACK (亮绿)
    UI_PKT_STAT_NACK,       // 无应答 NACK (亮红)
    UI_PKT_STAT_ERR,        // 总线冲突/错误 (亮红)
    UI_PKT_STAT_START,      // 起始信号 START (黄色)
    UI_PKT_STAT_STOP        // 停止信号 STOP (紫红)
} ui_pkt_status_t;

typedef struct {
    uint32_t        time_us;                    // 时间戳 (微秒)
    uint16_t        addr;                       // 设备地址 (0x00~0xFF, 0xFFFF 为无)
    bool            is_read;                    // true: 读(R), false: 写(W)
    uint8_t         data[UI_PKT_DATA_MAX];      // 负载数据
    uint8_t         data_len;                   // 有效长度
    ui_pkt_status_t status;                     // 状态标识
} ui_pkt_row_t;

typedef struct ui_packet_table ui_packet_table_t;
typedef void (*ui_pkt_select_cb_t)(ui_packet_table_t *table, int16_t index, const ui_pkt_row_t *row, void *user_data);

struct ui_packet_table {
    ui_rect_t           rect;           // 表格整体物理包围盒
    ui_pkt_row_t        rows[UI_PKT_MAX_ROWS]; // 环形缓冲区
    uint16_t            head;           // 写入头
    uint16_t            count;          // 有效总行数
    int16_t             selected_idx;   // 选中的逻辑行索引 (-1 为未选中)

    int16_t             header_h;       // 表头高度 (默认 22px)
    int16_t             row_h;          // 行高度 (默认 24px)
    int16_t             scroll_y;       // 当前滚动偏移
    bool                auto_scroll;    // 自动跟随吸底

    // ⚡ 触控手势防抖与防误触状态机
    int16_t             down_x;
    int16_t             down_y;
    int16_t             scroll_start_y;
    bool                is_touch_down;
    bool                is_dragged;     // 是否已识别为滑动拖拽 (位移 > 8px)
    bool                is_scrolling;   // 是否正在滚动

    // 配色方案 (RGB565)
    uint16_t            color_bg;
    uint16_t            color_header_bg;
    uint16_t            color_row_even;
    uint16_t            color_row_odd;
    uint16_t            color_selected_bg;
    uint16_t            color_border;
    uint16_t            color_scrollbar;

    ui_pkt_select_cb_t  on_select;
    void               *user_data;
};

// 初始化报文解析表格
void ui_packet_table_init(ui_packet_table_t *tbl, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h, ui_pkt_select_cb_t on_select);

// 清空全部报文记录
void ui_packet_table_clear(ui_packet_table_t *tbl);

// 推入一条新解析的报文
void ui_packet_table_push(ui_packet_table_t *tbl, uint32_t time_us, uint16_t addr, bool is_read, 
                          const uint8_t *data, uint8_t len, ui_pkt_status_t status);

// 获取当前选中的报文条目指针
const ui_pkt_row_t *ui_packet_table_get_selected(const ui_packet_table_t *tbl);

// 触控交互分发 (集成 8px 死区保护与 Touch-Up 松手触发)
bool ui_packet_table_process_touch(ui_packet_table_t *tbl, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick);

// 切片光栅化渲染 (表头固顶 + O(1) 索引算术剪枝)
void ui_packet_table_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_packet_table_t *tbl);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

#endif // UI_PACKET_TABLE_H
ui_progressbar.c.c 169 行 · 6262 B

#include "ui_progressbar.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

static const char *k_stage_names[6] = {
    "待机就绪", "扇区擦除中...", "固件烧录中...", "校验比对中...", "烧录完成 (PASS)", "烧录失败 (ERROR)"
};

static const uint16_t k_stage_colors[6] = {
    0x632C, // 待机: 灰暗
    0xFD20, // 擦除: 橙黄
    0x07E0, // 写入: 亮绿
    0x07FF, // 校验: 青蓝
    0x07E0, // 完成: 纯绿
    0xF800  // 失败: 亮红
};

void ui_progressbar_init(ui_progressbar_t *pb, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h, bool show_details) {
    if (!pb) return;

    pb->rect = ui_rect_make(x, y, w, h);
    pb->stage = UI_PROG_STAGE_IDLE;
    pb->progress = 0;

    pb->current_bytes = 0;
    pb->total_bytes   = 0;
    pb->speed_kbps    = 0.0f;
    pb->start_time_ms = 0;
    pb->elapsed_ms    = 0;
    pb->show_details  = show_details;

    pb->color_track_bg = 0x1082; // 极深蓝黑底槽
    pb->color_border   = 0x39E7; // 深灰外框
    pb->color_text     = 0xFFFF; // 纯白文字

    for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
        pb->color_stage[i] = k_stage_colors[i];
    }
}

void ui_progressbar_set_stage(ui_progressbar_t *pb, ui_prog_stage_t stage) {
    if (!pb || stage > UI_PROG_STAGE_ERROR) return;
    pb->stage = stage;
    if (stage == UI_PROG_STAGE_DONE) {
        pb->progress = 100;
    } else if (stage == UI_PROG_STAGE_IDLE) {
        pb->progress = 0;
    }
}

void ui_progressbar_set_progress(ui_progressbar_t *pb, uint8_t progress) {
    if (!pb) return;
    pb->progress = (progress > 100) ? 100 : progress;
}

void ui_progressbar_update_stats(ui_progressbar_t *pb, uint32_t current_bytes, uint32_t total_bytes, uint32_t now_ms) {
    if (!pb) return;

    pb->current_bytes = current_bytes;
    pb->total_bytes   = total_bytes;

    if (total_bytes > 0) {
        uint32_t prog = ((uint64_t)current_bytes * 100) / total_bytes;
        pb->progress = (prog > 100) ? 100 : (uint8_t)prog;
    }

    if (pb->start_time_ms == 0) {
        pb->start_time_ms = now_ms;
    }

    if (now_ms >= pb->start_time_ms) {
        pb->elapsed_ms = now_ms - pb->start_time_ms;
        if (pb->elapsed_ms > 50 && current_bytes > 0) {
            pb->speed_kbps = ((float)current_bytes / 1024.0f) / ((float)pb->elapsed_ms / 1000.0f);
        }
    }
}

void ui_progressbar_reset(ui_progressbar_t *pb) {
    if (!pb) return;
    pb->stage         = UI_PROG_STAGE_IDLE;
    pb->progress      = 0;
    pb->current_bytes = 0;
    pb->total_bytes   = 0;
    pb->speed_kbps    = 0.0f;
    pb->start_time_ms = 0;
    pb->elapsed_ms    = 0;
}

/* ================= 切片光栅化渲染 (行级算术剪枝) ================= */

void ui_progressbar_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_progressbar_t *pb) {
    if (!tile || !pb) return;

    ui_rect_t overlap;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &pb->rect, &overlap)) return;

    ui_draw_set_clip(&pb->rect);

    // 1. 顶部阶段状态标题行 (高度 20px)
    if (pb->show_details) {
        int16_t header_y = pb->rect.y + 2;
        if ((header_y + 16) > tile->rect.y && header_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
            ui_draw_string(tile, pb->rect.x + 2, header_y, "烧录状态:", FONT_16, 0x07FF, 0x0000);
            ui_draw_string(tile, pb->rect.x + 80, header_y, k_stage_names[pb->stage], FONT_16, pb->color_stage[pb->stage], 0x0000);
        }
    }

    // 2. 中间进度条槽体 (高度 22px)
    int16_t bar_y = pb->rect.y + (pb->show_details ? 22 : 0);
    int16_t bar_h = pb->show_details ? 22 : pb->rect.h;
    ui_rect_t bar_rect = ui_rect_make(pb->rect.x, bar_y, pb->rect.w, bar_h);

    ui_rect_t bar_ov;
    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &bar_rect, &bar_ov)) {
        // 绘制底槽与外框
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &bar_ov, pb->color_track_bg);
        ui_draw_rect(tile, &bar_rect, pb->color_border);

        // 绘制动态前进的填充条
        if (pb->progress > 0) {
            int16_t fill_w = (int16_t)(((int32_t)(bar_rect.w - 4) * pb->progress) / 100);
            if (fill_w < 2) fill_w = 2;
            ui_rect_t fill_rect = ui_rect_make(bar_rect.x + 2, bar_rect.y + 2, fill_w, bar_rect.h - 4);

            ui_rect_t fill_ov;
            if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &fill_rect, &fill_ov)) {
                ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &fill_ov, pb->color_stage[pb->stage]);
            }
        }

        // 进度条内部居中百分比数字
        int16_t text_y = bar_rect.y + (bar_rect.h - 16) / 2;
        if ((text_y + 16) > tile->rect.y && text_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
            char pct_str[8];
            snprintf(pct_str, sizeof(pct_str), "%d%%", pb->progress);
            int16_t str_w = (int16_t)strlen(pct_str) * 8;
            int16_t text_x = bar_rect.x + (bar_rect.w - str_w) / 2;
            ui_draw_string(tile, text_x, text_y, pct_str, FONT_16, 0xFFFF, pb->color_track_bg);
        }
    }

    // 3. 底部详细传输看板行 (高度 18px)
    if (pb->show_details) {
        int16_t stats_y = bar_rect.y + bar_rect.h + 8;
        if ((stats_y + 16) > tile->rect.y && stats_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
            char buf_size[32];
            char buf_speed[32];
            char buf_time[32];

            if (pb->total_bytes > 0) {
                snprintf(buf_size, sizeof(buf_size), "大小:%lu/%luB", 
                         (unsigned long)pb->current_bytes, (unsigned long)pb->total_bytes);
            } else {
                snprintf(buf_size, sizeof(buf_size), "大小: -- / --");
            }

            snprintf(buf_speed, sizeof(buf_speed), "速率:%.1fKB/s", pb->speed_kbps);
            snprintf(buf_time, sizeof(buf_time), "耗时:%.1fs", (float)pb->elapsed_ms / 1000.0f);

            ui_draw_string(tile, pb->rect.x + 2,   stats_y, buf_size,  FONT_16, 0x8410, 0x0000);
            ui_draw_string(tile, pb->rect.x + 200, stats_y, buf_speed, FONT_16, 0x07E0, 0x0000);
            ui_draw_string(tile, pb->rect.x + 320, stats_y, buf_time,  FONT_16, 0xFFE0, 0x0000);
        }
    }

    ui_draw_reset_clip();
}

#ifndef UI_PROGRESSBAR_H
#define UI_PROGRESSBAR_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include "ui_geometry.h"
#include "ui_tile.h"
#include "chinese.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {

// 烧录执行阶段枚举[cite: 28]
typedef enum {
    UI_PROG_STAGE_IDLE = 0,    // 0: 空闲就绪 (灰暗)[cite: 28]
    UI_PROG_STAGE_ERASE,       // 1: 扇区擦除中 (橙黄)[cite: 28]
    UI_PROG_STAGE_PROGRAM,     // 2: 固件写入中 (亮绿)[cite: 28]
    UI_PROG_STAGE_VERIFY,      // 3: 数据校验中 (青蓝)[cite: 28]
    UI_PROG_STAGE_DONE,        // 4: 烧录成功 (纯绿)[cite: 28]
    UI_PROG_STAGE_ERROR        // 5: 烧录失败/超时 (亮红)[cite: 28]
} ui_prog_stage_t;

typedef struct ui_progressbar ui_progressbar_t;

struct ui_progressbar {
    ui_rect_t           rect;           // 进度条控件外框物理包围盒[cite: 28]
    ui_prog_stage_t     stage;          // 当前执行阶段[cite: 28]
    uint8_t             progress;       // 0 ~ 100 百分比[cite: 28]

    // 统计数据[cite: 28]
    uint32_t            current_bytes;  // 已写入字节[cite: 28]
    uint32_t            total_bytes;    // 总固件字节[cite: 28]
    float               speed_kbps;     // 实时传输速率 (KB/s)[cite: 28]
    uint32_t            start_time_ms;  // 阶段起始时间戳[cite: 28]
    uint32_t            elapsed_ms;     // 阶段已耗时[cite: 28]
    bool                show_details;   // 是否渲染下方详细统计看板[cite: 28]

    // 配色方案 (RGB565)[cite: 28]
    uint16_t            color_track_bg; // 进度条底槽颜色[cite: 28]
    uint16_t            color_border;   // 边框颜色[cite: 28]
    uint16_t            color_text;     // 标签文字颜色[cite: 28]
    uint16_t            color_stage[6]; // 各阶段专属填充色[cite: 28]
};

// 初始化进度条 (show_details=true 时自动排版顶部阶段与底部速率/耗时看板)[cite: 28]
void ui_progressbar_init(ui_progressbar_t *pb, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h, bool show_details);

// 设置当前执行阶段 (自动切换对应主题色)[cite: 28]
void ui_progressbar_set_stage(ui_progressbar_t *pb, ui_prog_stage_t stage);

// 设置进度百分比 (0 ~ 100)[cite: 28]
void ui_progressbar_set_progress(ui_progressbar_t *pb, uint8_t progress);

// 更新传输统计数据 (自动计算百分比与实时 KB/s 速率)[cite: 28]
void ui_progressbar_update_stats(ui_progressbar_t *pb, uint32_t current_bytes, uint32_t total_bytes, uint32_t now_ms);

// 重置进度条到待机状态[cite: 28]
void ui_progressbar_reset(ui_progressbar_t *pb);

// 切片光栅化渲染 (带垂直行级算术剪枝与视口保护)[cite: 28]
void ui_progressbar_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_progressbar_t *pb);

// 获取进度条自身脏矩形 (用于 ui_render_dirty_clipped 极速局部推流)
static inline ui_rect_t ui_progressbar_get_dirty_rect(const ui_progressbar_t *pb) {
    return pb ? pb->rect : ui_rect_make(0, 0, 0, 0);
}
