ui_scrollview.c.c 131 行 · 4671 B
点击展开
#include "ui_scrollview.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <stdlib.h>

#define TOUCH_SLOP_PX   8 // 8 像素滑动防抖死区

void ui_scrollview_init(ui_scrollview_t *sv, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h,
                        int16_t content_h, ui_scrollview_draw_cb_t draw_content) {
    if (!sv) return;

    sv->view_rect = ui_rect_make(x, y, w, h);
    sv->content_h = (content_h < h) ? h : content_h;
    sv->scroll_y  = 0;

    sv->down_x         = 0;
    sv->down_y         = 0;
    sv->scroll_start_y = 0;
    sv->is_touch_down  = false;
    sv->is_dragged     = false;
    sv->is_scrolling   = false;

    sv->draw_content   = draw_content;
    sv->user_data      = NULL;

    sv->color_bg        = 0x10A2; // 默认深蓝黑底
    sv->color_scrollbar = 0x07FF; // 默认青蓝滚动条
    sv->show_scrollbar  = true;
}

bool ui_scrollview_process_touch(ui_scrollview_t *sv, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    (void)now_tick;
    if (!sv) return false;

    int16_t max_scroll = sv->content_h - sv->view_rect.h;
    if (max_scroll < 0) max_scroll = 0;

    bool need_redraw = false;

    if (is_touching) {
        if (!sv->is_touch_down) {
            // A. 初次按下 (Touch Down): 必须落在视口物理区域内
            if (ui_rect_contains_point(&sv->view_rect, tx, ty)) {
                sv->is_touch_down  = true;
                sv->is_dragged     = false;
                sv->is_scrolling   = false;
                sv->down_x         = tx;
                sv->down_y         = ty;
                sv->scroll_start_y = sv->scroll_y;
            }
        } else {
            // B. 持续移动 (Touch Move): ⚡ 获得 Touch Capture，哪怕滑出视口依然平滑追踪
            int16_t dy = ty - sv->down_y;
            int16_t dx = tx - sv->down_x;

            if (!sv->is_dragged && (abs(dy) >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX || abs(dx) >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX)) {
                sv->is_dragged   = true;
                sv->is_scrolling = true;
            }

            if (sv->is_dragged && max_scroll > 0) {
                int16_t new_scroll = sv->scroll_start_y - dy;
                if (new_scroll < 0) new_scroll = 0;
                if (new_scroll > max_scroll) new_scroll = max_scroll;

                if (sv->scroll_y != new_scroll) {
                    sv->scroll_y = new_scroll;
                    need_redraw  = true;
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        // C. 手指松开 (Touch Up): 释放手势锁
        if (sv->is_touch_down) {
            sv->is_touch_down = false;
            sv->is_dragged    = false;
            if (sv->is_scrolling) {
                sv->is_scrolling = false;
                need_redraw      = true;
            }
        }
    }

    return need_redraw;
}

void ui_scrollview_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_scrollview_t *sv) {
    if (!tile || !sv) return;

    ui_rect_t overlap;
    // 1. 切片相交裁剪：完全不相交的切片直接 0 开销退出
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &sv->view_rect, &overlap)) {
        return;
    }

    // 2. 擦除当前切片内的视口底色 (彻底消除旧图元残影与拖影)
    ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &overlap, sv->color_bg);

    // 3. 开启像素级视口裁剪
    ui_draw_set_clip(&sv->view_rect);

    // 4. 触发内容光栅化回调
    if (sv->draw_content) {
        sv->draw_content(tile, &sv->view_rect, sv->scroll_y, sv->user_data);
    }

    // 5. 恢复裁剪
    ui_draw_reset_clip();

    // 6. 绘制右侧平滑滚动条
    if (sv->show_scrollbar && (sv->content_h > sv->view_rect.h)) {
        int16_t bar_w      = 3;
        int16_t bar_margin = 2;
        int16_t track_h    = sv->view_rect.h - (bar_margin * 2);

        int16_t thumb_h = (int16_t)(((int32_t)sv->view_rect.h * track_h) / sv->content_h);
        if (thumb_h < 12) thumb_h = 12;
        if (thumb_h > track_h) thumb_h = track_h;

        int16_t max_scroll = sv->content_h - sv->view_rect.h;
        int16_t thumb_y    = sv->view_rect.y + bar_margin;
        if (max_scroll > 0) {
            thumb_y += (int16_t)(((int32_t)sv->scroll_y * (track_h - thumb_h)) / max_scroll);
        }
        int16_t thumb_x    = sv->view_rect.x + sv->view_rect.w - bar_w - bar_margin;

        ui_rect_t thumb_rect = ui_rect_make(thumb_x, thumb_y, bar_w, thumb_h);
        ui_rect_t thumb_overlap;
        if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &thumb_rect, &thumb_overlap)) {
            ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &thumb_overlap, sv->color_scrollbar);
        }
    }
}
ui_scrollview.h.h 54 行 · 2140 B
点击展开
#ifndef UI_SCROLLVIEW_H
#define UI_SCROLLVIEW_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include "ui_geometry.h"
#include "ui_tile.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

typedef struct ui_scrollview ui_scrollview_t;

// 内容切片绘制回调函数原型
typedef void (*ui_scrollview_draw_cb_t)(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_rect_t *viewport, int16_t scroll_y, void *user_data);

struct ui_scrollview {
    ui_rect_t               view_rect;          // 视口在屏幕上的绝对物理矩形
    int16_t                 content_h;          // 虚拟内容的总像素高度
    int16_t                 scroll_y;           // 当前 Y 轴滚动偏移量 (0 ~ content_h - view_rect.h)

    // ⚡ 触控手势防抖与全局捕获状态机
    int16_t                 down_x;
    int16_t                 down_y;
    int16_t                 scroll_start_y;     // 按下时的初始滚动偏移
    bool                    is_touch_down;      // 触控按压中
    bool                    is_dragged;         // 是否已识别为滑动手势 (位移 > 8px)
    bool                    is_scrolling;       // 是否处于滚动状态

    // 绘制回调与上下文
    ui_scrollview_draw_cb_t draw_content;
    void                   *user_data;

    // 配色与指示条
    uint16_t                color_bg;           // 视口背景色
    uint16_t                color_scrollbar;    // 滚动条滑块颜色
    bool                    show_scrollbar;     // 是否显示滚动条
};

// 初始化滚动视口
void ui_scrollview_init(ui_scrollview_t *sv, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h,
                        int16_t content_h, ui_scrollview_draw_cb_t draw_content);

// 视口触控调度 (集成 8px 死区保护与 Touch Capture 全局捕获)
bool ui_scrollview_process_touch(ui_scrollview_t *sv, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick);

// 切片光栅化渲染 (带相交快速剔除与像素级视口裁剪)
void ui_scrollview_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_scrollview_t *sv);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

#endif // UI_SCROLLVIEW_H
ui_slider.c.c 209 行 · 8540 B
点击展开
#include "ui_slider.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <stdlib.h>

#define TOUCH_SLOP_PX   8 // 8 像素滑动防抖死区

/* ================= 1. 内部辅助函数 ================= */

static inline bool ui_slider_hit_test(const ui_slider_t *slider, int16_t x, int16_t y) {
    // 扩展手柄半径 + 6px 触控容差，显著提高触控跟手度
    int16_t pad = slider->knob_r + 6;
    return (x >= (slider->rect.x - pad) && x < (slider->rect.x + slider->rect.w + pad) &&
            y >= (slider->rect.y - pad) && y < (slider->rect.y + slider->rect.h + pad));
}

static inline int32_t clamp_val(int32_t v, int32_t min_v, int32_t max_v) {
    if (v < min_v) return min_v;
    if (v > max_v) return max_v;
    return v;
}

/* ================= 2. 初始化与设值 ================= */

void ui_slider_init(ui_slider_t *slider, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h,
                    int32_t min_val, int32_t max_val, int32_t cur_val,
                    ui_slider_cb_t on_change) {
    if (!slider) return;

    slider->rect = ui_rect_make(x, y, w, h);
    slider->dir  = (w >= h) ? UI_SLIDER_DIR_HOR : UI_SLIDER_DIR_VER;

    slider->min_val = min_val;
    slider->max_val = max_val;
    slider->cur_val = clamp_val(cur_val, min_val, max_val);
    slider->step    = 1;

    slider->color_track = 0x2104; // 深暗灰底轨
    slider->color_fill  = 0x07E0; // 亮绿激活进度
    slider->color_knob  = 0xFFFF; // 纯白手柄
    slider->knob_r      = (slider->dir == UI_SLIDER_DIR_HOR) ? (uint8_t)(h / 2 + 3) : (uint8_t)(w / 2 + 3);

    slider->down_x        = 0;
    slider->down_y        = 0;
    slider->is_touch_down = false;
    slider->is_dragging   = false;

    slider->on_change = on_change;
    slider->user_data = NULL;
}

void ui_slider_set_val(ui_slider_t *slider, int32_t val) {
    if (!slider) return;
    int32_t clamped = clamp_val(val, slider->min_val, slider->max_val);
    if (slider->cur_val != clamped) {
        slider->cur_val = clamped;
        if (slider->on_change) {
            slider->on_change(slider, slider->cur_val, slider->user_data);
        }
    }
}

void ui_slider_set_color(ui_slider_t *slider, uint16_t track, uint16_t fill, uint16_t knob) {
    if (!slider) return;
    slider->color_track = track;
    slider->color_fill  = fill;
    slider->color_knob  = knob;
}

/* ================= 3. 触控拖拽状态机 (Touch Capture + 8px 死区) ================= */

bool ui_slider_process_touch(ui_slider_t *slider, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    (void)now_tick;
    if (!slider) return false;

    bool value_changed = false;

    if (is_touching) {
        if (!slider->is_touch_down) {
            // A. 初次按下 (Touch Down): 命中判定
            if (ui_slider_hit_test(slider, tx, ty)) {
                slider->is_touch_down = true;
                slider->is_dragging   = true; // 获得 Touch Capture，进入拖拽追踪模式
                slider->down_x        = tx;
                slider->down_y        = ty;
            }
        }

        // B. 持续移动 (Touch Move): 只要获得 Touch Capture，哪怕滑出感应区依然平滑调节
        if (slider->is_touch_down && slider->is_dragging) {
            int32_t range = slider->max_val - slider->min_val;
            if (range <= 0) range = 1;

            int32_t new_val;
            if (slider->dir == UI_SLIDER_DIR_HOR) {
                // 水平方向物理映射 (左侧 min -> 右侧 max)
                int16_t rel_x = tx - slider->rect.x;
                if (rel_x < 0) rel_x = 0;
                if (rel_x > slider->rect.w) rel_x = slider->rect.w;
                new_val = slider->min_val + (int32_t)(((int64_t)rel_x * range) / slider->rect.w);
            } else {
                // 垂直方向物理映射 (底部 min -> 顶部 max)
                int16_t rel_y = (slider->rect.y + slider->rect.h) - ty;
                if (rel_y < 0) rel_y = 0;
                if (rel_y > slider->rect.h) rel_y = slider->rect.h;
                new_val = slider->min_val + (int32_t)(((int64_t)rel_y * range) / slider->rect.h);
            }

            // 步进对齐
            if (slider->step > 1) {
                new_val = ((new_val + slider->step / 2) / slider->step) * slider->step;
            }
            new_val = clamp_val(new_val, slider->min_val, slider->max_val);

            if (slider->cur_val != new_val) {
                slider->cur_val = new_val;
                value_changed = true;
                if (slider->on_change) {
                    slider->on_change(slider, slider->cur_val, slider->user_data);
                }
            }
            return value_changed || slider->is_touch_down;
        }
    } else {
        // C. ⚡ 手指松开 (Touch Up): 释放手势锁
        if (slider->is_touch_down) {
            slider->is_touch_down = false;
            slider->is_dragging   = false;
            return true; // 重绘恢复手柄高亮颜色
        }
    }

    return false;
}

/* ================= 4. 切片光栅化渲染 (外扩手柄半径硬裁剪) ================= */

void ui_slider_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_slider_t *slider) {
    if (!tile || !slider) return;

    // 1. 包围盒外扩手柄半径，执行相交裁剪判定
    ui_rect_t bound = ui_slider_get_dirty_rect(slider);
    ui_rect_t overlap;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &bound, &overlap)) {
        return;
    }

    int32_t range = slider->max_val - slider->min_val;
    if (range <= 0) range = 1;

    // 2. 水平滑动条绘制
    if (slider->dir == UI_SLIDER_DIR_HOR) {
        int16_t track_h = (slider->rect.h > 4) ? (slider->rect.h / 3) : 3;
        if (track_h < 3) track_h = 3;
        int16_t track_y = slider->rect.y + (slider->rect.h - track_h) / 2;

        // 计算滑块中心与已填充宽度
        int32_t progress_w = ((int64_t)(slider->cur_val - slider->min_val) * slider->rect.w) / range;
        int16_t knob_x = slider->rect.x + (int16_t)progress_w;
        int16_t knob_y = slider->rect.y + slider->rect.h / 2;

        // A. 绘制未填充背景轨道
        ui_rect_t bg_track = ui_rect_make(
            (int16_t)(slider->rect.x + progress_w),
            track_y,
            (int16_t)(slider->rect.w - progress_w),
            track_h
        );
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &bg_track, slider->color_track);

        // B. 绘制已激活填充轨道
        if (progress_w > 0) {
            ui_rect_t fill_track = ui_rect_make(slider->rect.x, track_y, (int16_t)progress_w, track_h);
            ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &fill_track, slider->color_fill);
        }

        // C. 绘制圆形手柄 (拖拽时金黄高亮，静止时纯白，内部带黑心造型)
        uint16_t k_color = slider->is_dragging ? 0xFFE0 : slider->color_knob;
        ui_draw_fill_circle(tile, knob_x, knob_y, slider->knob_r, k_color);
        if (slider->knob_r > 3) {
            ui_draw_fill_circle(tile, knob_x, knob_y, slider->knob_r - 3, 0x10A2); // 极深蓝黑心造型
        }
    }
    // 3. 垂直滑动条绘制
    else {
        int16_t track_w = (slider->rect.w > 4) ? (slider->rect.w / 3) : 3;
        if (track_w < 3) track_w = 3;
        int16_t track_x = slider->rect.x + (slider->rect.w - track_w) / 2;

        int32_t progress_h = ((int64_t)(slider->cur_val - slider->min_val) * slider->rect.h) / range;
        int16_t knob_x = slider->rect.x + slider->rect.w / 2;
        int16_t knob_y = (slider->rect.y + slider->rect.h) - (int16_t)progress_h;

        // A. 绘制顶部未填充轨道
        ui_rect_t bg_track = ui_rect_make(track_x, slider->rect.y, track_w, (int16_t)(slider->rect.h - progress_h));
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &bg_track, slider->color_track);

        // B. 绘制底部已激活填充轨道
        if (progress_h > 0) {
            ui_rect_t fill_track = ui_rect_make(track_x, knob_y, track_w, (int16_t)progress_h);
            ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &fill_track, slider->color_fill);
        }

        // C. 绘制垂直圆形手柄
        uint16_t k_color = slider->is_dragging ? 0xFFE0 : slider->color_knob;
        ui_draw_fill_circle(tile, knob_x, knob_y, slider->knob_r, k_color);
        if (slider->knob_r > 3) {
            ui_draw_fill_circle(tile, knob_x, knob_y, slider->knob_r - 3, 0x10A2);
        }
    }
}

/*
 * @Author: 轩

#ifndef UI_SLIDER_H
#define UI_SLIDER_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include "ui_geometry.h"
#include "ui_tile.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

typedef struct ui_slider ui_slider_t;

// 滑块数值变更回调函数类型
typedef void (*ui_slider_cb_t)(ui_slider_t *slider, int32_t val, void *user_data);

typedef enum {
    UI_SLIDER_DIR_HOR = 0, // 水平滑动
    UI_SLIDER_DIR_VER      // 垂直滑动
} ui_slider_dir_t;

struct ui_slider {
    ui_rect_t       rect;           // 控件本体包围盒（触控感应区域）
    ui_slider_dir_t dir;            // 滑动方向
    
    int32_t         min_val;        // 最小值
    int32_t         max_val;        // 最大值
    int32_t         cur_val;        // 当前值
    int32_t         step;           // 步进值（<=1 为平滑连续）

    uint16_t        color_track;    // 轨道底色 (RGB565)
    uint16_t        color_fill;     // 进度激活填充色 (RGB565)
    uint16_t        color_knob;     // 滑块手柄普通色 (RGB565)
    uint8_t         knob_r;         // 滑块圆形手柄半径 (px)

    // ⚡ 触控手势防抖与全局捕获状态机
    int16_t         down_x;
    int16_t         down_y;
    bool            is_touch_down;  // 是否在滑块区域内按下
    bool            is_dragging;    // 是否处于拖拽滑动中

    ui_slider_cb_t  on_change;      // 数值变化回调
    void           *user_data;      // 用户私有数据
};

// 初始化滑动条
void ui_slider_init(ui_slider_t *slider, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h,
                    int32_t min_val, int32_t max_val, int32_t cur_val,
                    ui_slider_cb_t on_change);

// 设置当前值（自动边界钳位并触发回调）
void ui_slider_set_val(ui_slider_t *slider, int32_t val);

// 设置自定义配色
void ui_slider_set_color(ui_slider_t *slider, uint16_t track, uint16_t fill, uint16_t knob);

// 触控状态机 (集成 8px 死区保护与 Touch Capture 全局拖拽捕获)
bool ui_slider_process_touch(ui_slider_t *slider, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick);

// 切片光栅化渲染 (外扩手柄半径安全相交裁剪)
void ui_slider_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_slider_t *slider);

// 获取滑块完整脏矩形 (含 knob_r 外扩，用于 ui_render_dirty_clipped 局部极速推流)
static inline ui_rect_t ui_slider_get_dirty_rect(const ui_slider_t *slider) {
    if (!slider) return ui_rect_make(0, 0, 0, 0);
    return ui_rect_make(
        (int16_t)(slider->rect.x - slider->knob_r - 2),
        (int16_t)(slider->rect.y - slider->knob_r - 2),
        (int16_t)(slider->rect.w + (slider->knob_r + 2) * 2),
        (int16_t)(slider->rect.h + (slider->knob_r + 2) * 2)
    );
}

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

#endif // UI_SLIDER_H

#include "ui_status_bar.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

#define TOUCH_SLOP_PX   8

/* ================= 1. 内置微型纯矢量硬件图标引擎 ================= */

// 1. 绘制 W25Qxx 外部 SPI Flash 芯片矢量图标 (宽 18px, 高 12px)
static void draw_vector_w25(ui_tile_t *tile, int16_t x, int16_t y, bool is_ok, uint16_t col_act, uint16_t col_inact) {
    uint16_t color = is_ok ? col_act : col_inact;

    // SOP8 芯片主体封装 (12x10)
    ui_rect_t chip_body = ui_rect_make((int16_t)(x + 3), (int16_t)(y + 1), 12, 10);
    ui_draw_rect(tile, &chip_body, color);

    // 引脚 1 方向缺口圆点
    ui_draw_point(tile, x + 5, y + 3, color);

    // 左右各 4 根引脚焊盘
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        int16_t pin_y = (int16_t)(y + 2 + i * 2);
        ui_draw_point(tile, x + 1, pin_y, color);
        ui_draw_point(tile, x + 2, pin_y, color);
        ui_draw_point(tile, x + 15, pin_y, color);
        ui_draw_point(tile, x + 16, pin_y, color);
    }
}

// 2. 绘制 MicroSD 卡矢量图标 (宽 12px, 高 12px)
static void draw_vector_sd(ui_tile_t *tile, int16_t x, int16_t y, bool mounted, uint16_t col_act, uint16_t col_inact) {
    uint16_t color = mounted ? col_act : col_inact;

    ui_rect_t sd_rect = ui_rect_make(x, y, 11, 12);
    ui_draw_rect(tile, &sd_rect, color);

    // 右上缺角
    ui_draw_point(tile, x + 10, y, 0x0000);
    ui_draw_point(tile, x + 9,  y, 0x0000);

    // 金手指打点
    ui_draw_fast_vline(tile, x + 2, (int16_t)(y + 2), 3, color);
    ui_draw_fast_vline(tile, x + 4, (int16_t)(y + 2), 3, color);
    ui_draw_fast_vline(tile, x + 6, (int16_t)(y + 2), 3, color);
    ui_draw_fast_vline(tile, x + 8, (int16_t)(y + 3), 2, color);
}

// 3. 绘制 Touch 触摸靶心图标 (宽 14px, 高 14px)
static void draw_vector_touch(ui_tile_t *tile, int16_t x, int16_t y, bool is_active, uint16_t col_act, uint16_t col_inact) {
    int16_t cx = x + 7;
    int16_t cy = y + 6;
    uint16_t color = is_active ? col_act : col_inact;

    // 外圈触控圆环 (R=5)
    ui_draw_circle(tile, cx, cy, 5, color);

    // 中心触控点 (按下时实心亮橙，未按下时为空心十字靶心)
    if (is_active) {
        ui_draw_fill_circle(tile, cx, cy, 2, color);
    } else {
        ui_draw_point(tile, cx, cy, color);
        ui_draw_point(tile, cx - 3, cy, color);
        ui_draw_point(tile, cx + 3, cy, color);
        ui_draw_point(tile, cx, cy - 3, color);
        ui_draw_point(tile, cx, cy + 3, color);
    }
}

// 4. 绘制 USB 接口矢量图标 (宽 14px, 高 12px)
static void draw_vector_usb(ui_tile_t *tile, int16_t x, int16_t y, bool connected, uint16_t col_act, uint16_t col_inact) {
    uint16_t color = connected ? col_act : col_inact;

    ui_draw_fast_vline(tile, x + 7, (int16_t)(y + 2), 7, color);
    ui_draw_fast_hline(tile, x + 3, (int16_t)(y + 4), 8, color);
    ui_draw_point(tile, x + 3, y + 3, color);
    ui_draw_point(tile, x + 10, y + 3, color);
    ui_draw_fill_circle(tile, x + 7, y + 10, 2, color);
}

/* ================= 2. 初始化与配置 ================= */

void ui_status_bar_init(ui_status_bar_t *sb, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h,
                        const char *title, ui_status_bar_click_cb_t on_click) {
    if (!sb) return;

    sb->rect = ui_rect_make(x, y, w, h);
    strncpy(sb->title, title ? title : "MiniRTOS System", sizeof(sb->title) - 1);
    sb->title[sizeof(sb->title) - 1] = '\0';

    sb->cpu_usage     = 0;
    sb->fps           = 60;

    sb->w25_ok        = true;
    sb->sd_mounted    = false;
    sb->touch_active  = false;
    sb->usb_connected = true;

    sb->down_x        = 0;
    sb->down_y        = 0;
    sb->is_touch_down = false;
    sb->is_dragged    = false;

    sb->color_bg            = 0x000F; // 极深藏青黑底
    sb->color_border        = 0x07FF; // 青蓝底部分割线
    sb->color_title         = 0x07FF; // 青蓝标题
    sb->color_telemetry     = 0xFFE0; // 亮黄 CPU/FPS
    sb->color_icon_active   = 0x07E0; // 亮绿激活图标
    sb->color_icon_inactive = 0x4208; // 暗灰未激活图标
    sb->color_icon_touch    = 0xFD20; // 橙黄触控按下高亮

    sb->on_click  = on_click;
    sb->user_data = NULL;
}

void ui_status_bar_set_title(ui_status_bar_t *sb, const char *title) {
    if (!sb || !title) return;
    strncpy(sb->title, title, sizeof(sb->title) - 1);
    sb->title[sizeof(sb->title) - 1] = '\0';
}

void ui_status_bar_set_telemetry(ui_status_bar_t *sb, uint8_t cpu_usage, uint8_t fps) {
    if (!sb) return;
    sb->cpu_usage = (cpu_usage > 100) ? 100 : cpu_usage;
    sb->fps       = (fps > 99) ? 99 : fps;
}

void ui_status_bar_set_w25(ui_status_bar_t *sb, bool is_ok) {
    if (sb) sb->w25_ok = is_ok;
}

void ui_status_bar_set_sd(ui_status_bar_t *sb, bool is_mounted) {
    if (sb) sb->sd_mounted = is_mounted;
}

void ui_status_bar_set_touch(ui_status_bar_t *sb, bool is_active) {
    if (sb) sb->touch_active = is_active;
}

void ui_status_bar_set_usb(ui_status_bar_t *sb, bool is_connected) {
    if (sb) sb->usb_connected = is_connected;
}

/* ================= 3. 触控状态机 (区域识别与点选) ================= */

bool ui_status_bar_process_touch(ui_status_bar_t *sb, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    (void)now_tick;
    if (!sb) return false;

    if (is_touching) {
        if (!sb->is_touch_down) {
            if (ui_rect_contains_point(&sb->rect, tx, ty)) {
                sb->is_touch_down = true;
                sb->is_dragged    = false;
                sb->down_x        = tx;
                sb->down_y        = ty;
            }
        } else {
            int16_t dx = abs(tx - sb->down_x);
            int16_t dy = abs(ty - sb->down_y);
            if (!sb->is_dragged && (dx >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX || dy >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX)) {
                sb->is_dragged = true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    } else {
        if (sb->is_touch_down) {
            sb->is_touch_down = false;

            if (!sb->is_dragged && ui_rect_contains_point(&sb->rect, tx, ty)) {
                ui_sb_hit_t hit = UI_SB_HIT_NONE;

                if (tx < (sb->rect.x + 180)) {
                    hit = UI_SB_HIT_TITLE;
                } else if (tx >= (sb->rect.x + 190) && tx <= (sb->rect.x + 335)) {
                    hit = UI_SB_HIT_TELEMETRY;
                } else if (tx > (sb->rect.x + 340) && tx <= (sb->rect.x + 375)) {
                    hit = UI_SB_HIT_W25;
                } else if (tx > (sb->rect.x + 375) && tx <= (sb->rect.x + 410)) {
                    hit = UI_SB_HIT_SD;
                } else if (tx > (sb->rect.x + 410) && tx <= (sb->rect.x + 445)) {
                    hit = UI_SB_HIT_TOUCH;
                } else if (tx > (sb->rect.x + 445)) {
                    hit = UI_SB_HIT_USB;
                }

                if (hit != UI_SB_HIT_NONE && sb->on_click) {
                    sb->on_click(sb, hit, sb->user_data);
                }
                sb->is_dragged = false;
                return true;
            }
            sb->is_dragged = false;
        }
    }

    return false;
}

/* ================= 4. 切片光栅化渲染 (微秒级推流) ================= */

void ui_status_bar_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_status_bar_t *sb) {
    if (!tile || !sb) return;

    ui_rect_t overlap;
    // 仅与处于屏幕最顶部的切片行相交 (如 Tile 0, Y: 0~31)
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &sb->rect, &overlap)) {
        return;
    }

    // 1. 擦除背景与底部分割线
    ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &overlap, sb->color_bg);
    ui_draw_fast_hline(tile, sb->rect.x, (int16_t)(sb->rect.y + sb->rect.h - 1), sb->rect.w, sb->color_border);

    int16_t text_y = sb->rect.y + (sb->rect.h - 16) / 2;
    int16_t icon_y = sb->rect.y + (sb->rect.h - 12) / 2;

    if ((text_y + 16) > tile->rect.y && text_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
        // 2. 左侧系统标题 (支持中英文)
        ui_draw_string(tile, sb->rect.x + 8, text_y, sb->title, FONT_16, sb->color_title, sb->color_bg);

        // 3. 性能遥测 (CPU% 与 真实物理 FPS)
        char telem_buf[32];
        snprintf(telem_buf, sizeof(telem_buf), "CPU:%2u%% FPS:%-2u", sb->cpu_usage, sb->fps);
        ui_draw_string(tile, sb->rect.x + 200, text_y, telem_buf, FONT_16, sb->color_telemetry, sb->color_bg);
    }

    // 4. 右侧硬件外设状态图标列 (W25Qxx -> SD -> Touch -> USB)
    draw_vector_w25(tile, (int16_t)(sb->rect.x + 352), icon_y, sb->w25_ok, sb->color_icon_active, sb->color_icon_inactive);
    draw_vector_sd(tile, (int16_t)(sb->rect.x + 386), icon_y, sb->sd_mounted, sb->color_icon_active, sb->color_icon_inactive);
    draw_vector_touch(tile, (int16_t)(sb->rect.x + 418), (int16_t)(icon_y - 1), sb->touch_active, sb->color_icon_touch, sb->color_icon_inactive);
    draw_vector_usb(tile, (int16_t)(sb->rect.x + 450), icon_y, sb->usb_connected, sb->color_icon_active, sb->color_icon_inactive);
}

    return ui_rect_make((int16_t)(sb->rect.x + 414), (int16_t)(sb->rect.y + 4), 20, 22);
}

ui_stepper.c.c 262 行 · 11387 B
点击展开
#include "ui_stepper.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

#define TOUCH_SLOP_PX       8   // 8 像素滑动防抖死区
#define INITIAL_REPEAT_MS   400 // 长按初始触发延时 (400ms)
#define FASTEST_REPEAT_MS   50  // 长按最高连发频率 (50ms/次)

static inline int32_t clamp_val(int32_t v, int32_t min_v, int32_t max_v) {
    if (v < min_v) return min_v;
    if (v > max_v) return max_v;
    return v;
}

void ui_stepper_init(ui_stepper_t *sp, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h,
                     int32_t min_val, int32_t max_val, int32_t cur_val, int32_t step,
                     const char *unit, ui_stepper_change_cb_t on_change) {
    if (!sp) return;

    sp->rect    = ui_rect_make(x, y, w, h);
    sp->min_val = min_val;
    sp->max_val = (max_val > min_val) ? max_val : min_val + 1;
    sp->cur_val = clamp_val(cur_val, sp->min_val, sp->max_val);
    sp->step    = (step > 0) ? step : 1;

    sp->unit    = unit ? unit : "";
    sp->prefix  = NULL;

    sp->hit_target         = UI_STEPPER_HIT_NONE;
    sp->down_x             = 0;
    sp->down_y             = 0;
    sp->is_touch_down      = false;
    sp->is_dragged         = false;
    sp->press_start_tick   = 0;
    sp->last_step_tick     = 0;
    sp->repeat_interval_ms = INITIAL_REPEAT_MS;

    sp->color_bg         = 0x0842; // 中心深灰底
    sp->color_btn_bg     = 0x2126; // 按钮暗灰底
    sp->color_btn_active = 0x07E0; // 按下亮绿高亮
    sp->color_border     = 0x39E7; // 边框深灰
    sp->color_text       = 0xFFFF; // 纯白字
    sp->color_unit       = 0x07FF; // 青蓝单位文本

    sp->on_change      = on_change;
    sp->on_click_val   = NULL;
    sp->user_data      = NULL;
}

void ui_stepper_set_val(ui_stepper_t *sp, int32_t val) {
    if (!sp) return;
    int32_t clamped = clamp_val(val, sp->min_val, sp->max_val);
    if (sp->cur_val != clamped) {
        sp->cur_val = clamped;
        if (sp->on_change) {
            sp->on_change(sp, sp->cur_val, sp->user_data);
        }
    }
}

void ui_stepper_set_prefix(ui_stepper_t *sp, const char *prefix) {
    if (sp) sp->prefix = prefix;
}

/* ================= 触控状态机 (短按点选 + 长按动态加速连发) ================= */

bool ui_stepper_process_touch(ui_stepper_t *sp, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    if (!sp) return false;

    int16_t btn_w = sp->rect.h; // 减/加按钮宽度等于控件高度 (正方形手柄)
    ui_rect_t dec_rect = ui_rect_make(sp->rect.x, sp->rect.y, btn_w, sp->rect.h);
    ui_rect_t inc_rect = ui_rect_make((int16_t)(sp->rect.x + sp->rect.w - btn_w), sp->rect.y, btn_w, sp->rect.h);
    ui_rect_t val_rect = ui_rect_make((int16_t)(sp->rect.x + btn_w), sp->rect.y, (int16_t)(sp->rect.w - 2 * btn_w), sp->rect.h);

    bool need_redraw = false;

    if (is_touching) {
        if (!sp->is_touch_down) {
            // A. 初次按下 (Touch Down): 判定具体命中区域
            if (ui_rect_contains_point(&dec_rect, tx, ty)) {
                sp->is_touch_down      = true;
                sp->hit_target         = UI_STEPPER_HIT_DEC;
                sp->down_x             = tx;
                sp->down_y             = ty;
                sp->is_dragged         = false;
                sp->press_start_tick   = now_tick;
                sp->last_step_tick     = now_tick;
                sp->repeat_interval_ms = INITIAL_REPEAT_MS;

                // 立即执行单次减步进
                if (sp->cur_val > sp->min_val) {
                    sp->cur_val = clamp_val(sp->cur_val - sp->step, sp->min_val, sp->max_val);
                    if (sp->on_change) sp->on_change(sp, sp->cur_val, sp->user_data);
                }
                need_redraw = true;
            } else if (ui_rect_contains_point(&inc_rect, tx, ty)) {
                sp->is_touch_down      = true;
                sp->hit_target         = UI_STEPPER_HIT_INC;
                sp->down_x             = tx;
                sp->down_y             = ty;
                sp->is_dragged         = false;
                sp->press_start_tick   = now_tick;
                sp->last_step_tick     = now_tick;
                sp->repeat_interval_ms = INITIAL_REPEAT_MS;

                // 立即执行单次加步进
                if (sp->cur_val < sp->max_val) {
                    sp->cur_val = clamp_val(sp->cur_val + sp->step, sp->min_val, sp->max_val);
                    if (sp->on_change) sp->on_change(sp, sp->cur_val, sp->user_data);
                }
                need_redraw = true;
            } else if (ui_rect_contains_point(&val_rect, tx, ty)) {
                sp->is_touch_down = true;
                sp->hit_target    = UI_STEPPER_HIT_VAL;
                sp->down_x        = tx;
                sp->down_y        = ty;
                sp->is_dragged    = false;
            }
        } else {
            // B. 持续按压 (Touch Hold): 8px 防抖死区检测
            int16_t dx = abs(tx - sp->down_x);
            int16_t dy = abs(ty - sp->down_y);
            if (!sp->is_dragged && (dx >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX || dy >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX)) {
                sp->is_dragged = true; // 锁定为滑动，放弃长按连发
            }

            // ⚡ 长按动态加速连发逻辑
            if (!sp->is_dragged && (sp->hit_target == UI_STEPPER_HIT_DEC || sp->hit_target == UI_STEPPER_HIT_INC)) {
                uint32_t hold_time = now_tick - sp->press_start_tick;
                if (hold_time > 300) {
                    if (now_tick - sp->last_step_tick >= sp->repeat_interval_ms) {
                        sp->last_step_tick = now_tick;

                        // 线性加速递减连发间隔
                        if (sp->repeat_interval_ms > FASTEST_REPEAT_MS) {
                            sp->repeat_interval_ms = (sp->repeat_interval_ms > 20) ? (sp->repeat_interval_ms - 20) : FASTEST_REPEAT_MS;
                        }

                        if (sp->hit_target == UI_STEPPER_HIT_DEC && sp->cur_val > sp->min_val) {
                            sp->cur_val = clamp_val(sp->cur_val - sp->step, sp->min_val, sp->max_val);
                            if (sp->on_change) sp->on_change(sp, sp->cur_val, sp->user_data);
                            need_redraw = true;
                        } else if (sp->hit_target == UI_STEPPER_HIT_INC && sp->cur_val < sp->max_val) {
                            sp->cur_val = clamp_val(sp->cur_val + sp->step, sp->min_val, sp->max_val);
                            if (sp->on_change) sp->on_change(sp, sp->cur_val, sp->user_data);
                            need_redraw = true;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return need_redraw;
    } else {
        // C. ⚡ 手指松开 (Touch Up)
        if (sp->is_touch_down) {
            sp->is_touch_down = false;

            // 若松手点在中心数值区域且未发生滑动，触发数值框点击回调
            if (!sp->is_dragged && sp->hit_target == UI_STEPPER_HIT_VAL) {
                if (ui_rect_contains_point(&val_rect, tx, ty) && sp->on_click_val) {
                    sp->on_click_val(sp, sp->user_data);
                }
            }

            sp->hit_target = UI_STEPPER_HIT_NONE;
            sp->is_dragged = false;
            return true; // 恢复按钮非高亮底色
        }
    }

    return false;
}

/* ================= 切片光栅化渲染 (三段式独立切片相交裁剪) ================= */

void ui_stepper_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_stepper_t *sp) {
    if (!tile || !sp) return;

    ui_rect_t overlap;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &sp->rect, &overlap)) {
        return;
    }

    int16_t btn_w = sp->rect.h;
    ui_rect_t dec_rect = ui_rect_make(sp->rect.x, sp->rect.y, btn_w, sp->rect.h);
    ui_rect_t inc_rect = ui_rect_make((int16_t)(sp->rect.x + sp->rect.w - btn_w), sp->rect.y, btn_w, sp->rect.h);
    ui_rect_t val_rect = ui_rect_make((int16_t)(sp->rect.x + btn_w), sp->rect.y, (int16_t)(sp->rect.w - 2 * btn_w), sp->rect.h);

    // 1. 绘制左侧减号按钮 [-]
    ui_rect_t dec_ov;
    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &dec_rect, &dec_ov)) {
        bool is_dec_active = (sp->is_touch_down && sp->hit_target == UI_STEPPER_HIT_DEC);
        uint16_t dec_bg = is_dec_active ? sp->color_btn_active : sp->color_btn_bg;

        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &dec_ov, dec_bg);
        ui_draw_rect(tile, &dec_rect, sp->color_border);

        // 居中绘制 "-" 号
        int16_t text_y = dec_rect.y + (dec_rect.h - 16) / 2;
        if ((text_y + 16) > tile->rect.y && text_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
            ui_draw_string(tile, dec_rect.x + (btn_w - 8) / 2, text_y, "-", FONT_16, 
                           is_dec_active ? 0x0000 : sp->color_text, dec_bg);
        }
    }

    // 2. 绘制中间数值回显框 [ Prefix + Value + Unit ]
    ui_rect_t val_ov;
    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &val_rect, &val_ov)) {
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &val_ov, sp->color_bg);
        ui_draw_rect(tile, &val_rect, sp->color_border);

        char num_str[32];
        snprintf(num_str, sizeof(num_str), "%ld", (long)sp->cur_val);

        int16_t num_w  = (int16_t)strlen(num_str) * 8;
        int16_t unit_w = sp->unit ? ((int16_t)strlen(sp->unit) * 8 + 4) : 0;
        int16_t pref_w = sp->prefix ? ((int16_t)strlen(sp->prefix) * 8 + 4) : 0;
        int16_t total_w = pref_w + num_w + unit_w;

        int16_t start_x = val_rect.x + (val_rect.w - total_w) / 2;
        int16_t text_y  = val_rect.y + (val_rect.h - 16) / 2;

        if ((text_y + 16) > tile->rect.y && text_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
            ui_draw_set_clip(&val_rect);

            // 绘制前缀
            if (sp->prefix) {
                ui_draw_string(tile, start_x, text_y, sp->prefix, FONT_16, 0x8410, sp->color_bg);
                start_x += pref_w;
            }

            // 绘制当前数值
            ui_draw_string(tile, start_x, text_y, num_str, FONT_16, sp->color_text, sp->color_bg);
            start_x += num_w;

            // 绘制单位
            if (sp->unit && sp->unit[0] != '\0') {
                ui_draw_string(tile, start_x + 4, text_y, sp->unit, FONT_16, sp->color_unit, sp->color_bg);
            }

            ui_draw_reset_clip();
        }
    }

    // 3. 绘制右侧加号按钮 [+]
    ui_rect_t inc_ov;
    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &inc_rect, &inc_ov)) {
        bool is_inc_active = (sp->is_touch_down && sp->hit_target == UI_STEPPER_HIT_INC);
        uint16_t inc_bg = is_inc_active ? sp->color_btn_active : sp->color_btn_bg;

        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &inc_ov, inc_bg);
        ui_draw_rect(tile, &inc_rect, sp->color_border);

        // 居中绘制 "+" 号
        int16_t text_y = inc_rect.y + (inc_rect.h - 16) / 2;
        if ((text_y + 16) > tile->rect.y && text_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
            ui_draw_string(tile, inc_rect.x + (btn_w - 8) / 2, text_y, "+", FONT_16, 
                           is_inc_active ? 0x0000 : sp->color_text, inc_bg);
        }
    }
}
ui_stepper.h.h 93 行 · 3721 B
点击展开
/*
 * @Author: 轩
 * @Date: 2026-08-21 14:10:21
 * @LastEditTime: 2026-08-21 14:10:27
 * @FilePath: \pico\ui\ui_stepper.h
 */
#ifndef UI_STEPPER_H
#define UI_STEPPER_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include "ui_geometry.h"
#include "ui_tile.h"
#include "chinese.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

typedef struct ui_stepper ui_stepper_t;

// 数值变更回调
typedef void (*ui_stepper_change_cb_t)(ui_stepper_t *sp, int32_t val, void *user_data);
// 点击中心文本框回调 (通常用于唤起全键盘精确输入)
typedef void (*ui_stepper_click_val_cb_t)(ui_stepper_t *sp, void *user_data);

typedef enum {
    UI_STEPPER_HIT_NONE = 0,
    UI_STEPPER_HIT_DEC,       // 命中左侧减号按钮 [-]
    UI_STEPPER_HIT_INC,       // 命中右侧加号按钮 [+]
    UI_STEPPER_HIT_VAL        // 命中中心数值显示区域
} ui_stepper_hit_t;

struct ui_stepper {
    ui_rect_t               rect;               // 控件物理包围盒 (如 180x36)
    int32_t                 min_val;            // 最小值
    int32_t                 max_val;            // 最大值
    int32_t                 cur_val;            // 当前值
    int32_t                 step;               // 单次步进增量

    const char             *unit;               // 单位文本 (如 "kHz", "mV", "%")
    const char             *prefix;             // 前缀文本 (如 "CH1:", "PWM:")

    // ⚡ 触控手势、防抖与长按连发状态机
    ui_stepper_hit_t        hit_target;         // 当前按压目标
    int16_t                 down_x;
    int16_t                 down_y;
    bool                    is_touch_down;
    bool                    is_dragged;         // 8px 防抖死区锁定
    uint32_t                press_start_tick;   // 初次按下的时间戳
    uint32_t                last_step_tick;     // 上次连发步进时间戳
    uint16_t                repeat_interval_ms; // 动态加速连发周期 (400ms -> 60ms)

    // 配色方案 (RGB565)
    uint16_t                color_bg;           // 中心数值框底色
    uint16_t                color_btn_bg;       // 减/加按钮底色
    uint16_t                color_btn_active;   // 按钮按下高亮色
    uint16_t                color_border;       // 边框分割线
    uint16_t                color_text;         // 数值与标签文本色
    uint16_t                color_unit;         // 单位文本色

    ui_stepper_change_cb_t  on_change;
    ui_stepper_click_val_cb_t on_click_val;
    void                   *user_data;
};

// 初始化步进微调器 (默认三段式 [ - ] [ Value + Unit ] [ + ])
void ui_stepper_init(ui_stepper_t *sp, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h,
                     int32_t min_val, int32_t max_val, int32_t cur_val, int32_t step,
                     const char *unit, ui_stepper_change_cb_t on_change);

// 设置步进器当前值 (带边界钳位)
void ui_stepper_set_val(ui_stepper_t *sp, int32_t val);

// 设置前缀标签
void ui_stepper_set_prefix(ui_stepper_t *sp, const char *prefix);

// 触控交互驱动 (支持单击步进、长按动态加速连发、8px 防抖死区)
bool ui_stepper_process_touch(ui_stepper_t *sp, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick);

// 切片光栅化渲染 (三段式独立切片相交裁剪)
void ui_stepper_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_stepper_t *sp);

// 获取局部脏矩形包围盒
static inline ui_rect_t ui_stepper_get_dirty_rect(const ui_stepper_t *sp) {
    return sp ? sp->rect : ui_rect_make(0, 0, 0, 0);
}

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

#endif // UI_STEPPER_H
ui_switch.c.c 130 行 · 4751 B
点击展开
#include "ui_switch.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <stdlib.h>

#define TOUCH_SLOP_PX   8 // 8 像素滑动防抖死区

/* ================= 1. 内部辅助函数 ================= */

static inline bool ui_switch_hit_test(const ui_switch_t *sw, int16_t x, int16_t ty) {
    // 扩展 4px 触控容差，增强微型开关的触控命中率
    return (x >= (sw->rect.x - 4) && x < (sw->rect.x + sw->rect.w + 4) &&
            ty >= (sw->rect.y - 4) && ty < (sw->rect.y + sw->rect.h + 4));
}

/* ================= 2. 初始化与配置 ================= */

void ui_switch_init(ui_switch_t *sw, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h,
                    bool init_state, ui_switch_cb_t on_change) {
    if (!sw) return;

    sw->rect = ui_rect_make(x, y, w, h);
    sw->is_checked = init_state;

    sw->down_x        = 0;
    sw->down_y        = 0;
    sw->is_touch_down = false;
    sw->is_dragged    = false;

    sw->color_off  = 0x39E7; // 默认深灰关闭轨
    sw->color_on   = 0x07E0; // 默认亮绿开启轨
    sw->color_knob = 0xFFFF; // 默认纯白手柄

    sw->on_change  = on_change;
    sw->user_data  = NULL;
}

void ui_switch_set_state(ui_switch_t *sw, bool state) {
    if (!sw) return;
    if (sw->is_checked != state) {
        sw->is_checked = state;
        if (sw->on_change) {
            sw->on_change(sw, sw->is_checked, sw->user_data);
        }
    }
}

void ui_switch_set_color(ui_switch_t *sw, uint16_t off_color, uint16_t on_color, uint16_t knob_color) {
    if (!sw) return;
    sw->color_off  = off_color;
    sw->color_on   = on_color;
    sw->color_knob = knob_color;
}

/* ================= 3. 触控状态机 (8px 防抖 + Touch-Up 触发) ================= */

bool ui_switch_process_touch(ui_switch_t *sw, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    (void)now_tick;
    if (!sw) return false;

    if (is_touching) {
        if (!sw->is_touch_down) {
            // A. 初次按下 (Touch Down): 仅在命中本开关时记录起点
            if (ui_switch_hit_test(sw, tx, ty)) {
                sw->is_touch_down = true;
                sw->is_dragged    = false;
                sw->down_x        = tx;
                sw->down_y        = ty;
            }
        } else {
            // B. 持续移动 (Touch Move): 8px 防抖死区判定
            int16_t dx = abs(tx - sw->down_x);
            int16_t dy = abs(ty - sw->down_y);
            if (!sw->is_dragged && (dx >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX || dy >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX)) {
                sw->is_dragged = true; // 锁定为滑动，放弃翻转开关
            }
        }
        return false;
    } else {
        // C. ⚡ 手指松开 (Touch Up): 唯一允许翻转开关状态的时机
        if (sw->is_touch_down) {
            sw->is_touch_down = false;

            // 只有未发生滑动的纯点按，且松开点仍在开关附近才触发翻转
            if (!sw->is_dragged && ui_switch_hit_test(sw, tx, ty)) {
                sw->is_checked = !sw->is_checked;
                if (sw->on_change) {
                    sw->on_change(sw, sw->is_checked, sw->user_data);
                }
                sw->is_dragged = false;
                return true; // 状态改变，触发局部切片重绘
            }
            sw->is_dragged = false;
        }
    }

    return false;
}

/* ================= 4. 切片光栅化渲染 ================= */

void ui_switch_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_switch_t *sw) {
    if (!tile || !sw) return;

    // 1. 切片相交快速裁剪 (Early-Culling)
    ui_rect_t dirty_bound = ui_switch_get_dirty_rect(sw);
    ui_rect_t overlap;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &dirty_bound, &overlap)) {
        return;
    }

    int16_t r = sw->rect.h / 2;
    int16_t left_cx  = sw->rect.x + r;
    int16_t right_cx = sw->rect.x + sw->rect.w - r;
    int16_t cy       = sw->rect.y + r;

    // 2. 绘制胶囊形底轨 (两侧半圆 + 中间矩形连接段)
    uint16_t track_color = sw->is_checked ? sw->color_on : sw->color_off;
    ui_draw_fill_circle(tile, left_cx, cy, r, track_color);
    ui_draw_fill_circle(tile, right_cx, cy, r, track_color);

    if (sw->rect.w > sw->rect.h) {
        ui_rect_t mid_rect = ui_rect_make(left_cx, sw->rect.y, (int16_t)(sw->rect.w - 2 * r), sw->rect.h);
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &mid_rect, track_color);
    }

    // 3. 绘制圆形滑块手柄 (内缩 2px 呈现立体感，开启居右，关闭居左)
    int16_t knob_r = (r > 2) ? (r - 2) : r;
    int16_t knob_cx = sw->is_checked ? right_cx : left_cx;

    ui_draw_fill_circle(tile, knob_cx, cy, knob_r, sw->color_knob);
}

#ifndef UI_SWITCH_H
#define UI_SWITCH_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include "ui_geometry.h"
#include "ui_tile.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

typedef struct ui_switch ui_switch_t;

// 开关状态变更回调函数类型[cite: 28]
typedef void (*ui_switch_cb_t)(ui_switch_t *sw, bool state, void *user_data);

struct ui_switch {
    ui_rect_t       rect;           // 控件物理包围盒[cite: 28]
    bool            is_checked;     // 开关状态 (true: 开, false: 关)[cite: 28]

    uint16_t        color_off;      // 关闭状态底轨颜色 (RGB565)[cite: 28]
    uint16_t        color_on;       // 开启状态底轨颜色 (RGB565)[cite: 28]
    uint16_t        color_knob;     // 圆形滑块手柄颜色 (RGB565)[cite: 28]

    // ⚡ 触控手势防抖与防误触状态机
    int16_t         down_x;
    int16_t         down_y;
    bool            is_touch_down;  // 是否在开关范围内按下
    bool            is_dragged;     // 是否已识别为滑动手势 (位移 > 8px)

    ui_switch_cb_t  on_change;      // 状态变更回调[cite: 28]
    void           *user_data;      // 用户私有指针[cite: 28]
};

// 初始化开关[cite: 28]
void ui_switch_init(ui_switch_t *sw, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h,
                    bool init_state, ui_switch_cb_t on_change);

// 设置开关状态 (自动触发回调)[cite: 28]
void ui_switch_set_state(ui_switch_t *sw, bool state);

// 设置开关全套配色[cite: 28]
void ui_switch_set_color(ui_switch_t *sw, uint16_t off_color, uint16_t on_color, uint16_t knob_color);

// 触控驱动状态机 (集成 8px 死区保护与 Touch-Up 松手触发)[cite: 28]
bool ui_switch_process_touch(ui_switch_t *sw, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick);

// 切片光栅化渲染 (胶囊体底轨 + 边缘抗切边保护)[cite: 28]
void ui_switch_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_switch_t *sw);

// 获取开关脏矩形包围盒 (用于 ui_render_dirty_clipped 局部极速推流)
static inline ui_rect_t ui_switch_get_dirty_rect(const ui_switch_t *sw) {
    if (!sw) return ui_rect_make(0, 0, 0, 0);
    return ui_rect_make(
        (int16_t)(sw->rect.x - 2),
        (int16_t)(sw->rect.y - 2),
        (int16_t)(sw->rect.w + 4),
        (int16_t)(sw->rect.h + 4)
    );
}

ui_tabview.c.c 239 行 · 9347 B
点击展开
#include "ui_tabview.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#define TOUCH_SLOP_PX   8 // 8 像素滑动防抖死区

static int16_t tab_measure_str_width(const char *str, FontSize_t size) {
    if (!str) return 0;
    uint8_t font_h = (size >= FONT_24) ? 24 : ((size >= FONT_20) ? 20 : 16);
    uint8_t half_w = font_h / 2;
    int16_t width = 0;

    while (*str) {
        if ((uint8_t)*str >= 0x80) {
            if (*(str + 1) == '\0') break;
            width += font_h;
            str += 2;
        } else {
            width += half_w;
            str += 1;
        }
    }
    return width;
}

void ui_tabview_init(ui_tabview_t *tv, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h, 
                     int16_t bar_w, int16_t item_h,
                     const char **tab_names, uint8_t tab_count, uint8_t default_idx,
                     ui_tabview_cb_t on_change) {
    if (!tv) return;

    tv->rect         = ui_rect_make(x, y, w, h);
    tv->bar_rect     = ui_rect_make(x, y, bar_w, h);
    tv->content_rect = ui_rect_make((int16_t)(x + bar_w), y, (int16_t)(w - bar_w), h);

    tv->item_h    = (item_h > 24) ? item_h : 44;
    tv->tab_count = (tab_count > UI_TABVIEW_MAX_TABS) ? UI_TABVIEW_MAX_TABS : tab_count;
    tv->content_h = tv->tab_count * tv->item_h;
    tv->scroll_y  = 0;

    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < tv->tab_count; i++) {
        tv->tab_names[i] = tab_names ? tab_names[i] : "";
    }

    tv->active_tab_idx = (default_idx < tv->tab_count) ? default_idx : 0;
    tv->font_size      = FONT_16;

    tv->down_x         = 0;
    tv->down_y         = 0;
    tv->scroll_start_y = 0;
    tv->is_touch_down  = false;
    tv->is_dragged     = false;
    tv->is_scrolling   = false;

    tv->color_bar_bg        = 0x10A2; // 极深蓝黑底
    tv->color_tab_active_bg = 0x2126; // 选中暗灰蓝底
    tv->color_text          = 0x8410; // 未选中文字暗灰
    tv->color_active_text   = 0xFFFF; // 选中文字纯白
    tv->color_indicator     = 0x07FF; // 青蓝高亮指示条
    tv->color_border        = 0x39E7; // 分割线深灰色
    tv->color_scrollbar     = 0x07FF; // 青蓝指示滑块

    tv->on_change           = on_change;
    tv->user_data           = NULL;
}

void ui_tabview_set_active(ui_tabview_t *tv, uint8_t tab_idx) {
    if (!tv || tab_idx >= tv->tab_count || tv->active_tab_idx == tab_idx) return;
    uint8_t old_idx = tv->active_tab_idx;
    tv->active_tab_idx = tab_idx;
    if (tv->on_change) {
        tv->on_change(tv, old_idx, tab_idx, tv->user_data);
    }
}

/* ================= 3. 触控状态机 (Touch Slop + Touch-Up 触发) ================= */

bool ui_tabview_process_touch(ui_tabview_t *tv, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    (void)now_tick;
    if (!tv || tv->tab_count == 0) return false;

    int16_t max_scroll = tv->content_h - tv->bar_rect.h;
    if (max_scroll < 0) max_scroll = 0;

    bool need_redraw = false;

    if (is_touching) {
        if (!tv->is_touch_down) {
            // A. 初次按下 (Touch Down): 必须命中左侧侧边栏视口
            if (ui_rect_contains_point(&tv->bar_rect, tx, ty)) {
                tv->is_touch_down  = true;
                tv->is_dragged     = false;
                tv->is_scrolling   = false;
                tv->down_x         = tx;
                tv->down_y         = ty;
                tv->scroll_start_y = tv->scroll_y;
            }
        } else {
            // B. 持续移动 (Touch Move): 8px 防抖死区与 Touch Capture 全局追踪
            int16_t dy = ty - tv->down_y;
            int16_t dx = tx - tv->down_x;

            if (!tv->is_dragged && (abs(dy) >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX || abs(dx) >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX)) {
                tv->is_dragged   = true;
                tv->is_scrolling = true;
            }

            if (tv->is_dragged && max_scroll > 0) {
                int16_t new_scroll = tv->scroll_start_y - dy;
                if (new_scroll < 0) new_scroll = 0;
                if (new_scroll > max_scroll) new_scroll = max_scroll;

                if (tv->scroll_y != new_scroll) {
                    tv->scroll_y = new_scroll;
                    need_redraw  = true;
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        // C. ⚡ 手指松开 (Touch Up): 唯一允许触发 Tab 切换的时机
        if (tv->is_touch_down) {
            tv->is_touch_down = false;

            // 只有未发生滑动的纯点按，且松手点在侧边栏内才判定切换
            if (!tv->is_dragged && ui_rect_contains_point(&tv->bar_rect, tx, ty)) {
                int16_t rel_y = ty - (tv->bar_rect.y - tv->scroll_y);
                if (rel_y >= 0 && rel_y < tv->content_h) {
                    uint8_t clicked_tab = (uint8_t)(rel_y / tv->item_h);
                    if (clicked_tab < tv->tab_count && clicked_tab != tv->active_tab_idx) {
                        uint8_t old_tab = tv->active_tab_idx;
                        tv->active_tab_idx = clicked_tab;

                        if (tv->on_change) {
                            tv->on_change(tv, old_tab, clicked_tab, tv->user_data);
                        }
                        need_redraw = true;
                    }
                }
            }

            tv->is_dragged   = false;
            tv->is_scrolling = false;
        }
    }

    return need_redraw;
}

/* ================= 4. 切片光栅化渲染 (O(1) 索引剪枝) ================= */

void ui_tabview_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_tabview_t *tv) {
    if (!tile || !tv || tv->tab_count == 0) return;

    ui_rect_t overlap;
    // 1. 切片相交快速裁剪 (Early Exit)
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &tv->bar_rect, &overlap)) {
        return;
    }

    // 2. 擦除侧边栏背景
    ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &overlap, tv->color_bar_bg);

    // 3. 启用像素级局部裁剪保护
    ui_draw_set_clip(&tv->bar_rect);

    int16_t base_y = tv->bar_rect.y - tv->scroll_y;
    uint8_t font_h = (tv->font_size >= FONT_24) ? 24 : ((tv->font_size >= FONT_20) ? 20 : 16);

    // ⚡ 核心优化：仅计算与当前切片相交的 Tab 索引范围 (从 O(N) 降至 O(1))
    int16_t first_idx = (tile->rect.y - base_y) / tv->item_h;
    int16_t last_idx  = (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h - 1 - base_y) / tv->item_h;

    if (first_idx < 0) first_idx = 0;
    if (last_idx >= tv->tab_count) last_idx = (int16_t)tv->tab_count - 1;

    for (int16_t i = first_idx; i <= last_idx; i++) {
        int16_t item_y = base_y + i * tv->item_h;
        ui_rect_t item_rect = ui_rect_make(tv->bar_rect.x, item_y, tv->bar_rect.w, tv->item_h);

        ui_rect_t item_overlap;
        if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &item_rect, &item_overlap)) {
            continue;
        }

        bool is_active = (i == tv->active_tab_idx);

        if (is_active) {
            ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &item_rect, tv->color_tab_active_bg);

            // 选中项左侧 4px 高亮指示条
            ui_rect_t ind_rect = ui_rect_make(tv->bar_rect.x, item_y + 4, 4, tv->item_h - 8);
            ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &ind_rect, tv->color_indicator);
        }

        // 居中绘制标题文字
        const char *name = tv->tab_names[i];
        if (name && name[0] != '\0') {
            int16_t text_y = item_y + (tv->item_h - font_h) / 2;

            if ((text_y + font_h) > tile->rect.y && text_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
                int16_t str_w = tab_measure_str_width(name, tv->font_size);
                int16_t text_x = tv->bar_rect.x + (tv->bar_rect.w - str_w) / 2 + (is_active ? 2 : 0);
                uint16_t text_col = is_active ? tv->color_active_text : tv->color_text;
                uint16_t bg_col   = is_active ? tv->color_tab_active_bg : tv->color_bar_bg;

                ui_draw_string(tile, text_x, text_y, name, tv->font_size, text_col, bg_col);
            }
        }

        // 行底部分割线
        ui_draw_fast_hline(tile, tv->bar_rect.x + 4, item_y + tv->item_h - 1, tv->bar_rect.w - 8, tv->color_border);
    }

    // 4. 超出视口时绘制微型滚动条
    if (tv->content_h > tv->bar_rect.h) {
        int16_t bar_w = 2;
        int16_t track_h = tv->bar_rect.h - 4;
        int16_t thumb_h = (int16_t)(((int32_t)tv->bar_rect.h * track_h) / tv->content_h);
        if (thumb_h < 12) thumb_h = 12;

        int16_t max_scroll = tv->content_h - tv->bar_rect.h;
        int16_t thumb_y = tv->bar_rect.y + 2;
        if (max_scroll > 0) {
            thumb_y += (int16_t)(((int32_t)tv->scroll_y * (track_h - thumb_h)) / max_scroll);
        }
        int16_t thumb_x = tv->bar_rect.x + tv->bar_rect.w - bar_w - 2;

        ui_rect_t thumb_rect = ui_rect_make(thumb_x, thumb_y, bar_w, thumb_h);
        ui_rect_t thumb_overlap;
        if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &thumb_rect, &thumb_overlap)) {
            ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &thumb_overlap, tv->color_scrollbar);
        }
    }

    ui_draw_reset_clip();

    // 5. 绘制右侧纵向主分割线
    ui_draw_fast_vline(tile, tv->bar_rect.x + tv->bar_rect.w - 1, tv->bar_rect.y, tv->bar_rect.h, tv->color_border);
}
ui_tabview.h.h 88 行 · 3670 B

/*
 * @Author: 轩
 * @Date: 2026-08-20 12:59:16
 * @LastEditTime: 2026-08-21 12:30:37
 * @FilePath: \pico\ui\ui_tabview.h
 */
#ifndef UI_TABVIEW_H
#define UI_TABVIEW_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include "ui_geometry.h"
#include "ui_tile.h"
#include "chinese.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

// 支持最多 12 个标签页[cite: 30]
#define UI_TABVIEW_MAX_TABS 12

typedef struct ui_tabview ui_tabview_t;

// 标签切换回调函数类型[cite: 30]
typedef void (*ui_tabview_cb_t)(ui_tabview_t *tabview, uint8_t old_tab, uint8_t new_tab, void *user_data);

struct ui_tabview {
    ui_rect_t       rect;           // 整体包围盒[cite: 30]
    ui_rect_t       bar_rect;       // 左侧侧边栏物理视口[cite: 30]
    ui_rect_t       content_rect;   // 右侧工作区视口[cite: 30]

    const char     *tab_names[UI_TABVIEW_MAX_TABS]; // 各标签标题[cite: 30]
    uint8_t         tab_count;      // 标签总数[cite: 30]
    uint8_t         active_tab_idx; // 当前选中的标签索引[cite: 30]

    int16_t         item_h;         // 单个标签项高度 (推荐 42~46px)[cite: 30]
    int16_t         scroll_y;       // 侧边栏当前滚动偏移[cite: 30]
    int16_t         content_h;      // 侧边栏内容总高度 (tab_count * item_h)[cite: 30]

    // ⚡ 触控手势防抖与全局捕获状态机
    int16_t         down_x;
    int16_t         down_y;
    int16_t         scroll_start_y; // 按下时的初始滚动偏移[cite: 30]
    bool            is_touch_down;  // 是否在侧边栏内按下
    bool            is_dragged;     // 是否已识别为滑动手势 (位移 > 8px)
    bool            is_scrolling;   // 是否处于滚动中[cite: 30]

    FontSize_t      font_size;      // 字体大小 (默认 FONT_16)[cite: 30]

    // 配色方案 (RGB565)[cite: 30]
    uint16_t        color_bar_bg;       // 侧边栏底色[cite: 30]
    uint16_t        color_tab_active_bg;// 选中项背景底色[cite: 30]
    uint16_t        color_text;         // 未选中文字颜色[cite: 30]
    uint16_t        color_active_text;  // 选中文字颜色[cite: 30]
    uint16_t        color_indicator;    // 选中项左侧高亮条颜色[cite: 30]
    uint16_t        color_border;       // 分割线颜色[cite: 30]
    uint16_t        color_scrollbar;    // 滚动条颜色[cite: 30]

    ui_tabview_cb_t on_change;          // 切换回调[cite: 30]
    void           *user_data;          // 用户私有数据[cite: 30]
};

// 初始化支持滚动的侧边栏 TabView[cite: 30]
void ui_tabview_init(ui_tabview_t *tv, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h, 
                     int16_t bar_w, int16_t item_h,
                     const char **tab_names, uint8_t tab_count, uint8_t default_idx,
                     ui_tabview_cb_t on_change);

// 设置活动选项卡 (自动触发切换回调)[cite: 30]
void ui_tabview_set_active(ui_tabview_t *tv, uint8_t tab_idx);

// 触控输入调度 (集成 8px 死区保护与 Touch Capture 全局追踪)[cite: 30]
bool ui_tabview_process_touch(ui_tabview_t *tv, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick);

// 切片光栅化渲染 (O(1) 条目索引剪枝 + 视口硬裁剪保护)[cite: 30]
void ui_tabview_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_tabview_t *tv);

// 获取侧边栏脏矩形 (用于 ui_render_dirty_clipped 局部极速推流)
static inline ui_rect_t ui_tabview_get_bar_dirty_rect(const ui_tabview_t *tv) {
    return tv ? tv->bar_rect : ui_rect_make(0, 0, 0, 0);
}

#include "ui_terminal.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdarg.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

#define TOUCH_SLOP_PX   8 // 8 像素滑动防抖死区

void ui_terminal_init(ui_terminal_t *term, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h) {
    if (!term) return;

    term->rect = ui_rect_make(x, y, w, h);
    term->mode = UI_TERM_MODE_ASCII;

    term->head  = 0;
    term->count = 0;
    memset(term->lines, 0, sizeof(term->lines));

    term->line_height = 18;
    term->scroll_y    = 0;
    term->auto_scroll = true;

    term->down_x         = 0;
    term->down_y         = 0;
    term->scroll_start_y = 0;
    term->is_touch_down  = false;
    term->is_dragged     = false;
    term->is_scrolling   = false;

    term->color_bg        = 0x0842; // 极深灰黑底
    term->color_border    = 0x39E7; // 分割边框
    term->color_text_def  = 0xFFFF; // 默认白字
    term->color_scrollbar = 0x07FF; // 青蓝滚动指示条
}

static void push_single_line(ui_terminal_t *term, const char *str, uint16_t color) {
    if (!term || !str) return;

    strncpy(term->lines[term->head], str, UI_TERM_LINE_LEN - 1);
    term->lines[term->head][UI_TERM_LINE_LEN - 1] = '\0';
    term->line_colors[term->head] = color;

    term->head = (term->head + 1) % UI_TERM_MAX_LINES;
    if (term->count < UI_TERM_MAX_LINES) {
        term->count++;
    }

    // 自动跟随吸附到底端
    if (term->auto_scroll) {
        int16_t total_h = term->count * term->line_height;
        int16_t max_scroll = total_h - (term->rect.h - 8);
        term->scroll_y = (max_scroll > 0) ? max_scroll : 0;
    }
}

void ui_terminal_write_line(ui_terminal_t *term, const char *str, uint16_t color) {
    if (!term || !str) return;

    char buf[UI_TERM_LINE_LEN];
    uint16_t b_idx = 0;

    while (*str) {
        if (*str == '\r') {
            str++;
            continue;
        }
        if (*str == '\n') {
            buf[b_idx] = '\0';
            push_single_line(term, buf, color);
            b_idx = 0;
            str++;
            continue;
        }

        buf[b_idx++] = *str++;
        if (b_idx >= UI_TERM_LINE_LEN - 1) {
            buf[b_idx] = '\0';
            push_single_line(term, buf, color);
            b_idx = 0;
        }
    }

    if (b_idx > 0) {
        buf[b_idx] = '\0';
        push_single_line(term, buf, color);
    }
}

void ui_terminal_printf(ui_terminal_t *term, uint16_t color, const char *fmt, ...) {
    if (!term || !fmt) return;
    char temp[128];
    va_list args;
    va_start(args, fmt);
    vsnprintf(temp, sizeof(temp), fmt, args);
    va_end(args);
    ui_terminal_write_line(term, temp, color);
}

void ui_terminal_write_hex(ui_terminal_t *term, const uint8_t *data, size_t len, uint16_t color) {
    if (!term || !data || len == 0) return;

    char line_buf[UI_TERM_LINE_LEN];
    for (size_t i = 0; i < len; i += 8) {
        int offset = snprintf(line_buf, sizeof(line_buf), "[%04X] ", (unsigned int)i);
        for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
            if (i + j < len) {
                offset += snprintf(line_buf + offset, sizeof(line_buf) - offset, "%02X ", data[i + j]);
            } else {
                offset += snprintf(line_buf + offset, sizeof(line_buf) - offset, "   ");
            }
        }
        push_single_line(term, line_buf, color);
    }
}

void ui_terminal_clear(ui_terminal_t *term) {
    if (!term) return;
    term->head        = 0;
    term->count       = 0;
    term->scroll_y    = 0;
    term->auto_scroll = true;
}

void ui_terminal_set_autoscroll(ui_terminal_t *term, bool enable) {
    if (term) term->auto_scroll = enable;
}

void ui_terminal_set_mode(ui_terminal_t *term, ui_terminal_mode_t mode) {
    if (term) term->mode = mode;
}

/* ================= 3. 触控状态机 (Touch Slop + Touch Capture) ================= */

bool ui_terminal_process_touch(ui_terminal_t *term, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    (void)now_tick;
    if (!term || term->count == 0) return false;

    int16_t total_h = term->count * term->line_height;
    int16_t max_scroll = total_h - (term->rect.h - 8);
    if (max_scroll < 0) max_scroll = 0;

    bool need_redraw = false;

    if (is_touching) {
        if (!term->is_touch_down) {
            // A. 初次按下 (Touch Down): 命中视口
            if (ui_rect_contains_point(&term->rect, tx, ty)) {
                term->is_touch_down  = true;
                term->is_dragged     = false;
                term->is_scrolling   = false;
                term->down_x         = tx;
                term->down_y         = ty;
                term->scroll_start_y = term->scroll_y;
            }
        } else {
            // B. 持续移动 (Touch Move): 8px 防抖死区与 Touch Capture 追踪
            int16_t dy = ty - term->down_y;
            int16_t dx = tx - term->down_x;

            if (!term->is_dragged && (abs(dy) >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX || abs(dx) >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX)) {
                term->is_dragged   = true;
                term->is_scrolling = true;
                term->auto_scroll  = false; // 向上拖动查看历史时暂停吸底
            }

            if (term->is_dragged && max_scroll > 0) {
                int16_t new_scroll = term->scroll_start_y - dy;
                if (new_scroll < 0) new_scroll = 0;
                if (new_scroll > max_scroll) new_scroll = max_scroll;

                if (term->scroll_y != new_scroll) {
                    term->scroll_y = new_scroll;
                    term->auto_scroll = (term->scroll_y >= max_scroll); // 滑回底部恢复吸底
                    need_redraw = true;
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        // C. 手指松开 (Touch Up): 释放手势锁
        if (term->is_touch_down) {
            term->is_touch_down = false;
            term->is_dragged    = false;
            if (term->is_scrolling) {
                term->is_scrolling = false;
                need_redraw = true;
            }
        }
    }

    return need_redraw;
}

/* ================= 4. 切片光栅化渲染 (O(1) 索引剪枝) ================= */

void ui_terminal_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_terminal_t *term) {
    if (!tile || !term) return;

    ui_rect_t overlap;
    // 1. 切片相交快速裁剪
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &term->rect, &overlap)) {
        return;
    }

    // 2. 擦除背景与绘制边框
    ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &overlap, term->color_bg);
    ui_draw_rect(tile, &term->rect, term->color_border);

    // 3. 锁定像素级视口裁剪保护
    ui_draw_set_clip(&term->rect);

    int16_t base_y = term->rect.y + 4 - term->scroll_y;
    int16_t text_x = term->rect.x + 6;

    // ⚡ 核心优化：仅计算与当前切片相交的行索引范围 (从 O(N) 降至 O(1))
    int16_t first_idx = (tile->rect.y - base_y) / term->line_height;
    int16_t last_idx  = (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h - 1 - base_y) / term->line_height;

    if (first_idx < 0) first_idx = 0;
    if (last_idx >= (int16_t)term->count) last_idx = (int16_t)term->count - 1;

    uint16_t start_idx = (term->count < UI_TERM_MAX_LINES) ? 0 : term->head;

    for (int16_t i = first_idx; i <= last_idx; i++) {
        int16_t line_y = base_y + i * term->line_height;

        if ((line_y + 16) > tile->rect.y && line_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
            uint16_t ring_idx = (start_idx + i) % UI_TERM_MAX_LINES;
            const char *line_str = term->lines[ring_idx];
            uint16_t color = term->line_colors[ring_idx];

            ui_draw_string(tile, text_x, line_y, line_str, FONT_16, color, term->color_bg);
        }
    }

    // 4. 绘制右侧微型滚动条
    int16_t total_h = term->count * term->line_height;
    if (total_h > (term->rect.h - 8)) {
        int16_t bar_w = 2;
        int16_t track_h = term->rect.h - 8;
        int16_t thumb_h = (int16_t)(((int32_t)(term->rect.h - 8) * track_h) / total_h);
        if (thumb_h < 10) thumb_h = 10;

        int16_t max_scroll = total_h - (term->rect.h - 8);
        int16_t thumb_y = term->rect.y + 4;
        if (max_scroll > 0) {
            thumb_y += (int16_t)(((int32_t)term->scroll_y * (track_h - thumb_h)) / max_scroll);
        }
        int16_t thumb_x = term->rect.x + term->rect.w - bar_w - 3;

        ui_rect_t thumb_rect = ui_rect_make(thumb_x, thumb_y, bar_w, thumb_h);
        ui_rect_t thumb_overlap;
        if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &thumb_rect, &thumb_overlap)) {
            ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &thumb_overlap, term->color_scrollbar);
        }
    }

    ui_draw_reset_clip();
}

#ifndef UI_TERMINAL_H
#define UI_TERMINAL_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include "ui_geometry.h"
#include "ui_tile.h"
#include "chinese.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

#define UI_TERM_MAX_LINES    64   // 环形缓冲最大行数
#define UI_TERM_LINE_LEN     64   // 单行最大字符数

typedef enum {
    UI_TERM_MODE_ASCII = 0,       // 标准 ASCII 文本模式
    UI_TERM_MODE_HEX              // 16 进制 HEX 分栏模式
} ui_terminal_mode_t;

typedef struct ui_terminal ui_terminal_t;

struct ui_terminal {
    ui_rect_t           rect;           // 控件视口物理包围盒
    ui_terminal_mode_t  mode;           // 运行模式

    // 环形行缓冲区 (静态 4KB 内存)
    char                lines[UI_TERM_MAX_LINES][UI_TERM_LINE_LEN];
    uint16_t            line_colors[UI_TERM_MAX_LINES];
    uint16_t            head;           // 写入头指针
    uint16_t            count;          // 当前有效总行数

    // 滚动与自动跟滚机制
    int16_t             line_height;    // 单行像素高度 (默认 18px)
    int16_t             scroll_y;       // 当前滚动偏移
    bool                auto_scroll;    // 新数据写入时是否自动吸附到底部

    // ⚡ 触控手势防抖与全局捕获状态机
    int16_t             down_x;
    int16_t             down_y;
    int16_t             scroll_start_y; // 按下时的初始滚动偏移
    bool                is_touch_down;  // 是否在终端范围内按下
    bool                is_dragged;     // 是否已识别为滑动手势 (位移 > 8px)
    bool                is_scrolling;   // 是否处于拖拽滚动中

    // 配色方案 (RGB565)
    uint16_t            color_bg;       // 控制台底色 (极深黑蓝)
    uint16_t            color_border;   // 边框色
    uint16_t            color_text_def; // 默认文本色
    uint16_t            color_scrollbar;// 滚动条颜色
};

// 初始化终端控件 (默认每行高 18px，配合 FONT_16 点阵)
void ui_terminal_init(ui_terminal_t *term, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h);

// 写入单行文本 (支持自动按 \r\n 拆行与颜色指定)
void ui_terminal_write_line(ui_terminal_t *term, const char *str, uint16_t color);

// 格式化输出 (类似 printf)
void ui_terminal_printf(ui_terminal_t *term, uint16_t color, const char *fmt, ...);

// 写入二进制 Hex Dump 数据流 (自动按每 8 字节分行排版)[cite: 31, 32]
void ui_terminal_write_hex(ui_terminal_t *term, const uint8_t *data, size_t len, uint16_t color);

// 清空控制台历史缓冲区
void ui_terminal_clear(ui_terminal_t *term);

// 切换自动跟滚与查看模式
void ui_terminal_set_autoscroll(ui_terminal_t *term, bool enable);
void ui_terminal_set_mode(ui_terminal_t *term, ui_terminal_mode_t mode);

// 触控交互调度 (集成 8px 死区保护与 Touch Capture 全局追踪)
bool ui_terminal_process_touch(ui_terminal_t *term, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick);

// 切片光栅化渲染 (O(1) 算术行剪枝 + 视口硬裁剪保护)
void ui_terminal_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_terminal_t *term);

// 获取终端脏矩形包围盒 (用于 ui_render_dirty_clipped 局部极速推流)
static inline ui_rect_t ui_terminal_get_dirty_rect(const ui_terminal_t *term) {
    return term ? term->rect : ui_rect_make(0, 0, 0, 0);
}

}
#endif

#endif // UI_TERMINAL_H

#include "ui_tile.h"
#include "lcd.h"
#include <string.h>

static uint16_t  s_tile_bufs[2][UI_TILE_W * UI_TILE_H];
static uint8_t   s_buf_idx = 0;
static ui_tile_t s_current_tile;

void ui_tile_init(void)
{
    s_buf_idx = 0;
    memset(s_tile_bufs, 0, sizeof(s_tile_bufs));
}

ui_tile_t *ui_tile_get_next(uint8_t row_idx)
{
    s_current_tile.buf  = s_tile_bufs[s_buf_idx];
    s_current_tile.rect = ui_rect_make(0, (int16_t)(row_idx * UI_TILE_H), UI_TILE_W, UI_TILE_H);
    
    // 强制清零当前切片显存，消除上一帧残像
    memset(s_current_tile.buf, 0, UI_TILE_W * UI_TILE_H * sizeof(uint16_t));
    
    return &s_current_tile;
}

void ui_tile_flush(ui_tile_t *tile)
{
    // 必须确保上一包 DMA 发送彻底完成[cite: 4]
    LCD_WaitDMA();

    // 启动当前切片异步推流[cite: 4]
    LCD_PushTileDMA_Async(tile->rect.x, tile->rect.y, tile->rect.w, tile->rect.h, tile->buf);

    // 切换乒乓缓冲区[cite: 4]
    s_buf_idx ^= 1;
}

void ui_tile_wait_finish(void)
{
    LCD_WaitDMA();
}

#include "ui_toast.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>

#define TOAST_ANIM_MS 150 // 入场与出场动画耗时 150ms

void ui_toast_init(ui_toast_t *toast) {
    if (!toast) return;
    toast->is_active   = false;
    toast->message[0]  = '\0';
    toast->type        = UI_TOAST_INFO;
    toast->start_tick  = 0;
    toast->duration_ms = 2300; // 150ms滑入 + 2000ms驻留 + 150ms滑出

    toast->w = 240;
    toast->h = 34;
    // 屏幕右下角静止坐标 (屏幕 480x320, 距右边 8px, 距底边 10px)
    toast->target_x = 480 - toast->w - 8; // 232
    toast->target_y = 320 - toast->h - 10; // 276

    toast->rect     = ui_rect_make(480, toast->target_y, toast->w, toast->h);
    toast->old_rect = toast->rect;
}

void ui_toast_show(ui_toast_t *toast, const char *msg, ui_toast_type_t type, uint32_t now_tick) {
    if (!toast || !msg) return;
    toast->is_active  = true;
    toast->type       = type;
    toast->start_tick = now_tick;
    strncpy(toast->message, msg, sizeof(toast->message) - 1);
    toast->message[sizeof(toast->message) - 1] = '\0';

    // 初始位置设定在屏幕右侧外部
    toast->old_rect = toast->rect;
    toast->rect     = ui_rect_make(480, toast->target_y, toast->w, toast->h);
}

bool ui_toast_update(ui_toast_t *toast, uint32_t now_tick) {
    if (!toast || !toast->is_active) return false;

    uint32_t elapsed = now_tick - toast->start_tick;
    toast->old_rect  = toast->rect; // 备份上一帧位置
    int16_t cur_x    = toast->target_x;

    // 1. 超时关闭
    if (elapsed >= toast->duration_ms) {
        toast->is_active = false;
        toast->rect.x    = 480;
        return true; // 触发最后一次擦除重绘
    }

    // 2. ⚡ 入场阶段 (0 ~ 150ms: 纯定点整数平滑插值从 480 移动至 target_x)
    if (elapsed < TOAST_ANIM_MS) {
        int32_t total_dist = 480 - toast->target_x;
        cur_x = (int16_t)(480 - (total_dist * (int32_t)elapsed) / TOAST_ANIM_MS);
    }
    // 3. ⚡ 出场阶段 (倒数 150ms: 纯定点整数插值从 target_x 移动至 480)
    else if (elapsed >= (toast->duration_ms - TOAST_ANIM_MS)) {
        uint32_t out_elapsed = elapsed - (toast->duration_ms - TOAST_ANIM_MS);
        int32_t total_dist = 480 - toast->target_x;
        cur_x = (int16_t)(toast->target_x + (total_dist * (int32_t)out_elapsed) / TOAST_ANIM_MS);
    }
    // 4. 静止驻留阶段
    else {
        cur_x = toast->target_x;
    }

    toast->rect.x = cur_x;
    return (toast->rect.x != toast->old_rect.x);
}

void ui_toast_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_toast_t *toast) {
    if (!tile || !toast || !toast->is_active) return;

    ui_rect_t overlap;
    // 1. 切片相交快速裁剪
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &toast->rect, &overlap)) return;

    uint16_t bg_col   = 0x1082; // 极深蓝黑底
    uint16_t bdr_col  = 0x07FF; // 青蓝框
    uint16_t icon_col = 0x07FF;
    const char *tag   = "[INFO]";

    if (toast->type == UI_TOAST_SUCCESS) {
        bdr_col = 0x07E0; icon_col = 0x07E0; tag = "[OK]";
    } else if (toast->type == UI_TOAST_WARN) {
        bdr_col = 0xFFE0; icon_col = 0xFFE0; tag = "[WARN]";
    } else if (toast->type == UI_TOAST_ERROR) {
        bdr_col = 0xF800; icon_col = 0xF800; tag = "[ERR]";
    }

    // 2. 填充气泡背景与发光外框
    ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &overlap, bg_col);
    ui_draw_rect(tile, &toast->rect, bdr_col);

    // 3. 锁定视口像素级硬裁剪保护 (滑入时不溢出)
    ui_draw_set_clip(&toast->rect);

    // 左侧彩色指示条 (宽 4px)
    ui_rect_t left_bar = ui_rect_make((int16_t)(toast->rect.x + 1), (int16_t)(toast->rect.y + 1), 4, (int16_t)(toast->rect.h - 2));
    ui_rect_t bar_ov;
    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &left_bar, &bar_ov)) {
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &bar_ov, icon_col);
    }

    int16_t text_y = (int16_t)(toast->rect.y + 9);
    if ((text_y + 16) > tile->rect.y && text_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
        // 状态徽标
        ui_draw_string(tile, (int16_t)(toast->rect.x + 10), text_y, tag, FONT_16, icon_col, bg_col);

        // 提示正文文本
        ui_draw_string(tile, (int16_t)(toast->rect.x + 62), text_y, toast->message, FONT_16, 0xFFFF, bg_col);
    }

    ui_draw_reset_clip();
}

/*
 * @Author: 轩
 * @Date: 2026-08-20 16:24:08
 * @LastEditTime: 2026-08-21 12:38:18

#ifndef UI_TOAST_H
#define UI_TOAST_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include "ui_geometry.h"
#include "ui_tile.h"
#include "chinese.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

typedef enum {
    UI_TOAST_INFO = 0,    // 0: 蓝色普通信息[cite: 36]
    UI_TOAST_SUCCESS,     // 1: 绿色成功提示[cite: 36]
    UI_TOAST_WARN,        // 2: 黄色告警提示[cite: 36]
    UI_TOAST_ERROR        // 3: 红色错误提示[cite: 36]
} ui_toast_type_t;

typedef struct {
    bool            is_active;    // 激活状态[cite: 36]
    char            message[48];  // 提示文本[cite: 36]
    ui_toast_type_t type;         // 提示类型[cite: 36]

    uint32_t        start_tick;   // 弹出时刻时间戳[cite: 36]
    uint32_t        duration_ms;  // 驻留与动画总时长 (默认 2300ms)[cite: 36]

    int16_t         target_x;     // 静止停靠目标 X (默认 232)[cite: 36]
    int16_t         target_y;     // 静止停靠目标 Y (默认 276)[cite: 36]
    int16_t         w;            // 气泡宽度 (默认 240)[cite: 36]
    int16_t         h;            // 气泡高度 (默认 34)[cite: 36]

    ui_rect_t       rect;         // 当前帧计算出的实时物理包围盒[cite: 36]
    ui_rect_t       old_rect;     // 上一帧物理包围盒 (用于计算脏矩形并集)
} ui_toast_t;

// 初始化全局 Toast 管理器[cite: 36]
void ui_toast_init(ui_toast_t *toast);

// 触发右下角滑入气泡[cite: 36]
void ui_toast_show(ui_toast_t *toast, const char *msg, ui_toast_type_t type, uint32_t now_tick);

// 驱动动画与倒计时 (返回 true 表示正在平移或状态变更，需局部重绘切片)[cite: 36]
bool ui_toast_update(ui_toast_t *toast, uint32_t now_tick);

// 切片光栅化渲染 (带像素级视口裁剪保护)[cite: 36]
void ui_toast_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_toast_t *toast);

// 计算 Toast 动画帧涉及的完整脏矩形并集 (包含旧位置与新位置，彻底消除残影)
static inline ui_rect_t ui_toast_get_dirty_union(const ui_toast_t *toast) {
    if (!toast) return ui_rect_make(0, 0, 0, 0);
    ui_rect_t dirty;
    ui_rect_union(&toast->old_rect, &toast->rect, &dirty);
    return dirty;
}

#endif
