#include "ui_waveform.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>

#define TOUCH_SLOP_PX   8 // 8 像素滑动防抖死区

/* ================= 1. 默认通道配色表 ================= */
static const uint16_t k_default_ch_colors[UI_WAVE_MAX_CHANNELS] = {
    0x07E0, // CH0: 亮绿
    0x07FF, // CH1: 青蓝
    0xFFE0, // CH2: 亮黄
    0xFD20, // CH3: 橙色
    0xF81F, // CH4: 紫红
    0x3BFF, // CH5: 冰蓝
    0xFA60, // CH6: 暖橙
    0xFFFF  // CH7: 纯白
};

/* ================= 2. 初始化与绑定 ================= */

void ui_waveform_init(ui_waveform_t *wf, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h,
                      ui_waveform_mode_t mode, uint32_t sample_rate_hz) {
    if (!wf) return;

    wf->rect = ui_rect_make(x, y, w, h);
    wf->mode = mode;

    wf->digital_data        = NULL;
    wf->analog_data         = NULL;
    wf->total_samples       = 0;

    wf->view_offset         = 0;
    wf->samples_per_pixel   = 1;
    wf->active_channels     = 8;
    wf->sample_rate_hz      = (sample_rate_hz > 0) ? sample_rate_hz : 10000000; // 默认 10MHz

    wf->show_cursors        = true;
    wf->cursor_a_x          = x + w / 4;
    wf->cursor_b_x          = x + (w * 3) / 4;

    wf->drag_state          = UI_WAVE_DRAG_NONE;
    wf->down_x              = 0;
    wf->down_y              = 0;
    wf->touch_start_x       = 0;
    wf->view_start_offset   = 0;
    wf->is_touch_down       = false;
    wf->is_dragged          = false;

    wf->color_bg            = 0x0000; // 纯黑底
    wf->color_grid          = 0x2104; // 深暗灰网格线
    wf->color_cursor_a      = 0xFFE0; // 金黄游标 A
    wf->color_cursor_b      = 0xF81F; // 紫红游标 B

    for (int i = 0; i < UI_WAVE_MAX_CHANNELS; i++) {
        wf->color_channels[i] = k_default_ch_colors[i];
    }

    wf->on_change           = NULL;
    wf->user_data           = NULL;
}

void ui_waveform_bind_digital(ui_waveform_t *wf, const uint8_t *data, uint32_t total_samples) {
    if (!wf) return;
    wf->digital_data  = data;
    wf->total_samples = total_samples;
}

void ui_waveform_bind_analog(ui_waveform_t *wf, const int16_t *data, uint32_t total_samples) {
    if (!wf) return;
    wf->analog_data   = data;
    wf->total_samples = total_samples;
}

void ui_waveform_set_scale(ui_waveform_t *wf, uint16_t samples_per_pixel, uint8_t active_channels) {
    if (!wf) return;
    wf->samples_per_pixel = (samples_per_pixel > 0) ? samples_per_pixel : 1;
    wf->active_channels   = (active_channels > 0 && active_channels <= UI_WAVE_MAX_CHANNELS) ? active_channels : 8;
}

/* ================= 3. 触控状态机 (游标抓取 + 时间轴平移) ================= */

bool ui_waveform_process_touch(ui_waveform_t *wf, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    (void)now_tick;
    if (!wf) return false;

    bool need_redraw = false;

    if (is_touching) {
        if (!wf->is_touch_down) {
            // A. 初次按下 (Touch Down): 必须命中波形视口
            if (ui_rect_contains_point(&wf->rect, tx, ty)) {
                wf->is_touch_down     = true;
                wf->down_x            = tx;
                wf->down_y            = ty;
                wf->touch_start_x     = tx;
                wf->view_start_offset = wf->view_offset;

                // 优先判定是否命中测量游标 (±12 像素容差)
                if (wf->show_cursors && abs(tx - wf->cursor_a_x) <= 12) {
                    wf->drag_state = UI_WAVE_DRAG_CURSOR_A;
                    wf->is_dragged = true;
                } else if (wf->show_cursors && abs(tx - wf->cursor_b_x) <= 12) {
                    wf->drag_state = UI_WAVE_DRAG_CURSOR_B;
                    wf->is_dragged = true;
                } else {
                    wf->drag_state = UI_WAVE_DRAG_PAN;
                    wf->is_dragged = false;
                }
            }
        } else {
            // B. 持续移动 (Touch Move): 8px 防抖死区与平滑追踪
            int16_t delta_x = tx - wf->touch_start_x;

            if (wf->drag_state == UI_WAVE_DRAG_CURSOR_A) {
                int16_t nx = tx;
                if (nx < wf->rect.x) nx = wf->rect.x;
                if (nx >= wf->rect.x + wf->rect.w) nx = (int16_t)(wf->rect.x + wf->rect.w - 1);
                if (wf->cursor_a_x != nx) {
                    wf->cursor_a_x = nx;
                    need_redraw = true;
                }
            } else if (wf->drag_state == UI_WAVE_DRAG_CURSOR_B) {
                int16_t nx = tx;
                if (nx < wf->rect.x) nx = wf->rect.x;
                if (nx >= wf->rect.x + wf->rect.w) nx = (int16_t)(wf->rect.x + wf->rect.w - 1);
                if (wf->cursor_b_x != nx) {
                    wf->cursor_b_x = nx;
                    need_redraw = true;
                }
            } else if (wf->drag_state == UI_WAVE_DRAG_PAN) {
                if (!wf->is_dragged && (abs(tx - wf->down_x) >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX || abs(ty - wf->down_y) >= TOUCH_SLOP_PX)) {
                    wf->is_dragged = true;
                }

                if (wf->is_dragged) {
                    // 水平平移波形时间轴
                    int32_t shift_samples = (int32_t)delta_x * wf->samples_per_pixel;
                    int32_t new_offset = (int32_t)wf->view_start_offset - shift_samples;
                    if (new_offset < 0) new_offset = 0;

                    int32_t max_offset = (int32_t)wf->total_samples - ((int32_t)wf->rect.w * wf->samples_per_pixel);
                    if (max_offset < 0) max_offset = 0;
                    if (new_offset > max_offset) new_offset = max_offset;

                    if (wf->view_offset != (uint32_t)new_offset) {
                        wf->view_offset = (uint32_t)new_offset;
                        need_redraw = true;
                    }
                }
            }

            if (need_redraw && wf->on_change) {
                wf->on_change(wf, wf->user_data);
            }
        }
        return need_redraw;
    } else {
        // C. 手指松开 (Touch Up)
        if (wf->is_touch_down) {
            wf->is_touch_down = false;
            wf->is_dragged    = false;
            if (wf->drag_state != UI_WAVE_DRAG_NONE) {
                wf->drag_state = UI_WAVE_DRAG_NONE;
                return true;
            }
        }
    }

    return false;
}

/* ================= 4. 切片光栅化渲染核心 ================= */

static void format_time_delta(char *buf, size_t buf_size, uint32_t delta_samples, uint32_t sample_rate) {
    if (sample_rate == 0) sample_rate = 1;
    double dt_sec = (double)delta_samples / (double)sample_rate;

    if (dt_sec < 1e-6) {
        snprintf(buf, buf_size, "dT:%.1fns", dt_sec * 1e9);
    } else if (dt_sec < 1e-3) {
        snprintf(buf, buf_size, "dT:%.2fus", dt_sec * 1e6);
    } else if (dt_sec < 1.0) {
        snprintf(buf, buf_size, "dT:%.2fms", dt_sec * 1e3);
    } else {
        snprintf(buf, buf_size, "dT:%.2fs", dt_sec);
    }
}

void ui_waveform_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_waveform_t *wf) {
    if (!tile || !wf) return;

    ui_rect_t overlap;
    if (!ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &wf->rect, &overlap)) {
        return;
    }

    // 1. 擦除波形视口背景与绘制边框
    ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &overlap, wf->color_bg);
    ui_draw_rect(tile, &wf->rect, 0x39E7);

    // 2. 锁定像素级视口裁剪
    ui_draw_set_clip(&wf->rect);

    // ================= A. 8 通道数字逻辑时序渲染 =================
    if (wf->mode == UI_WAVE_MODE_DIGITAL_8CH && wf->digital_data) {
        uint8_t ch_count = wf->active_channels;
        if (ch_count == 0) ch_count = 1;

        int16_t ch_slot_h = (int16_t)((wf->rect.h - 20) / ch_count); // 预留顶部 20px 标尺栏
        int16_t wave_h = (ch_slot_h > 10) ? (int16_t)(ch_slot_h - 6) : 6;

        int16_t draw_start_x = (int16_t)(wf->rect.x + 36); // 左侧留出 36px 绘制通道名
        int16_t draw_end_x   = (int16_t)(wf->rect.x + wf->rect.w);

        // ⚡ 核心算术剪枝：仅处理与当前切片相交的通道
        int16_t first_ch = (tile->rect.y - (wf->rect.y + 20)) / ch_slot_h;
        int16_t last_ch  = (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h - 1 - (wf->rect.y + 20)) / ch_slot_h;

        if (first_ch < 0) first_ch = 0;
        if (last_ch >= (int16_t)ch_count) last_ch = (int16_t)ch_count - 1;

        for (int16_t ch = first_ch; ch <= last_ch; ch++) {
            int16_t slot_top = (int16_t)(wf->rect.y + 20 + ch * ch_slot_h);
            int16_t y_low  = (int16_t)(slot_top + wave_h);
            int16_t y_high = (int16_t)(slot_top + 2);

            // 绘制通道分割基准线
            int16_t grid_y = (int16_t)(slot_top + ch_slot_h - 1);
            if (grid_y >= tile->rect.y && grid_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
                ui_draw_fast_hline(tile, wf->rect.x, grid_y, wf->rect.w, wf->color_grid);
            }

            // 绘制左侧通道标签 (如 D0, D1...)
            int16_t label_y = (int16_t)(slot_top + (ch_slot_h - 16) / 2);
            if ((label_y + 16) > tile->rect.y && label_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
                char label[6];
                snprintf(label, sizeof(label), "D%d", ch);
                ui_draw_string(tile, (int16_t)(wf->rect.x + 6), label_y, 
                               label, FONT_16, wf->color_channels[ch], wf->color_bg);
            }

            // 光栅化渲染方波电平与跃变边沿
            uint8_t mask = (uint8_t)(1 << ch);
            int16_t prev_y = -1;

            for (int16_t px = draw_start_x; px < draw_end_x; px++) {
                uint32_t s_idx = wf->view_offset + (uint32_t)(px - draw_start_x) * wf->samples_per_pixel;
                if (s_idx >= wf->total_samples) break;

                bool is_high = (wf->digital_data[s_idx] & mask) != 0;
                int16_t cur_y = is_high ? y_high : y_low;

                // 绘制水平电平点
                if (cur_y >= tile->rect.y && cur_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
                    ui_draw_point(tile, px, cur_y, wf->color_channels[ch]);
                }

                // 绘制电平跳变垂直边沿
                if (prev_y != -1 && prev_y != cur_y) {
                    int16_t v_top = (prev_y < cur_y) ? prev_y : cur_y;
                    int16_t v_h   = (int16_t)(abs(cur_y - prev_y) + 1);
                    ui_draw_fast_vline(tile, px, v_top, v_h, wf->color_channels[ch]);
                }
                prev_y = cur_y;
            }
        }
    }

    // ================= B. 模拟示波器曲线渲染 =================
    else if (wf->mode == UI_WAVE_MODE_ANALOG_1CH && wf->analog_data) {
        int16_t mid_y = (int16_t)(wf->rect.y + wf->rect.h / 2);
        int16_t prev_x = -1, prev_y = -1;

        // 绘制中心基准线
        if (mid_y >= tile->rect.y && mid_y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
            ui_draw_fast_hline(tile, wf->rect.x, mid_y, wf->rect.w, wf->color_grid);
        }

        for (int16_t px = wf->rect.x; px < (wf->rect.x + wf->rect.w); px++) {
            uint32_t s_idx = wf->view_offset + (uint32_t)(px - wf->rect.x) * wf->samples_per_pixel;
            if (s_idx >= wf->total_samples) break;

            int16_t val = wf->analog_data[s_idx];
            int16_t py = (int16_t)(mid_y - val);

            if (prev_x != -1) {
                ui_draw_line(tile, prev_x, prev_y, px, py, 0x07E0);
            }
            prev_x = px;
            prev_y = py;
        }
    }

    // ================= C. 双游标测量线与测量看板 =================
    if (wf->show_cursors) {
        // 绘制 Cursor A (金黄线)
        if (wf->cursor_a_x >= wf->rect.x && wf->cursor_a_x < (wf->rect.x + wf->rect.w)) {
            ui_draw_fast_vline(tile, wf->cursor_a_x, wf->rect.y, wf->rect.h, wf->color_cursor_a);
            if ((wf->rect.y + 16) > tile->rect.y && wf->rect.y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
                ui_draw_string(tile, (int16_t)(wf->cursor_a_x + 2), (int16_t)(wf->rect.y + 2), "A", FONT_16, wf->color_cursor_a, wf->color_bg);
            }
        }

        // 绘制 Cursor B (紫红线)
        if (wf->cursor_b_x >= wf->rect.x && wf->cursor_b_x < (wf->rect.x + wf->rect.w)) {
            ui_draw_fast_vline(tile, wf->cursor_b_x, wf->rect.y, wf->rect.h, wf->color_cursor_b);
            if ((wf->rect.y + 16) > tile->rect.y && wf->rect.y < (tile->rect.y + tile->rect.h)) {
                ui_draw_string(tile, (int16_t)(wf->cursor_b_x + 2), (int16_t)(wf->rect.y + 2), "B", FONT_16, wf->color_cursor_b, wf->color_bg);
            }
        }

        // 顶部测量标尺看板 (显示 dT)
        int16_t draw_start_x = (wf->mode == UI_WAVE_MODE_DIGITAL_8CH) ? (int16_t)(wf->rect.x + 36) : wf->rect.x;
        int16_t ca = (wf->cursor_a_x > draw_start_x) ? (int16_t)(wf->cursor_a_x - draw_start_x) : 0;
        int16_t cb = (wf->cursor_b_x > draw_start_x) ? (int16_t)(wf->cursor_b_x - draw_start_x) : 0;

        uint32_t delta_pts = (uint32_t)abs(ca - cb) * wf->samples_per_pixel;
        char time_str[32];
        format_time_delta(time_str, sizeof(time_str), delta_pts, wf->sample_rate_hz);

        ui_rect_t banner_rect = ui_rect_make((int16_t)(wf->rect.x + wf->rect.w - 140), (int16_t)(wf->rect.y + 2), 136, 16);
        ui_rect_t ban_ov;
        if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &banner_rect, &ban_ov)) {
            ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &ban_ov, 0x18C3);
            ui_draw_string(tile, (int16_t)(banner_rect.x + 4), banner_rect.y, time_str, FONT_16, 0x07FF, 0x18C3);
        }
    }

    ui_draw_reset_clip();
}

点击展开
/*
 * @Author: 轩
 * @Date: 2026-08-20 13:19:04
 * @LastEditTime: 2026-08-21 12:36:23
 * @FilePath: \pico\ui\ui_waveform.h
 */
#ifndef UI_WAVEFORM_H
#define UI_WAVEFORM_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include "ui_geometry.h"
#include "ui_tile.h"
#include "chinese.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

#define UI_WAVE_MAX_CHANNELS    8

typedef enum {
    UI_WAVE_MODE_DIGITAL_8CH = 0, // 8 通道数字逻辑时序图 (每个字节 8 bit)
    UI_WAVE_MODE_ANALOG_1CH       // 单通道模拟示波器曲线 (int16_t/uint16_t ADC 值)
} ui_waveform_mode_t;

typedef enum {
    UI_WAVE_DRAG_NONE = 0,
    UI_WAVE_DRAG_PAN,             // 左右滑动平移时间轴[cite: 34]
    UI_WAVE_DRAG_CURSOR_A,        // 拖拽测量游标 A[cite: 34]
    UI_WAVE_DRAG_CURSOR_B         // 拖拽测量游标 B[cite: 34]
} ui_waveform_drag_t;

typedef struct ui_waveform ui_waveform_t;

// 波形光标/视野改变回调[cite: 34]
typedef void (*ui_waveform_cb_t)(ui_waveform_t *wf, void *user_data);

struct ui_waveform {
    ui_rect_t           rect;               // 波形视口物理包围盒[cite: 34]
    ui_waveform_mode_t  mode;               // 渲染模式[cite: 34]

    // 数据源绑定[cite: 34]
    const uint8_t      *digital_data;       // 8 通道数字数据流 (D0~D7 分布在 bit0~bit7)[cite: 34]
    const int16_t      *analog_data;        // 模拟数据流[cite: 34]
    uint32_t            total_samples;      // 采样总点数[cite: 34]

    // 视野与缩放 (时间轴)[cite: 34]
    uint32_t            view_offset;        // 左边缘对应的起始采样点序号[cite: 34]
    uint16_t            samples_per_pixel;  // 采样压缩比 (1: 1像素1点, 2: 1像素2点...)[cite: 34]
    uint8_t             active_channels;    // 逻辑通道启用数量 (1 ~ 8)[cite: 34]

    // 双游标测量系统 (Cursor A / B)[cite: 34]
    bool                show_cursors;       // 是否显示游标[cite: 34]
    int16_t             cursor_a_x;         // 游标 A 屏幕 X 像素坐标[cite: 34]
    int16_t             cursor_b_x;         // 游标 B 屏幕 X 像素坐标[cite: 34]
    uint32_t            sample_rate_hz;     // 当前采样率 (用于换算真实时间 ns/us/ms)[cite: 34]

    // ⚡ 触控手势防抖与全局捕获状态机
    ui_waveform_drag_t  drag_state;         // 当前拖拽目标 (游标 A / 游标 B / 时间轴)[cite: 34]
    int16_t             down_x;
    int16_t             down_y;
    int16_t             touch_start_x;      // 按下时的基准 X 坐标[cite: 34]
    uint32_t            view_start_offset;  // 按下时的起始视野偏移[cite: 34]
    bool                is_touch_down;      // 是否处于触控按压中
    bool                is_dragged;         // 是否已确认为拖拽手势 (位移 > 8px)

    // 配色方案 (RGB565)[cite: 34]
    uint16_t            color_bg;           // 背景底色 (通常为纯黑)[cite: 34]
    uint16_t            color_grid;         // 网格与通道基准线暗色[cite: 34]
    uint16_t            color_cursor_a;     // 游标 A 颜色 (金黄)[cite: 34]
    uint16_t            color_cursor_b;     // 游标 B 颜色 (紫红)[cite: 34]
    uint16_t            color_channels[UI_WAVE_MAX_CHANNELS]; // CH0 ~ CH7 各通道专属颜色[cite: 34]

    ui_waveform_cb_t    on_change;          // 视野/游标改变回调[cite: 34]
    void               *user_data;          // 用户私有指针[cite: 34]
};

// 初始化波形画布[cite: 34]
void ui_waveform_init(ui_waveform_t *wf, int16_t x, int16_t y, int16_t w, int16_t h,
                      ui_waveform_mode_t mode, uint32_t sample_rate_hz);

// 绑定采样数据流[cite: 34]
void ui_waveform_bind_digital(ui_waveform_t *wf, const uint8_t *data, uint32_t total_samples);
void ui_waveform_bind_analog(ui_waveform_t *wf, const int16_t *data, uint32_t total_samples);

// 设置缩放与通道数[cite: 34]
void ui_waveform_set_scale(ui_waveform_t *wf, uint16_t samples_per_pixel, uint8_t active_channels);

// 触控交互调度 (集成 8px 死区保护、游标抓取与时间轴 Touch Capture 追踪)[cite: 34]
bool ui_waveform_process_touch(ui_waveform_t *wf, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick);

// 切片光栅化渲染 (通道级 O(1) 算术剪枝 + 视口硬裁剪保护)[cite: 34]
void ui_waveform_draw(ui_tile_t *tile, const ui_waveform_t *wf);

// 获取波形画布脏矩形包围盒 (用于 ui_render_dirty_clipped 局部极速推流)
static inline ui_rect_t ui_waveform_get_dirty_rect(const ui_waveform_t *wf) {
    return wf ? wf->rect : ui_rect_make(0, 0, 0, 0);
}

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

#endif // UI_WAVEFORM_H

// 应用唯一标识枚举
typedef enum {
    APP_ID_GPIO_MATRIX = 0, // 0: GPIO 矩阵 / LED 控制
    APP_ID_SETTINGS,        // 1: 系统设置
    APP_ID_LOGIC_LA,        // 2: 逻辑分析
    APP_ID_OSCILLOSCOPE,    // 3: 示波时序
    APP_ID_DAP_FLASHER,     // 4: 离线烧录
    APP_ID_UART_TERM,       // 5: 串口助手
    APP_ID_I2C_SNIFFER,     // 6: I2C 嗅探
    APP_ID_PWM_GEN,         // 7: PWM 发生
    APP_ID_HW_INFO,         // 8: 硬件自检
    APP_ID_MAX
} app_id_t;

typedef struct app app_t;

struct app {
    app_id_t    id;
    const char *name;           // 应用全名 (如 "LED 控制器")
    const char *icon_symbol;    // 图标字符 (如 "LED", "PWM")
    uint16_t    icon_color;     // 图标底色 (RGB565)

    // 生命周期与渲染回调
    void (*init)(app_t *self);
    void (*enter)(app_t *self);
    void (*exit)(app_t *self);
    void (*update)(app_t *self, uint32_t now_tick);
    bool (*process_touch)(app_t *self, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick);
    void (*draw)(app_t *self, ui_tile_t *tile);

    void *user_data;
};

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

#endif // APP_BASE_H
app_led.c.c 139 行 · 5224 B

#include "app_led.h"
#include "app_manager.h"

#include "ui_draw.h"
#include "ui_btn.h"
#include "ui_switch.h"
#include "lcd.h"

typedef struct {
    ui_rect_t   dirty_rect;  // 必须放在第 1 位，供 app_manager 提取局部脏矩形
    bool        sw_state;    // 纯软件开关状态
    ui_switch_t sw_led;      // 开关控件
    ui_btn_t    btn_toggle;  // 翻转按钮
    ui_rect_t   status_card; // 状态卡片区域
} app_led_data_t;

static app_led_data_t s_led_data;
static app_t          s_app_led;

static void on_switch_change(ui_switch_t *sw, bool state, void *user_data) {
    (void)sw;
    app_led_data_t *data = (app_led_data_t *)user_data;
    data->sw_state = state; // 仅更新软件状态，不操作硬件
}

static void on_btn_toggle_click(ui_btn_t *btn, void *user_data) {
    (void)btn;
    app_led_data_t *data = (app_led_data_t *)user_data;
    data->sw_state = !data->sw_state;
    ui_switch_set_state(&data->sw_led, data->sw_state); // 同步更新 Switch 控件显示
}

static void app_led_init(app_t *self) {
    self->user_data = &s_led_data;
    app_led_data_t *data = &s_led_data;

    data->sw_state    = false;
    data->status_card = ui_rect_make(20, 50, 440, 76);
    data->dirty_rect  = ui_rect_make(0, 0, 0, 0);

    // 1. 初始化纯软件 Switch 开关
    ui_switch_init(&data->sw_led, 350, 152, 64, 32, false, on_switch_change);
    data->sw_led.user_data = data;

    // 2. 初始化点按翻转按钮
    ui_btn_init(&data->btn_toggle, 130, 218, 220, 44, "翻转软件状态", on_btn_toggle_click);
    data->btn_toggle.user_data   = data;
    data->btn_toggle.color_bg    = 0x18C3;
    data->btn_toggle.color_active= 0x07E0;
}

static void app_led_enter(app_t *self) {
    (void)self;
}

static void app_led_exit(app_t *self) {
    (void)self;
}

static void app_led_update(app_t *self, uint32_t now_tick) {
    (void)self; (void)now_tick;
}

static bool app_led_process_touch(app_t *self, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, uint32_t now_tick) {
    app_led_data_t *data = (app_led_data_t *)self->user_data;
    bool sw_changed  = ui_switch_process_touch(&data->sw_led, is_touching, tx, ty, now_tick);
    bool btn_changed = ui_btn_process_touch(&data->btn_toggle, is_touching, tx, ty, now_tick);

    if (sw_changed) {
        // 1. 若是开关动作：仅刷新开关自身的小矩形 + 紧邻它的状态指示区
        ui_rect_t c_sw = ui_switch_get_dirty_rect(&data->sw_led);
        ui_rect_t c_status = ui_rect_make(30, 80, 300, 32);
        ui_rect_union(&c_sw, &c_status, &data->dirty_rect);
        return true;
    }
    
    if (btn_changed) {
        // 2. 若是底部按钮动作：仅刷新按钮自身矩形 (绝不和顶部做跨区域并集！高度仅 44px，完美命中单个切片)
        data->dirty_rect = data->btn_toggle.rect;
        return true;
    }

    return false;
}

static void app_led_draw(app_t *self, ui_tile_t *tile) {
    app_led_data_t *data = (app_led_data_t *)self->user_data;
    ui_rect_t overlap;

    // 工作区背景
    ui_rect_t body = ui_rect_make(0, 32, LCD_WIDTH, LCD_HEIGHT - 32);
    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &body, &overlap)) {
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &overlap, 0x0947);
    }

    // 状态显示卡片
    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &data->status_card, &overlap)) {
        ui_draw_fill_rect(tile, &overlap, 0x11A9);
        ui_rect_t card_rect = data->status_card;
        ui_draw_rect(tile, &card_rect, 0x07FF);

        ui_draw_string(tile, 35, 60, "【纯软件开关状态指示】", FONT_16, YELLOW, 0x11A9);

        uint16_t dot_color = data->sw_state ? 0x07E0 : 0x4208;
        ui_draw_fill_circle(tile, 45, 96, 7, dot_color);
        ui_draw_circle(tile, 45, 96, 7, WHITE);

        const char *state_str = data->sw_state ? "状态: 开启 (TRUE / HIGH)" : "状态: 关闭 (FALSE / LOW)";
        ui_draw_string(tile, 62, 88, state_str, FONT_16, data->sw_state ? 0x07E0 : WHITE, 0x11A9);
    }

    // 控件提示
    ui_rect_t label_rect = ui_rect_make(40, 158, 220, 20);
    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &label_rect, &overlap)) {
        ui_draw_string(tile, 40, 158, "滑动开关控制状态:", FONT_16, WHITE, 0x0947);
    }

    ui_switch_draw(tile, &data->sw_led);
    ui_btn_draw(tile, &data->btn_toggle);

    // 底部提示
    ui_rect_t tip_rect = ui_rect_make(0, 288, LCD_WIDTH, 32);
    if (ui_rect_intersect(&tile->rect, &tip_rect, &overlap)) {
        ui_draw_string(tile, 20, 292, "屏幕左边缘向右滑: 菜单 | 右边缘向左滑: 退出", FONT_16, 0x07FF, 0x0947);
    }
}

app_t *app_led_get_instance(void) {
    s_app_led.id            = APP_ID_GPIO_MATRIX;
    s_app_led.name          = "开关控制器";
    s_app_led.icon_symbol   = "SW";
    s_app_led.icon_color    = 0x07E0;
    s_app_led.init          = app_led_init;
    s_app_led.enter         = app_led_enter;
    s_app_led.exit          = app_led_exit;
    s_app_led.update        = app_led_update;
    s_app_led.process_touch = app_led_process_touch;
    s_app_led.draw          = app_led_draw;
    return &s_app_led;
}

#include "app_manager.h"
#include "ui_draw.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include "lcd.h"
#define EDGE_ZONE_LEFT_PX   50
#define EDGE_ZONE_RIGHT_PX  430
#define SWIPE_THRESHOLD_PX  40
#define SWIPE_MAX_DEVIATION 60
#define DRAWER_WIDTH        140

static void on_drawer_app_select(uint8_t app_idx, void *user_data) {
    app_manager_t *mgr = (app_manager_t *)user_data;
    if (!mgr) return;
    app_manager_switch_to(mgr, app_idx);
}

static void draw_home_dashboard(ui_tile_t *tile, const app_manager_t *mgr) {
    ui_rect_t body = ui_rect_make(0, 32, LCD_WIDTH, LCD_HEIGHT - 32);

void app_manager_init(app_manager_t *mgr) {
    if (!mgr) return;
    mgr->app_count        = 0;
    mgr->current_idx      = -1;
    mgr->is_home          = true;
    mgr->edge_touch_down  = false;
    mgr->edge_candidate   = 0;
    mgr->gesture_consumed = false;

    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < APP_MAX_COUNT; i++) {
        mgr->apps[i] = NULL;
    }

    ui_drawer_init(&mgr->drawer, 32, 288, DRAWER_WIDTH, on_drawer_app_select, mgr);
}

bool app_manager_register(app_manager_t *mgr, app_t *app) {
    if (!mgr || !app || mgr->app_count >= APP_MAX_COUNT) return false;
    if (app->init) app->init(app);
    mgr->apps[mgr->app_count++] = app;
    ui_drawer_register_app(&mgr->drawer, app);
    return true;
}

bool app_manager_switch_to(app_manager_t *mgr, uint8_t app_idx) {
    if (!mgr || app_idx >= mgr->app_count) return false;

    if (!mgr->is_home) {
        app_t *old_app = app_manager_get_current(mgr);
        if (old_app && old_app->exit) old_app->exit(old_app);
    }

    mgr->is_home     = false;
    mgr->current_idx = (int8_t)app_idx;
    mgr->drawer.selected_idx = (int8_t)app_idx;

    app_t *new_app = mgr->apps[app_idx];
    if (new_app && new_app->enter) new_app->enter(new_app);
    return true;
}

bool app_manager_return_home(app_manager_t *mgr) {
    if (!mgr || mgr->is_home) return false;
    app_t *cur = app_manager_get_current(mgr);
    if (cur && cur->exit) cur->exit(cur);

    mgr->is_home     = true;
    mgr->current_idx = -1;
    mgr->drawer.selected_idx = -1;
    return true;
}

void app_manager_open_drawer(app_manager_t *mgr) {
    if (mgr) ui_drawer_open(&mgr->drawer);
}

void app_manager_close_drawer(app_manager_t *mgr) {
    if (mgr) ui_drawer_close(&mgr->drawer);
}

void app_manager_toggle_drawer(app_manager_t *mgr) {
    if (mgr) ui_drawer_toggle(&mgr->drawer);
}

bool app_manager_is_drawer_open(const app_manager_t *mgr) {
    return mgr ? mgr->drawer.is_open : false;
}

app_t *app_manager_get_current(const app_manager_t *mgr) {
    if (!mgr || mgr->is_home || mgr->current_idx < 0 || mgr->current_idx >= (int8_t)mgr->app_count) {
        return NULL;
    }
    return mgr->apps[mgr->current_idx];
}

void app_manager_update(app_manager_t *mgr, uint32_t now_tick) {
    if (!mgr || mgr->is_home) return;
    app_t *cur = app_manager_get_current(mgr);
    if (cur && cur->update) cur->update(cur, now_tick);
}

/* ================= ⚡ 脏矩形捕获分发核心 ================= */

bool app_manager_process_touch_dirty(app_manager_t *mgr, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, 
                                     uint32_t now_tick, ui_rect_t *out_dirty, bool *is_full_frame) {
    if (!mgr || !out_dirty || !is_full_frame) return false;
    *is_full_frame = false;

    // 1. 抽屉菜单处于打开状态
    if (mgr->drawer.is_open) {
        bool drawer_changed = ui_drawer_process_touch(&mgr->drawer, is_touching, tx, ty, now_tick);
        if (drawer_changed) {
            // 抽屉滑动时仅推流抽屉所在的 140px 区域
            *out_dirty = mgr->drawer.rect;
            // 若点击项触发了换页，则需要全屏刷新
            if (!mgr->drawer.is_open) {
                *is_full_frame = true;
            }
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    // 2. 边缘手势状态机
    if (is_touching) {
        if (!mgr->edge_touch_down) {
            mgr->edge_touch_down  = true;
            mgr->edge_down_x       = tx;
            mgr->edge_down_y       = ty;
            mgr->gesture_consumed  = false;

            if (tx <= EDGE_ZONE_LEFT_PX) {
                mgr->edge_candidate = 1;
            } else if (tx >= EDGE_ZONE_RIGHT_PX) {
                mgr->edge_candidate = 2;
            } else {
                mgr->edge_candidate = 0;
            }
        } else {
            if (!mgr->gesture_consumed) {
                int16_t dx = tx - mgr->edge_down_x;
                int16_t dy = abs(ty - mgr->edge_down_y);

                // 右滑呼出抽屉
                if (mgr->edge_candidate == 1 && dx >= SWIPE_THRESHOLD_PX && dy <= SWIPE_MAX_DEVIATION) {
                    mgr->gesture_consumed = true;
                    ui_drawer_open(&mgr->drawer);
                    *out_dirty = mgr->drawer.rect; // 仅推流抽屉区域
                    return true;
                }

                // 左滑退出到主界面
                if (mgr->edge_candidate == 2 && dx <= -SWIPE_THRESHOLD_PX && dy <= SWIPE_MAX_DEVIATION) {
                    mgr->gesture_consumed = true;
                    if (!mgr->is_home) {
                        app_manager_return_home(mgr);
                        *is_full_frame = true; // 页面切换才全刷
                        return true;
                    }
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }

        if (mgr->gesture_consumed) return false;

        // 3. 非边缘手势：分发给当前 App
        if (!mgr->is_home) {
            app_t *cur = app_manager_get_current(mgr);
            if (cur && cur->process_touch) {
                // ⚡ App 内部处理，并返回 App 私有的局部脏矩形
                bool redraw = cur->process_touch(cur, is_touching, tx, ty, now_tick);
                if (redraw) {
                    // 若 App 挂载了 user_data 且记录了局部脏矩形，则提取该脏矩形
                    *out_dirty = *(ui_rect_t *)cur->user_data;
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    } else {
        mgr->edge_touch_down  = false;
        mgr->edge_candidate   = 0;
        bool was_consumed     = mgr->gesture_consumed;
        mgr->gesture_consumed = false;

        if (was_consumed) return false;

        if (!mgr->is_home) {
            app_t *cur = app_manager_get_current(mgr);
            if (cur && cur->process_touch) {
                bool redraw = cur->process_touch(cur, false, tx, ty, now_tick);
                if (redraw) {
                    *out_dirty = *(ui_rect_t *)cur->user_data;
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    return false;
}

void app_manager_draw(app_manager_t *mgr, ui_tile_t *tile) {
    if (!mgr) return;

    if (mgr->is_home) {
        draw_home_dashboard(tile, mgr);
    } else {
        app_t *cur = app_manager_get_current(mgr);
        if (cur && cur->draw) cur->draw(cur, tile);
    }

    if (mgr->drawer.is_open) {
        ui_drawer_draw(tile, &mgr->drawer);
    }
}

#ifndef APP_MANAGER_H
#define APP_MANAGER_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include "app_base.h"
#include "ui_drawer.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

#define APP_MAX_COUNT 16

typedef struct {
    app_t          *apps[APP_MAX_COUNT];
    uint8_t         app_count;
    int8_t          current_idx;    // -1 为主界面，>=0 为具体 App
    bool            is_home;

    ui_drawer_t     drawer;

    int16_t         edge_down_x;
    int16_t         edge_down_y;
    bool            edge_touch_down;
    uint8_t         edge_candidate;
    bool            gesture_consumed;
} app_manager_t;

void app_manager_init(app_manager_t *mgr);
bool app_manager_register(app_manager_t *mgr, app_t *app);
bool app_manager_switch_to(app_manager_t *mgr, uint8_t app_idx);
bool app_manager_return_home(app_manager_t *mgr);

void app_manager_open_drawer(app_manager_t *mgr);
void app_manager_close_drawer(app_manager_t *mgr);
void app_manager_toggle_drawer(app_manager_t *mgr);
bool app_manager_is_drawer_open(const app_manager_t *mgr);

app_t *app_manager_get_current(const app_manager_t *mgr);
void app_manager_update(app_manager_t *mgr, uint32_t now_tick);

// ⚡ 带精确脏矩形捕获的触控分发：out_dirty 返回需要重绘的局部区域，is_full_frame 返回是否发生整页切换
bool app_manager_process_touch_dirty(app_manager_t *mgr, bool is_touching, int16_t tx, int16_t ty, 
                                     uint32_t now_tick, ui_rect_t *out_dirty, bool *is_full_frame);

void app_manager_draw(app_manager_t *mgr, ui_tile_t *tile);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

#endif // APP_MANAGER_H
